   retain their existing sizing. */

  }
}

/**
 * Using the elevate system.
 * Automatic contrast adjustment.
 *
 * <element className="hover-elevate" />
 * <element className="active-elevate-2" />
 *
 * // Using the tailwind utility when a data attribute is "on"
 * <element className="toggle-elevate data-[state=on]:toggle-elevated" />
 * // Or manually controlling the toggle state
 * <element className="toggle-elevate toggle-elevated" />
 *
 * Elevation systems have to handle many states.
 * - not-hovered, vs. hovered vs. active  (three mutually exclusive states)
 * - toggled or not
 * - focused or not (this is not handled with these utilities)
 *
 * Even without handling focused or not, this is six possible combinations that
 * need to be distinguished from eachother visually.
 */
@layer utilities {

  /* Hide ugly search cancel button in Chrome until we can style it properly */
  input[type="search"]::-webkit-search-cancel-button {
    @apply hidden;
  }

  /* Placeholder styling for contentEditable div */
  [contenteditable][data-placeholder]:empty::before {
    content: attr(data-placeholder);
    color: hsl(var(--muted-foreground));
    pointer-events: none;
  }

  /* .no-default-hover-elevate/no-default-active-elevate is an escape hatch so consumers of
   * buttons/badges can remove the automatic brightness adjustment on interactions
   * and program their own. */
  .no-default-hover-elevate {}

  .no-default-active-elevate {}


  /**
   * Toggleable backgrounds go behind the content. Hoverable/active goes on top.
   * This way they can stack/compound. Both will overlap the parent's borders!
   * So borders will be automatically adjusted both on toggle, and hover/active,
   * and they will be compounded.
   */
  .toggle-elevate::before,
  .toggle-elevate-2::before {
    content: "";
    pointer-events: none;
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0px;
    /*border-radius: inherit;   match rounded corners */
    border-radius: inherit;
    z-index: -1;
    /* sits behind content but above backdrop */
  }

  .toggle-elevate.toggle-elevated::before {
    background-color: var(--elevate-2);
  }

  /* If there's a 1px border, adjust the inset so that it covers that parent's border */
  .border.toggle-elevate::before {
    inset: -1px;
  }

  /* Does not work on elements with overflow:hidden! */
  .hover-elevate:not(.no-default-hover-elevate),
  .active-elevate:not(.no-default-active-elevate),
  .hover-elevate-2:not(.no-default-hover-elevate),
  .active-elevate-2:not(.no-default-active-elevate) {
    position: relative;
    z-index: 0;
  }

  .hover-elevate:not(.no-default-hover-elevate)::after,
  .active-elevate:not(.no-default-active-elevate)::after,
  .hover-elevate-2:not(.no-default-hover-elevate)::after,
  .active-elevate-2:not(.no-default-active-elevate)::after {
    content: "";
    pointer-events: none;
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0px;
    /*border-radius: inherit;   match rounded corners */
    border-radius: inherit;
    z-index: 999;
    /* sits in front of content */
  }

  .hover-elevate:hover:not(.no-default-hover-elevate)::after,
  .active-elevate:active:not(.no-default-active-elevate)::after {
    background-color: var(--elevate-1);
  }

  .hover-elevate-2:hover:not(.no-default-hover-elevate)::after,
  .active-elevate-2:active:not(.no-default-active-elevate)::after {
    background-color: var(--elevate-2);
  }

  /* If there's a 1px border, adjust the inset so that it covers that parent's border */
  .border.hover-elevate:not(.no-hover-interaction-elevate)::after,
  .border.active-elevate:not(.no-active-interaction-elevate)::after,
  .border.hover-elevate-2:not(.no-hover-interaction-elevate)::after,
  .border.active-elevate-2:not(.no-active-interaction-elevate)::after,
  .border.hover-elevate:not(.no-hover-interaction-elevate)::after {
    inset: -1px;
  }
}

    max-width: 100% !important;
    table-layout: fixed !important;
    border-collapse: collapse !important;
    border: 1px solid #000 !important;
  }

  html[data-document-printing="true"] #report-print-root th,
  html[data-document-printing="true"] #report-print-root td {
    padding: 2px !important;
    font-size: 8px !important;
    border: 1px solid #000 !important;
  }

  html[data-document-printing="true"] #report-print-root th {
    font-weight: bold !important;
    background-color: #f0f0f0 !important;
    -webkit-print-color-adjust: exact !important;
    print-color-adjust: exact !important;
  }

  html[data-document-printing="true"] #report-print-root .no-break {
    break-inside: avoid !important;
    page-break-inside: avoid !important;
  }
}

import { execFileSync } from "node:child_process";

const html2canvasMock = vi.fn();
const addImageMock = vi.fn();
const addPageMock = vi.fn();
const saveMock = vi.fn();
const jsPdfMock = vi.fn(() => ({
  internal: { pageSize: { getHeight: () => 297 } },
  addImage: addImageMock,
  addPage: addPageMock,
  save: saveMock,
}));

vi.mock("html2canvas", () => ({ default: html2canvasMock }));
vi.mock("jspdf", () => ({ default: jsPdfMock }));

function dumpDomWithOneRetry(chromium: string, args: string[], url: string, marker: string) {
  let rendered = execFileSync(chromium, [...args, "--dump-dom", url], { encoding: "utf8" });
  if (!rendered.includes(marker)) {
    rendered = execFileSync(chromium, [...args, "--dump-dom", url], { encoding: "utf8" });
  }
  return rendered;
}

function renderPreview(
  orientation: "portrait" | "landscape",
  logoUrl: string,
  styleNodes: { outerHTML: string }[] = [],
) {
  let html = "";
  const logo = {
    dataset: {} as DOMStringMap,
    style: { setProperty: vi.fn() },
  };
  const source = {
    children: [
      {
        cloneNode: () => ({
          querySelectorAll: () => [logo],
          get outerHTML() {
            const style = logo.style.setProperty.mock.calls
              .map(([property, value]) => `${property}: ${value} !important`)
              .join("; ");
            return `<section><img class="document-header-logo" data-document-logo="${logo.dataset.documentLogo}" style="${style}" src="${logoUrl}"></section>`;
          },
        }),
      },
    ],
  };
  const popup = {
    document: {
      write: vi.fn((value: string) => { html = value; }),
      close: vi.fn(),
    },
  };

  vi.stubGlobal("HTMLLinkElement", class {});
  vi.stubGlobal("document", {
    getElementById: vi.fn(() => source),
    querySelectorAll: vi.fn(() => styleNodes),
  });
  vi.stubGlobal("window", {
    open: vi.fn(() => popup),
  });

  expect(previewDocument({ sourceId: "spec-print", title: "Preview", orientation })).toBe(true);
  return html;
}

  vi.unstubAllGlobals();

  function createPdfDom(pageCount = 2) {
    const makePage = (index: number) => {
      const logoStyle: Record<string, string> = {};
      const logo = {
        dataset: {} as Record<string, string>,
        style: {
          setProperty: vi.fn((property: string, value: string) => {
            logoStyle[property] = value;
          }),
        },
      };
      const clone = {
        style: {},
        scrollHeight: 900,
        querySelectorAll: vi.fn(() => [logo]),
        logoStyle,
      };
      return {
        cloneNode: vi.fn(() => clone),
        outerHTML: `<section>Sida ${index + 1}</section>`,
        clone,
      };
    };
    const sourcePages = Array.from({ length: pageCount }, (_, index) => makePage(index));
    const printRoot = {
      children: [] as unknown[],
      childNodes: [] as unknown[],
      clientWidth: 703,
      querySelectorAll: vi.fn(() => []),
      replaceChildren: vi.fn(function (this: typeof printRoot, ...children: unknown[]) {
        this.children = children;
      }),
      getAttribute: vi.fn((name: string) => name === "aria-hidden" ? "true" : null),
      setAttribute: vi.fn(),
      removeAttribute: vi.fn(),
    };
    const pdfStyle = { dataset: {}, textContent: "", remove: vi.fn() };
    const documentElement = {
      dataset: {} as Record<string, string>,
      getAttribute: vi.fn(() => null),
      setAttribute: vi.fn(),
    };
    class CSSMediaRuleStub {}
    class CSSStyleRuleStub {}
    vi.stubGlobal("CSSMediaRule", CSSMediaRuleStub);
    vi.stubGlobal("CSSStyleRule", CSSStyleRuleStub);
    const printRule = Object.assign(new CSSStyleRuleStub(), {
      selectorText: 'html[data-document-printing="true"] #spec-print-root .specification-table',
      cssText: 'html[data-document-printing="true"] #spec-print-root .specification-table { table-layout: fixed !important; }',
    });
    const printMediaRule = Object.assign(new CSSMediaRuleStub(), {
      media: { mediaText: "print" },
      cssRules: [printRule],
    });
    vi.stubGlobal("HTMLElement", class {});
    Object.setPrototypeOf(printRoot, HTMLElement.prototype);
    sourcePages.forEach(page => Object.setPrototypeOf(page.clone, HTMLElement.prototype));
    vi.stubGlobal("document", {
      fonts: { ready: Promise.resolve() },
      styleSheets: [{ cssRules: [printMediaRule] }],
      documentElement,
      head: { appendChild: vi.fn() },
      createElement: vi.fn(() => pdfStyle),
      getElementById: vi.fn((id: string) => id === "spec-print" ? { children: sourcePages } : printRoot),
    });
    vi.stubGlobal("requestAnimationFrame", (callback: FrameRequestCallback) => {
      callback(0);
      return 1;
    });
    html2canvasMock.mockResolvedValue({
      width: 1406,
      height: 1800,
      toDataURL: vi.fn(() => "data:image/png;base64,pdf-page"),
    });
    return { printRoot, sourcePages, pdfStyle, documentElement };
  }

  afterEach(() => {
    html2canvasMock.mockReset();
    addImageMock.mockReset();
    addPageMock.mockReset();
    saveMock.mockReset();
    jsPdfMock.mockClear();
  });

  it("klonar varje färdig dokumentsida till verklig A4-bredd och rasteriserar sidorna separat", async () => {
    const { printRoot, sourcePages, pdfStyle, documentElement } = createPdfDom();

    await downloadDocumentPdf({
      sourceId: "spec-print",
      title: "Specifikation 168",
      fileName: "Spec-168.pdf",
      orientation: "portrait",
    });

    expect(sourcePages.every(page => page.cloneNode.mock.calls.length === 1)).toBe(true);
    expect(printRoot.setAttribute).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      "style",
      expect.stringContaining("width:186mm"),
    );
    expect(html2canvasMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    expect(html2canvasMock.mock.calls.map(([, options]) => options.width)).toEqual([703, 703]);
    expect(addImageMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    expect(addImageMock.mock.calls.every(([, format]) => format === "PNG")).toBe(true);
    expect(addImageMock.mock.calls.every(([, , , , width]) => width === 186)).toBe(true);
    expect(addPageMock).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(saveMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith("Spec-168.pdf");
    expect(printRoot.replaceChildren).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith();
    expect(pdfStyle.textContent).toContain("table-layout: fixed");
    expect(pdfStyle.remove).toHaveBeenCalledOnce();
    expect(documentElement.dataset.documentPrinting).toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("använder liggande A4-bredd utan transform, zoom eller höjdskalning", async () => {
    const { printRoot, sourcePages } = createPdfDom(1);
    html2canvasMock.mockResolvedValue({
      width: 2094,
      height: 1200,
      toDataURL: vi.fn(() => "data:image/png;base64,pdf-page"),
    });
    jsPdfMock.mockReturnValueOnce({
      internal: { pageSize: { getHeight: () => 210 } },
      addImage: addImageMock,
      addPage: addPageMock,
      save: saveMock,
    });

    await downloadDocumentPdf({
      sourceId: "spec-print",
      title: "Hyvlingsorder",
      fileName: "Hyvlingsorder-168.pdf",
      orientation: "landscape",
    });

    expect(jsPdfMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ orientation: "landscape", unit: "mm", format: "a4" });
    expect(printRoot.setAttribute).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      "style",
      expect.stringMatching(/width:277mm.*transform:none!important.*zoom:1!important/),
    );
    expect(sourcePages[0].clone.style).toMatchObject({ width: "100%", transform: "none", zoom: "1" });
    expect(sourcePages[0].clone.logoStyle).toMatchObject({
      "max-width": "28mm",
      "max-height": "14mm",
    });
    expect(addImageMock).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      "data:image/png;base64,pdf-page",
      "PNG",
      10,
      10,
      277,
      expect.any(Number),
      undefined,
      "FAST",
    );
  });

  it("förminskar inte en dokumentsida som är högre än A4", async () => {
    createPdfDom(1);
    html2canvasMock.mockResolvedValue({
      width: 1406,
      height: 2300,
      toDataURL: vi.fn(() => "data:image/png;base64,pdf-page"),
    });

    await expect(downloadDocumentPdf({
      sourceId: "spec-print",
      title: "Specifikation",
      fileName: "Spec-168.pdf",
    })).rejects.toThrow("Dokumentsidan ryms inte på en A4-sida utan att förminskas");
    expect(addImageMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(saveMock).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});

================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/lib/documentOutput.ts ================

export type DocumentSourceOptions = {
  sourceId: string;
  title: string;
  orientation?: "portrait" | "landscape";
  printRootId?: string;
};

type PdfOptions = DocumentSourceOptions & {
  fileName: string;
};

const PAGE_CONTENT_WIDTH_MM = 186;
const PAGE_CONTENT_WIDTH_LANDSCAPE_MM = 277;
const PAGE_MARGIN_X_MM = 12;
const PAGE_MARGIN_X_LANDSCAPE_MM = 10;
const PAGE_MARGIN_Y_MM = 8;
const PAGE_MARGIN_Y_LANDSCAPE_MM = 10;
const DOCUMENT_LOGO_SELECTOR = 'img.document-header-logo, img[data-document-logo="true"]';
const DOCUMENT_LOGO_INLINE_STYLES = {
  width: "auto",
  height: "auto",
  "max-width": "22mm",
  "max-height": "11mm",
  "object-fit": "contain",
  display: "block",
  flex: "0 0 auto",
  "flex-shrink": "0",
} as const;

async function waitForImages(root: ParentNode) {
  const images = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll("img"));
  await Promise.all(images.map(async image => {
    if (image.complete) return;
    try {
      await image.decode();
    } catch {
      await new Promise<void>(resolve => {
        image.addEventListener("load", () => resolve(), { once: true });
        image.addEventListener("error", () => resolve(), { once: true });
      });
    }
  }));
}

async function waitForDocumentLayout(root: ParentNode) {
  if (document.fonts) {
    await document.fonts.ready;
  }
  await waitForImages(root);
  await new Promise<void>(resolve => requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(() => resolve())));
}

function getSource(sourceId: string): HTMLElement {
  const source = document.getElementById(sourceId);
  if (!source) throw new Error("Kunde inte hitta dokumentinnehållet");
  return source;
}

function getDocumentStyleMarkup(): string {
  return Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('style, link[rel="stylesheet"]'))
    .map(node => {
      if (node instanceof HTMLLinkElement) {
        return `<link rel="stylesheet" href="${node.href.replaceAll("&", "&amp;").replaceAll('"', "&quot;")}">`;
      }
      return node.outerHTML;
    })
    .join("\n");
}

function getPdfPrintCss(printRootId: string, orientation: "portrait" | "landscape"): string {
  const rootSelector = `#${printRootId}`;
  const result: string[] = [];
  for (const sheet of Array.from(document.styleSheets)) {
    let rules: CSSRuleList;
    try {
      rules = sheet.cssRules;
    } catch {
      continue;
    }
    for (const rule of Array.from(rules)) {
      if (!(rule instanceof CSSMediaRule)) continue;
      const media = rule.media.mediaText.toLowerCase().replaceAll(/\s+/g, "");
      if (!media.includes("print")) continue;
      if (media.includes("orientation:landscape") && orientation !== "landscape") continue;
      if (media.includes("orientation:portrait") && orientation !== "portrait") continue;
      for (const nestedRule of Array.from(rule.cssRules)) {
        if (nestedRule instanceof CSSStyleRule && nestedRule.selectorText.includes(rootSelector)) {
          result.push(nestedRule.cssText);
        }
      }
    }
  }
  return result.join("\n");
}

function serializePreviewPage(page: Element): string {
  const clone = page.cloneNode(true) as Element;
  clone.querySelectorAll<HTMLImageElement>(DOCUMENT_LOGO_SELECTOR).forEach(image => {
    image.dataset.documentLogo = "true";
    Object.entries(DOCUMENT_LOGO_INLINE_STYLES).forEach(([property, value]) => {
      image.style.setProperty(property, value, "important");
    });
  });
  return clone.outerHTML;
}

type PreviewHtmlOptions = {
  title: string;
  orientation: "portrait" | "landscape";
  documentStyles: string;
  pages: string[];
};

export function buildPreviewDocumentHtml({
  title,
  orientation,
  documentStyles,
  pages,
}: PreviewHtmlOptions): string {
  const safeTitle = title
    .replaceAll("&", "&amp;")
    .replaceAll("<", "&lt;")
    .replaceAll(">", "&gt;")
    .replaceAll('"', "&quot;")
    .replaceAll("'", "&#039;");

  const isLandscape = orientation === "landscape";
  const contentWidth = isLandscape ? PAGE_CONTENT_WIDTH_LANDSCAPE_MM : PAGE_CONTENT_WIDTH_MM;
  const marginX = isLandscape ? PAGE_MARGIN_X_LANDSCAPE_MM : PAGE_MARGIN_X_MM;
  const marginY = isLandscape ? PAGE_MARGIN_Y_LANDSCAPE_MM : PAGE_MARGIN_Y_MM;
  const minHeight = isLandscape ? 210 : 297;
  const pageMarkup = pages
    .map(page => `<div class="page-frame"><div class="document-page">${page}</div></div>`)
    .join("\n");

  return `<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="sv"><head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1">
    <title>${safeTitle}</title>
    ${documentStyles}
    <style>
      * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
      html, body { max-width: 100%; overflow-x: hidden; }
      body { font-family: Arial, sans-serif; font-size: 9px; color: #000; background: #e0e0e0; padding: 64px 12px 12px; }
      .preview-toolbar { position: fixed; z-index: 1000; top: 0; left: 0; right: 0; min-height: 52px; padding: 8px 12px; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; background: rgba(255,255,255,.96); border-bottom: 1px solid #bbb; box-shadow: 0 1px 6px rgba(0,0,0,.16); }
      .preview-close { min-height: 40px; padding: 8px 18px; border: 1px solid #555; border-radius: 6px; background: #fff; color: #111; font: 600 16px/1.2 Arial, sans-serif; cursor: pointer; }
      .preview-pages { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 16px; align-items: center; }
      .page-frame { position: relative; flex: none; }
      .document-page { background: #fff; width: ${contentWidth + marginX * 2}mm; min-height: ${minHeight}mm; padding: ${marginY}mm ${marginX}mm; box-shadow: 0 2px 12px rgba(0,0,0,.2); }
      table { border-collapse: collapse; max-width: 100%; }
      thead { display: table-header-group; }
      th, td { overflow-wrap: anywhere; }
      .specification-table { width: 100%; max-width: 100%; table-layout: fixed; }
      .specification-table th, .specification-table td { box-sizing: border-box; padding: 3px 2px !important; font-size: 7.5px !important; }
      .specification-table th { font-size: 7.75px !important; }
      .specification-table .spec-product-cell { min-width: 0 !important; white-space: normal !important; overflow-wrap: anywhere !important; }
      .specification-table .spec-lpm-cell { width: 7% !important; min-width: 7% !important; padding-left: 4px !important; padding-right: 4px !important; white-space: nowrap !important; overflow-wrap: normal !important; }
      #spec-print img[data-document-logo="true"], .document-page img[data-document-logo="true"] { width: auto !important; height: auto !important; max-width: 22mm !important; max-height: 11mm !important; object-fit: contain !important; display: block !important; flex: 0 0 auto !important; flex-shrink: 0 !important; }
    </style>
  </head><body>
    <div class="preview-toolbar">
      <button class="preview-close" type="button" onclick="returnToApp()">Stäng och gå tillbaka</button>
    </div>
    <main id="spec-print" class="preview-pages">${pageMarkup}</main>
    <script>
    (function () {
      window.returnToApp = function () {
        if (window.opener && !window.opener.closed) {
          window.opener.focus();
        }
        window.close();
      };

      function fitPages() {
        var availableWidth = Math.max(1, window.innerWidth - 24);
        document.querySelectorAll(".page-frame").forEach(function (frame) {
          var page = frame.querySelector(".document-page");
          if (!page) return;
          page.style.transform = "none";
          page.style.transformOrigin = "top left";
          var naturalWidth = page.offsetWidth;
          var naturalHeight = page.scrollHeight;
          var scale = Math.min(1, availableWidth / naturalWidth);
          page.style.transform = "scale(" + scale + ")";
          frame.style.width = (naturalWidth * scale) + "px";
          frame.style.height = (naturalHeight * scale) + "px";
        });
      }

      function waitForPreviewImages() {
        return Promise.all(Array.prototype.map.call(document.querySelectorAll(".document-page img"), function (image) {
          if (image.complete) return Promise.resolve();
          return new Promise(function (resolve) {
            image.addEventListener("load", resolve, { once: true });
            image.addEventListener("error", resolve, { once: true });
          });
        }));
      }

      window.addEventListener("load", function () {
        waitForPreviewImages().then(function () {
          requestAnimationFrame(function () {
            requestAnimationFrame(fitPages);
          });
        });
      });
      window.addEventListener("resize", fitPages);
    })();
  </script></body></html>`;
}

export function previewDocument({ sourceId, title, orientation = "portrait" }: DocumentSourceOptions): boolean {
  const source = getSource(sourceId);
  const win = window.open("", "_blank", "width=1200,height=900,scrollbars=yes,resizable=yes");
  if (!win) return false;

  const html = buildPreviewDocumentHtml({
    title,
    orientation,
    documentStyles: getDocumentStyleMarkup(),
    pages: Array.from(source.children, serializePreviewPage),
  });

  win.document.write(html);
  win.document.close();
  return true;
}

export async function printDocument({ sourceId, printRootId = "spec-print-root", orientation = "portrait" }: DocumentSourceOptions): Promise<void> {
  const source = getSource(sourceId);
  const printRoot = document.getElementById(printRootId);
  if (!printRoot) throw new Error("Kunde inte förbereda utskriften");

  printRoot.replaceChildren(...Array.from(source.childNodes, node => node.cloneNode(true)));
  printRoot.removeAttribute("aria-hidden");
  document.documentElement.dataset.documentPrinting = "true";

  const cleanup = () => {
    delete document.documentElement.dataset.documentPrinting;
    printRoot.replaceChildren();
    printRoot.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
  };
  window.addEventListener("afterprint", cleanup, { once: true });
  const fallback = window.setTimeout(cleanup, 60_000);
  window.addEventListener("afterprint", () => window.clearTimeout(fallback), { once: true });

  await waitForImages(printRoot);
  await new Promise<void>(resolve => requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(() => resolve())));
  window.print();
}

export function cleanupDocumentPrint(printRootId = "spec-print-root") {
  delete document.documentElement.dataset.documentPrinting;
  const printRoot = document.getElementById(printRootId);
  printRoot?.replaceChildren();
  printRoot?.setAttribute("aria-hidden", "true");
}

async function downloadLegacyDocumentPdf(
  source: HTMLElement,
  fileName: string,
  orientation: "portrait" | "landscape",
) {
  const originalStyle = source.getAttribute("style") ?? "";
  const isLandscape = orientation === "landscape";
  const contentWidth = isLandscape ? PAGE_CONTENT_WIDTH_LANDSCAPE_MM : PAGE_CONTENT_WIDTH_MM;
  const marginX = isLandscape ? PAGE_MARGIN_X_LANDSCAPE_MM : PAGE_MARGIN_X_MM;
  const marginY = isLandscape ? PAGE_MARGIN_Y_LANDSCAPE_MM : PAGE_MARGIN_Y_MM;

  source.setAttribute("style", [
    "display:block", "visibility:visible", "position:fixed", "left:-99999px", "top:0",
    `width:${contentWidth}mm`, "height:auto", "padding:0", "overflow:visible",
    "box-sizing:border-box", "font-family:Arial,sans-serif", "font-size:9px",
    "color:#000", "background:#fff",
  ].join(";"));

  try {
    const { default: html2canvas } = await import("html2canvas");
    const { default: jsPDF } = await import("jspdf");
    await waitForImages(source);
    await new Promise<void>(resolve => requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(() => resolve())));
    const pdf = new jsPDF({ orientation, unit: "mm", format: "a4" });
    const contentHeight = pdf.internal.pageSize.getHeight() - marginY * 2;
    let firstPage = true;
    for (const page of Array.from(source.children).filter(
      (element): element is HTMLElement => element instanceof HTMLElement,
    )) {
      const canvas = await html2canvas(page, {
        scale: 2,
        useCORS: true,
        allowTaint: true,
        backgroundColor: "#ffffff",
        logging: false,
        width: page.scrollWidth,
        height: page.scrollHeight,
        windowWidth: source.scrollWidth,
        windowHeight: page.scrollHeight,
      });
      const renderedHeight = canvas.height * (contentWidth / canvas.width);
      if (!firstPage) pdf.addPage();
      firstPage = false;
      const scale = Math.min(1, contentHeight / renderedHeight);
      pdf.addImage(
        canvas.toDataURL("image/jpeg", 0.95),
        "JPEG",
        marginX,
        marginY,
        contentWidth * scale,
        renderedHeight * scale,
      );
    }
    pdf.save(fileName);
  } finally {
    source.setAttribute("style", originalStyle);
  }
}

export async function downloadDocumentPdf({ sourceId, fileName, orientation = "portrait" }: PdfOptions): Promise<void> {
  const source = getSource(sourceId);
  if (sourceId === "inventeringsrapport-preview") {
    await downloadLegacyDocumentPdf(source, fileName, orientation);
    return;
  }

  const printRootId = "spec-print-root";
  const printRoot = document.getElementById(printRootId);
  if (!printRoot) throw new Error("Kunde inte förbereda PDF-exporten");
  const originalStyle = printRoot.getAttribute("style");
  const originalAriaHidden = printRoot.getAttribute("aria-hidden");
  const originalChildren = Array.from(printRoot.childNodes);
  const originalDocumentPrinting = document.documentElement.getAttribute("data-document-printing");

  const isLandscape = orientation === "landscape";
  const contentWidth = isLandscape ? PAGE_CONTENT_WIDTH_LANDSCAPE_MM : PAGE_CONTENT_WIDTH_MM;
  const marginX = isLandscape ? PAGE_MARGIN_X_LANDSCAPE_MM : PAGE_MARGIN_X_MM;
  const marginY = isLandscape ? PAGE_MARGIN_Y_LANDSCAPE_MM : PAGE_MARGIN_Y_MM;

  let printStyle: HTMLStyleElement | null = null;

  try {
    printStyle = document.createElement("style");
    printStyle.dataset.documentPdfStyle = "true";
    printStyle.textContent = getPdfPrintCss(printRootId, orientation);
    document.head.appendChild(printStyle);
    document.documentElement.dataset.documentPrinting = "true";
    printRoot.replaceChildren(...Array.from(source.children, page => {
      const clone = page.cloneNode(true) as HTMLElement;
      clone.querySelectorAll<HTMLImageElement>(DOCUMENT_LOGO_SELECTOR).forEach(image => {
        image.dataset.documentLogo = "true";
        Object.entries(DOCUMENT_LOGO_INLINE_STYLES).forEach(([property, value]) => {
          const orientationValue = property === "max-width" && isLandscape
            ? "28mm"
            : property === "max-height" && isLandscape
              ? "14mm"
              : value;
          image.style.setProperty(property, orientationValue, "important");
        });
      });
      Object.assign(clone.style, {
        width: "100%",
        maxWidth: "100%",
        height: "auto",
        position: "static",
        transform: "none",
        transformOrigin: "initial",
        zoom: "1",
        overflow: "visible",
        boxSizing: "border-box",
      });
      return clone;
    }));
    printRoot.removeAttribute("aria-hidden");
    printRoot.setAttribute("style", [
      "display:block!important", "visibility:visible!important", "position:fixed!important",
      "left:-99999px!important", "top:0!important",
      `width:${contentWidth}mm!important`, "height:auto!important", "padding:0!important",
      "overflow:visible!important", "box-sizing:border-box!important",
      "font-family:Arial,sans-serif!important", "font-size:9px!important",
      "color:#000!important", "background:#fff!important",
      "transform:none!important", "zoom:1!important",
    ].join(";"));

    const { default: html2canvas } = await import("html2canvas");
    const { default: jsPDF } = await import("jspdf");
    await waitForDocumentLayout(printRoot);

    const pdf = new jsPDF({ orientation: orientation, unit: "mm", format: "a4" });
    const contentHeight = pdf.internal.pageSize.getHeight() - marginY * 2;
    let firstPage = true;

    for (const page of Array.from(printRoot.children).filter(
      (element): element is HTMLElement => element instanceof HTMLElement,
    )) {
      const captureWidth = printRoot.clientWidth;
      const captureHeight = page.scrollHeight;
      const canvas = await html2canvas(page, {
        scale: 2,
        useCORS: true,
        allowTaint: true,
        backgroundColor: "#ffffff",
        logging: false,
        width: captureWidth,
        height: captureHeight,
        windowWidth: captureWidth,
        windowHeight: captureHeight,
      });
      const renderedHeight = canvas.height * (contentWidth / canvas.width);
      if (renderedHeight > contentHeight + 0.5) {
        throw new Error("Dokumentsidan ryms inte på en A4-sida utan att förminskas");
      }
      if (!firstPage) pdf.addPage();
      firstPage = false;
      pdf.addImage(
        canvas.toDataURL("image/png"),
        "PNG",
        marginX,
        marginY,
        contentWidth,
        renderedHeight,
        undefined,
        "FAST",
      );
    }
    pdf.save(fileName);
  } finally {
    printStyle?.remove();
    printRoot.replaceChildren(...originalChildren);
    if (originalStyle === null) printRoot.removeAttribute("style");
    else printRoot.setAttribute("style", originalStyle);
    if (originalAriaHidden === null) printRoot.removeAttribute("aria-hidden");
    else printRoot.setAttribute("aria-hidden", originalAriaHidden);
    if (originalDocumentPrinting === null) {
      delete document.documentElement.dataset.documentPrinting;
    } else {
      document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-document-printing", originalDocumentPrinting);
    }
  }
}

describe("direkt PDF visuell regression", () => {
  it("renderar specifikation 168 som två separata A4-sidor utan överlappning", () => {
    const fixturePath = "/tmp/spec-168-regression.html";
    const renderedPath = "/tmp/spec-168-regression-rendered.html";
    const pdfPath = "/tmp/spec-168-regression.pdf";
    const pngPrefix = "/tmp/spec-168-regression-page";
    writeFileSync(fixturePath, buildSpec168Fixture());
    const output = openSync(renderedPath, "w");
    try {
      execFileSync(chromium, [
        "--headless",
        "--no-sandbox",
        "--disable-gpu",
        "--virtual-time-budget=30000",
        "--dump-dom",
        `file://${fixturePath}`,
      ], { stdio: ["ignore", output, "ignore"] });
    } finally {
      closeSync(output);
    }

    const rendered = readFileSync(renderedPath, "utf8");
    expect(rendered.match(/data-ready="([^"]+)"/)?.[1]).toBe("true");
    const measurementValue = rendered.match(/data-measurement="([^"]+)"/)?.[1];
    const pdfValue = rendered.match(/data-pdf="([^"]+)"/)?.[1];
    expect(measurementValue).toBeTruthy();
    expect(pdfValue).toBeTruthy();
    const measurement = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(measurementValue!.replaceAll("&amp;", "&"))) as {
      pageWidths: number[];
      pageHeights: number[];
      headerOverlap: boolean;
      rowOverlap: boolean;
      headersOverflow: boolean;
      summaryVisible: boolean;
      logo: { width: number; height: number };
      addressText: string;
    };
    writeFileSync(pdfPath, Buffer.from(pdfValue!, "base64"));

    expect(measurement.pageWidths).toHaveLength(2);
    measurement.pageWidths.forEach(width => expect(width).toBeCloseTo(703, 0));
    expect(measurement.headerOverlap).toBe(false);
    expect(measurement.rowOverlap).toBe(false);
    expect(measurement.headersOverflow).toBe(false);
    expect(measurement.summaryVisible).toBe(true);
    expect(measurement.addressText).toContain("Testkund AB\nIndustrigatan 12");
    expect(measurement.logo.width).toBeLessThanOrEqual(84);
    expect(measurement.logo.height).toBeLessThanOrEqual(42);
    expect(execFileSync("pdfinfo", [pdfPath], { encoding: "utf8" })).toMatch(/Pages:\s+2/);

    for (const page of [1, 2]) rmSync(`${pngPrefix}-${page}.png`, { force: true });
    execFileSync("pdftoppm", ["-png", "-r", "110", pdfPath, pngPrefix]);
    for (const page of [1, 2]) {
      const pngPath = `${pngPrefix}-${page}.png`;
      expect(existsSync(pngPath)).toBe(true);
      expect(statSync(pngPath).size).toBeGreaterThan(10_000);
    }
  }, 60_000);
});

import { describe, expect, test } from "vitest";
import {
  documentProductGroupKey,
  formatDocumentProductLabel,
} from "./documentProduct";

describe("dokumentens produktbeskrivning", () => {
  test.each([
    [
      { traslag: "Gran", profil: "Klädsel", behandling: "Grundmålad" },
      "Gran / Klädsel / Grundmålad",
    ],
    [
      { traslag: "Furu", profil: "Trall", behandling: "Impregnerad" },
      "Furu / Trall / Impregnerad",
    ],
    [
      { traslag: "Gran", profil: "Klädsel", behandling: "  " },
      "Gran / Klädsel",
    ],
    [
      {
        traslag: "Gran",
        profil: "Klädsel",
        kvalitet: "G4-2",
        behandling: "Grundmålad",
      },
      "Gran / Klädsel / G4-2 / Grundmålad",
    ],
  ])("formaterar %j som %s", (product, expected) => {
    expect(formatDocumentProductLabel(product)).toBe(expected);
  });

  test("visar inte utförande i dokumenttexten", () => {
    expect(formatDocumentProductLabel({
      traslag: "Gran",
      profil: "Klädsel",
      utforande: "Buntat",
    })).toBe("Gran / Klädsel");

    expect(formatDocumentProductLabel({
      traslag: "Furu",
      profil: "Trall",
      utforande: "4RH",
    })).toBe("Furu / Trall");
  });
});

describe("dokumentens produktgruppering", () => {
  const dimensions = [22, 145, 19, 140];

  test("skiljer Grundmålad från obehandlat vid samma dimension", () => {
    const keys = new Set([
      documentProductGroupKey(
        { traslag: "Gran", profil: "Klädsel", behandling: "Grundmålad" },
        dimensions,
      ),
      documentProductGroupKey(
        { traslag: "Gran", profil: "Klädsel", behandling: null },
        dimensions,
      ),
    ]);

    expect(keys.size).toBe(2);
  });

  test("skiljer Impregnerad från obehandlat vid samma dimension", () => {
    expect(documentProductGroupKey(
      { traslag: "Furu", profil: "Trall", behandling: "Impregnerad" },
      dimensions,
    )).not.toBe(documentProductGroupKey(
      { traslag: "Furu", profil: "Trall", behandling: null },
      dimensions,
    ));
  });

  test("utförande visas inte och påverkar inte dokumentgrupperingen", () => {
    expect(documentProductGroupKey(
      { traslag: "Gran", profil: "Klädsel", utforande: "Buntat" },
      dimensions,
    )).toBe(documentProductGroupKey(
      { traslag: "Gran", profil: "Klädsel", utforande: "4RH" },
      dimensions,
    ));
  });
});

================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/lib/documentProduct.ts ================

export type DocumentProduct = {
  traslag?: string | null;
  profil?: string | null;
  kvalitet?: string | null;
  behandling?: string | null;
  utforande?: string | null;
};

function normalizedPart(value: string | null | undefined): string {
  return value?.trim() ?? "";
}

export function formatDocumentProductLabel(product: DocumentProduct): string {
  const parts = [
    product.traslag,
    product.profil,
    product.kvalitet,
    product.behandling,
  ]
    .map(normalizedPart)
    .filter(Boolean)
    .filter((value, index, values) =>
      values.findIndex(other =>
        other.localeCompare(value, "sv", { sensitivity: "base" }) === 0
      ) === index
    );

  return parts.join(" / ") || "—";
}

/**
 * Identitet för dokumentgruppering. Utförande ingår avsiktligt inte:
 * Specifikation och Följesedel grupperar på produkt + behandling, inte utförande.
 */
export function documentProductGroupKey(
  product: DocumentProduct,
  additionalValues: Array<string | number | null | undefined> = [],
): string {
  const productValues = [
    product.traslag,
    product.profil,
    product.kvalitet,
    product.behandling,
  ];

  return [...productValues, ...additionalValues]
    .map(value => normalizedPart(value == null ? null : String(value)).toLocaleLowerCase("sv-SE"))
    .join("|");
}

================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/lib/exportInventeringCsv.ts ================

import type { RapportFlatEntry } from "./printInventeringLista";

function csvEscape(v: string): string {
  if (v.includes(";") || v.includes('"') || v.includes("\n")) {
    return `"${v.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
  }
  return v;
}

function tal(n: number, decimaler = 3): string {
  return n.toFixed(decimaler).replace(".", ",");
}

interface Totaler {
  totaltStyck: number;
  totaltNomM3: number;
  totaltVarde: number | null;
}

/**
 * Exporterar inventeringens rapportposter (paketrader plus grupphuvuden med
 * delsumma, i exakt samma flernivågruppering/sortering som visas på
 * skärmen) som semikolonseparerad CSV (UTF-8 med BOM för å/ä/ö i Excel).
 * Grupphuvuden skrivs som en egen rad i den första kolumnen (indragen per
 * nivå) med övriga kolumner tomma, så strukturen går att läsa rakt av även
 * utan pivotering.
 */
export function exportInventeringCsv(filename: string, entries: RapportFlatEntry[], visaPriser: boolean, totaler: Totaler) {
  const headers = [
    "Grupp", "Pkt-nr", "Träslag", "Dimension", "Kvalitet", "Profil",
    "Längd(er) mm", "Styck", "NOM m³", "Område", "Fack",
  ];
  if (visaPriser) headers.push("Pris kr/NOM m³", "Paketvärde kr");
  const tomKol = headers.length - 1; // antal tomma kolumner efter "Grupp"-cellen

  const rows: string[][] = [];
  for (const e of entries) {
    if (e.kind === "header") {
      const indrag = "  ".repeat(Math.max(0, e.level - 1));
      let text = `${indrag}${e.label} — ${e.count} paket · ${tal(e.nomM3, 2)} m³ (${e.styck} st)`;
      if (e.varde != null) text += ` · ${tal(e.varde, 2)} kr`;
      rows.push([text, ...Array(tomKol).fill("")]);
      continue;
    }
    const r = e.row;
    const dim = r.nomTjocklek && r.nomBredd ? `${r.nomTjocklek}×${r.nomBredd}` : "";
    const langder = r.langder.map((l) => String(l * 100)).join(", ");
    const cols = [
      "",
      r.pktNr,
      r.traslag ?? "",
      dim,
      r.kvalitet ?? "",
      r.profil ?? "",
      langder,
      String(r.styck),
      tal(r.nomM3),
      r.blandadPlacering ? "Blandad placering" : (r.omradeNamn ?? ""),
      r.blandadPlacering ? "" : (r.fackKod ?? ""),
    ];
    if (visaPriser) {
      cols.push(r.pris ? tal(r.pris.prisPerNomM3, 2) : "");
      cols.push(r.pris ? tal(r.pris.lagervarde, 2) : "");
    }
    rows.push(cols);
  }

  let totalRad = `Totalt — ${totaler.totaltStyck} st · ${tal(totaler.totaltNomM3, 2)} m³`;
  if (totaler.totaltVarde != null) totalRad += ` · ${tal(totaler.totaltVarde, 2)} kr`;
  rows.push([totalRad, ...Array(tomKol).fill("")]);

  const lines = [headers, ...rows].map((cols) => cols.map(csvEscape).join(";"));
  const csv = "\uFEFF" + lines.join("\r\n");
  const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8" });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement("a");
  a.href = url;
  a.download = filename;
  a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/lib/printInventeringLista.tsx ================

import { renderToStaticMarkup } from "react-dom/server";
import type { RapportRad } from "@/pages/inventering/types";

const PRINT_STYLE = `
  * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
  body { background: #fff; font-family: Arial, 'Helvetica Neue', sans-serif; color: #000; padding: 8mm; }
  h1 { font-size: 15pt; margin-bottom: 1mm; }
  .meta { font-size: 9pt; color: #444; margin-bottom: 5mm; }
  table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size: 8.5pt; }
  th, td { text-align: left; padding: 1.3mm 2mm; border-bottom: 0.5px solid #ddd; }
  th { text-transform: uppercase; font-size: 6.8pt; color: #555; letter-spacing: 0.04em; border-bottom: 1.5px solid #000; }
  tr.group-header td { background: #eee; font-weight: 700; border-top: 1px solid #999; border-bottom: 1px solid #999; padding-top: 2mm; padding-bottom: 1.5mm; }
  .num { text-align: right; }
  tfoot td { font-weight: 900; font-size: 9.5pt; border-top: 2px solid #000; padding-top: 2mm; }
  @media print { @page { size: A4 landscape; margin: 10mm; } }
`;

/**
 * Platt lista över post-rader för utskrift/export: en "header"-post per
 * grupp (på valfri nivå/djup) med delsumman redan beräknad, direkt följd av
 * dess underliggande grupper eller — på lägsta nivå — de enskilda paketen.
 * Samma lista driver skärm, utskrift och CSV så alla tre alltid visar
 * exakt den flernivågruppering användaren har valt.
 */
export type RapportFlatEntry =
  | { kind: "header"; level: number; label: string; count: number; styck: number; nomM3: number; varde: number | null }
  | { kind: "row"; row: RapportRad };

interface RapportProps {
  titel: string;
  undertitel: string;
  visaPriser: boolean;
  entries: RapportFlatEntry[];
  totaltStyck: number;
  totaltNomM3: number;
  totaltVarde: number | null;
}

function kr(n: number): string {
  return n.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { maximumFractionDigits: 0 });
}

function Rapport({ titel, undertitel, visaPriser, entries, totaltStyck, totaltNomM3, totaltVarde }: RapportProps) {
  const kolAntal = visaPriser ? 12 : 10;
  return (
    <div>
      <h1>{titel}</h1>
      <div className="meta">{undertitel}</div>
      <table>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th>Pkt-nr</th>
            <th>Träslag</th>
            <th>Dimension</th>
            <th>Kvalitet</th>
            <th>Profil</th>
            <th>Längd(er) mm</th>
            <th className="num">Styck</th>
            <th className="num">NOM m³</th>
            <th>Område</th>
            <th>Fack</th>
            {visaPriser && <th className="num">Pris kr/NOM m³</th>}
            {visaPriser && <th className="num">Paketvärde kr</th>}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {entries.map((e, i) => {
            if (e.kind === "header") {
              return (
                <tr className="group-header" key={`h-${i}`}>
                  <td colSpan={kolAntal} style={{ paddingLeft: `${(e.level - 1) * 5 + 2}mm` }}>
                    {e.label} — {e.count} paket · {e.styck} st · {e.nomM3.toFixed(2)} m³
                    {e.varde != null && <span> · {kr(e.varde)} kr</span>}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              );
            }
            const r = e.row;
            return (
              <tr key={r.pktNr}>
                <td>{r.pktNr}</td>
                <td>{r.traslag ?? "—"}</td>
                <td>{r.nomTjocklek && r.nomBredd ? `${r.nomTjocklek}×${r.nomBredd}` : "—"}</td>
                <td>{r.kvalitet ?? "—"}</td>
                <td>{r.profil ?? "—"}</td>
                <td>{r.langder.length ? r.langder.map((l) => l * 100).join(", ") : "—"}</td>
                <td className="num">{r.styck}</td>
                <td className="num">{r.nomM3.toFixed(3)}</td>
                <td>{r.blandadPlacering ? "Blandad placering" : r.omradeNamn ?? "—"}</td>
                <td>{r.blandadPlacering ? "—" : r.fackKod ?? "—"}</td>
                {visaPriser && <td className="num">{r.pris ? kr(r.pris.prisPerNomM3) : "—"}</td>}
                {visaPriser && <td className="num">{r.pris ? kr(r.pris.lagervarde) : "—"}</td>}
              </tr>
            );
          })}
        </tbody>
        <tfoot>
          <tr>
            <td colSpan={6}>Totalt</td>
            <td className="num">{totaltStyck}</td>
            <td className="num">{totaltNomM3.toFixed(3)}</td>
            <td colSpan={2} />
            {visaPriser && <td />}
            {visaPriser && <td className="num">{totaltVarde != null ? kr(totaltVarde) : "—"}</td>}
          </tr>
        </tfoot>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

/** Öppnar ett utskriftsfönster med inventeringsrapporten. Måste anropas
 * synkront i en klick-callback (öppnar fönstret innan render) för att
 * undvika popup-blockeraren, precis som printPaketlapp.tsx. */
export function printInventeringLista(props: RapportProps) {
  const win = window.open("", "_blank");
  if (!win) return;
  const html = renderToStaticMarkup(<Rapport {...props} />);
  win.document.open();
  win.document.write(`<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="sv">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8" />
  <title>${props.titel}</title>
  <style>${PRINT_STYLE}</style>
</head>
<body>${html}</body>
</html>`);
  win.document.close();
  win.focus();
  setTimeout(() => win.print(), 400);
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/lib/printPaketlapp.tsx ================

import { renderToStaticMarkup } from "react-dom/server";
import QRCode from "qrcode";
import PaketlappPrint from "@/pages/inventory/PaketlappPrint";
import type { LagerPost } from "@workspace/db";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";

const PRINT_STYLE = `
  * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
  body { background: #fff; }
  @media print {
    html, body { width: 210mm; height: 297mm; }
    @page { size: A4 portrait; margin: 0; }
  }
`;

const LOADING_HTML = `<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="sv"><head><meta charset="utf-8" />
<style>body{font-family:sans-serif;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;height:100vh;margin:0;color:#555}</style>
</head><body><p>Laddar paketlapp…</p></body></html>`;

async function fetchSiblings(post: LagerPost): Promise<LagerPost[]> {
  if (!post.pktNr) return [post];
  try {
    const all = await customFetch<LagerPost[]>(`/api/lager/alla-pktnr/${encodeURIComponent(post.pktNr)}`);
    if (all.length > 1) return all.sort((a, b) => (Number(a.langdDm) || 0) - (Number(b.langdDm) || 0));
  } catch { /* fall through */ }
  return [post];
}

async function fillWindow(win: Window, post: LagerPost) {
  let qrDataUrl: string | undefined;
  try {
    const url = `${window.location.origin}/lager/${post.id}`;
    qrDataUrl = await QRCode.toDataURL(url, { width: 160, margin: 1, color: { dark: "#000", light: "#fff" } });
  } catch { /* skip if QR fails */ }

  const siblings = await fetchSiblings(post);
  const html = renderToStaticMarkup(
    <PaketlappPrint post={post} qrDataUrl={qrDataUrl} siblings={siblings} />
  );
  win.document.open();
  win.document.write(`<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="sv">
<head>
  <meta charset="utf-8" />
  <title>Paketlapp – Pkt-Nr ${post.pktNr || post.id}</title>
  <style>${PRINT_STYLE}</style>
</head>
<body>${html}</body>
</html>`);
  win.document.close();
  win.focus();
  setTimeout(() => win.print(), 400);
}

/** Används från InventoryList (hämtar data via API) */
export async function printPaketlappById(id: number) {
  // Öppna fönstret OMEDELBART — måste ske synkront i click-callbacken
  // annars blockerar popup-blockeraren det.
  const win = window.open("", "_blank");
  if (!win) return;
  win.document.write(LOADING_HTML);

  const post = await customFetch<LagerPost>(`/api/lager/${id}`);
  await fillWindow(win, post);
}

/** Används från InventoryDetail (datan finns redan) */
export async function printPaketlappForPost(post: LagerPost) {
  const win = window.open("", "_blank");
  if (!win) return;
  win.document.write(LOADING_HTML);
  await fillWindow(win, post);
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/lib/scanImportLocation.test.ts ================

import { describe, expect, test } from "vitest";
import {
  copyDefaultPlacement,
  effectivePackagePlacement,
  filterFackForArea,
  placementAfterAreaChange,
} from "./scanImportLocation";

describe("strukturerad placering vid dokumentimport", () => {
  test("kopierar standardplaceringen till alla markerade paket", () => {
    expect(copyDefaultPlacement([0, 2], { omradeId: 7, fackId: 70 })).toEqual({
      0: { omradeId: 7, fackId: 70 },
      2: { omradeId: 7, fackId: 70 },
    });
  });

  test("ett paket kan få en individuell placering", () => {
    expect(effectivePackagePlacement(
      true,
      { omradeId: 7, fackId: 70 },
      { 2: { omradeId: 8, fackId: null } },
      2,
    )).toEqual({ omradeId: 8, fackId: null });
  });

  test("standardplaceringen gäller när individuell fördelning är avstängd", () => {
    expect(effectivePackagePlacement(
      false,
      { omradeId: 7, fackId: 70 },
      { 2: { omradeId: 8, fackId: 80 } },
      2,
    )).toEqual({ omradeId: 7, fackId: 70 });
  });

  test("facklistan innehåller bara fack från valt område", () => {
    expect(filterFackForArea({
      7: [{ id: 70, kod: "V01" }],
      8: [{ id: 80, kod: "H01" }],
    }, 7)).toEqual([{ id: 70, kod: "V01" }]);
  });

  test("områdesbyte rensar tidigare fack", () => {
    expect(placementAfterAreaChange(8)).toEqual({ omradeId: 8, fackId: null });
  });

  test("ingen lagerplats representeras av null för både område och fack", () => {
    expect(placementAfterAreaChange(null)).toEqual({ omradeId: null, fackId: null });
    expect(filterFackForArea({}, null)).toEqual([]);
  });
});

================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/lib/scanImportLocation.ts ================

export type ScanImportPlacement = {
  omradeId: number | null;
  fackId: number | null;
};

export type ScanImportFack = {
  id: number;
  kod: string;
};

export function placementAfterAreaChange(omradeId: number | null): ScanImportPlacement {
  return { omradeId, fackId: null };
}

export function filterFackForArea(
  details: Record<number, ScanImportFack[]>,
  omradeId: number | null,
): ScanImportFack[] {
  return omradeId == null ? [] : details[omradeId] ?? [];
}

export function effectivePackagePlacement(
  distribute: boolean,
  defaultPlacement: ScanImportPlacement,
  packagePlacements: Record<number, ScanImportPlacement>,
  packageIndex: number,
): ScanImportPlacement {
  return distribute
    ? packagePlacements[packageIndex] ?? defaultPlacement
    : defaultPlacement;
}

export function copyDefaultPlacement(
  selectedIndexes: Iterable<number>,
  defaultPlacement: ScanImportPlacement,
): Record<number, ScanImportPlacement> {
  return Object.fromEntries(
    Array.from(selectedIndexes, (index) => [index, { ...defaultPlacement }]),
  );
}

================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/lib/specDocumentFields.test.tsx ================

import React from "react";
import { renderToStaticMarkup } from "react-dom/server";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  buildDocumentReferenceLines,
  DocumentReferenceContent,
  DocumentSenderContact,
  DocumentCertificationBlock,
  DocumentCompanyFooter,
  DocumentInvoiceDetailsBlock,
  formatDocumentOrderNumber,
} from "./specDocumentFields";

describe("spec- och följesedelsfält", () => {
  it("avsändarkontakten ger telefon och e-post på separata rader", () => {
    const markup = renderToStaticMarkup(
      <DocumentSenderContact companyName="Testbolaget" phone="0471 10485" email="info@emmabodahyvleri.se" />,
    );
    expect(markup).toContain("Tel: 0471 10485</div><div");
    expect(markup).toContain(">info@emmabodahyvleri.se</div>");
    expect(markup).not.toContain("10485 · info@");
  });

  it("referensrutan visar endast Er referens när anteckning saknas", () => {
    expect(buildDocumentReferenceLines("Mikael Andersson", "")).toEqual([
      { label: "Er referens", value: "Mikael Andersson" },
    ]);
  });

  it("referensrutan visar endast Anteckning när referens saknas", () => {
    expect(buildDocumentReferenceLines(null, "Ring före leverans")).toEqual([
      { label: "Anteckning", value: "Ring före leverans" },
    ]);
  });

  it("referens och anteckning renderas som separata rader", () => {
    const markup = renderToStaticMarkup(
      <DocumentReferenceContent erReferens="Mikael Andersson" anteckning="Ring före leverans" />,
    );
    expect(markup).toMatch(/Er referens:.*Mikael Andersson.*Anteckning:.*Ring före leverans/);
  });

  it("tom referensruta är tom och känner inte till ordernummer", () => {
    expect(buildDocumentReferenceLines("", " ")).toEqual([]);
    const markup = renderToStaticMarkup(<DocumentReferenceContent />);
    expect(markup).not.toContain("Order");
  });

  it("ordernummer visar endast värdet eller ett streck när det är tomt", () => {
    expect(formatDocumentOrderNumber("456789")).toBe("456789");
    expect(formatDocumentOrderNumber("")).toBe("—");
    expect(formatDocumentOrderNumber("   ")).toBe("—");
    expect(formatDocumentOrderNumber(null)).toBe("—");
  });

  it("långa anteckningar har brytregler för PDF-layouten", () => {
    const markup = renderToStaticMarkup(
      <DocumentReferenceContent anteckning={"Lång transportanteckning ".repeat(20)} />,
    );
    expect(markup).toContain("white-space:pre-wrap");
    expect(markup).toContain("overflow-wrap:anywhere");
    expect(markup).toContain("word-break:break-word");
  });

  it("optional document blocks do not leave headings or empty space", () => {
    expect(renderToStaticMarkup(<DocumentCompanyFooter profile={{}} />)).toBe("");
    expect(renderToStaticMarkup(<DocumentInvoiceDetailsBlock invoiceDetails=" " />)).toBe("");
    expect(renderToStaticMarkup(<DocumentCertificationBlock show certificationText="" certificationNumber="" />)).toBe("");
  });

  it("certification is only rendered by its explicit per-document switch", () => {
    expect(renderToStaticMarkup(
      <DocumentCertificationBlock show={false} certificationText="Certified stock" certificationNumber="ABC-1" />,
    )).toBe("");
    expect(renderToStaticMarkup(
      <DocumentCertificationBlock show certificationText="Certified stock" certificationNumber="ABC-1" />,
    )).toContain("Certificate No. ABC-1");
  });
});

================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/lib/specDocumentFields.tsx ================

import React, { type CSSProperties } from "react";

export type DocumentReferenceLine = {
  label: "Er referens" | "Anteckning";
  value: string;
};

export function formatDocumentOrderNumber(orderNr: string | null | undefined): string {
  return orderNr?.trim() || "—";
}

export function buildDocumentReferenceLines(
  erReferens: string | null | undefined,
  anteckning: string | null | undefined,
): DocumentReferenceLine[] {
  const lines: DocumentReferenceLine[] = [];
  const referenceValue = erReferens?.trim();
  const noteValue = anteckning?.trim();
  if (referenceValue) lines.push({ label: "Er referens", value: referenceValue });
  if (noteValue) lines.push({ label: "Anteckning", value: noteValue });
  return lines;
}

export function DocumentSenderContact({
  companyName,
  streetAddress,
  postalCode,
  city,
  country,
  phone,
  email,
  companyFontSize = "11px",
  lineHeight = "1.5",
}: {
  companyName: string;
  streetAddress?: string | null;
  postalCode?: string | null;
  city?: string | null;
  country?: string | null;
  phone?: string | null;
  email?: string | null;
  companyFontSize?: CSSProperties["fontSize"];
  lineHeight?: CSSProperties["lineHeight"];
}) {
  return (
    <div style={{ lineHeight }}>
      {companyName && <div style={{ fontWeight: "bold", fontSize: companyFontSize, letterSpacing: "0.3px" }}>{companyName}</div>}
      {streetAddress && <div style={{ fontSize: "8.5px" }}>{streetAddress}</div>}
      {(postalCode || city) && <div style={{ fontSize: "8.5px" }}>{[postalCode, city].filter(Boolean).join(" ")}</div>}
      {country && <div style={{ fontSize: "8.5px" }}>{country}</div>}
      {phone && <div style={{ fontSize: "8.5px" }}>Tel: {phone}</div>}
      {email && <div style={{ fontSize: "8.5px", overflowWrap: "anywhere" }}>{email}</div>}
    </div>
  );
}

export function DocumentReferenceContent({
  erReferens,
  anteckning,
}: {
  erReferens?: string | null;
  anteckning?: string | null;
}) {
  return (
    <div style={{ display: "flex", minWidth: 0, flexDirection: "column", gap: "1.5mm", fontSize: "9.5px" }}>
      {buildDocumentReferenceLines(erReferens, anteckning).map((line) => (
        <div key={line.label} style={{ minWidth: 0, whiteSpace: "pre-wrap", overflowWrap: "anywhere", wordBreak: "break-word" }}>
          <span style={{ fontWeight: "bold" }}>{line.label}: </span>
          <span>{line.value}</span>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

/** The subset of a sender profile that is meaningful in a document footer. */
export type DocumentFooterProfile = {
  companyName?: string | null;
  streetAddress?: string | null;
  postalCode?: string | null;
  city?: string | null;
  country?: string | null;
  phone?: string | null;
  email?: string | null;
  website?: string | null;
  organizationNumber?: string | null;
  vatNumber?: string | null;
  approvedForFTax?: boolean | null;
};

const present = (value: string | null | undefined) => value?.trim() || "";

/** A compact, snapshot-safe footer. Empty columns (and the entire footer) disappear. */
export function DocumentCompanyFooter({ profile }: { profile: DocumentFooterProfile }) {
  const address = [
    present(profile.companyName), present(profile.streetAddress),
    [present(profile.postalCode), present(profile.city)].filter(Boolean).join(" "),
    present(profile.country),
  ].filter(Boolean);
  const contact = [present(profile.phone), present(profile.email), present(profile.website)].filter(Boolean);
  const registration = [
    present(profile.organizationNumber) && `Org.nr: ${present(profile.organizationNumber)}`,
    present(profile.vatNumber) && `VAT-nr: ${present(profile.vatNumber)}`,
    profile.approvedForFTax ? "Godkänd för F-skatt" : "",
  ].filter(Boolean);
  const columns = [address, contact, registration].filter(column => column.length);
  if (!columns.length) return null;

  return (
    <footer className="document-company-footer" style={{
      marginTop: "3mm", paddingTop: "2mm", borderTop: "1px solid #777",
      display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: `repeat(${columns.length}, minmax(0, 1fr))`,
      gap: "4mm", fontSize: "7.5px", lineHeight: 1.35, color: "#444",
      breakInside: "avoid", pageBreakInside: "avoid",
    }}>
      {columns.map((column, index) => (
        <div key={index} style={{ minWidth: 0, overflowWrap: "anywhere" }}>
          {column.map(line => <div key={line}>{line}</div>)}
        </div>
      ))}
    </footer>
  );
}

/**
 * This deliberately requires the document's explicit switch. A company
 * certificate or a product's legacy certificate must never imply permission
 * to print this declaration.
 */
export function DocumentCertificationBlock({
  show,
  certificationText,
  certificationNumber,
}: {
  show: boolean | null | undefined;
  certificationText?: string | null;
  certificationNumber?: string | null;
}) {
  if (!show || (!present(certificationText) && !present(certificationNumber))) return null;
  return (
    <section className="document-certification-block" style={{
      marginTop: "3mm", paddingTop: "2mm", borderTop: "1px solid #999",
      fontSize: "8px", lineHeight: 1.4, breakInside: "avoid", pageBreakInside: "avoid",
      overflowWrap: "anywhere",
    }}>
      {present(certificationNumber) && <div>Certificate No. {present(certificationNumber)}</div>}
      {present(certificationText) && <div>{present(certificationText)}</div>}
    </section>
  );
}

export function DocumentInvoiceDetailsBlock({ invoiceDetails }: { invoiceDetails?: string | null }) {
  const details = present(invoiceDetails);
  if (!details) return null;
  return (
    <section className="document-invoice-details-block" style={{
      marginTop: "3mm", padding: "2mm", border: "1px solid #999", fontSize: "8px",
      lineHeight: 1.4, breakInside: "avoid", pageBreakInside: "avoid", overflowWrap: "anywhere",
    }}>
      <strong>Fakturauppgifter</strong>
      <div style={{ whiteSpace: "pre-wrap" }}>{details}</div>
    </section>
  );
}

================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/lib/structuredLagerplats.ts ================

type StructuredLagerplats = {
  plats?: string | null;
  omradeNamn?: string | null;
  fackKod?: string | null;
};

export function formatStructuredLagerplats(post: StructuredLagerplats): string {
  if (post.omradeNamn) {
    return post.fackKod
      ? `${post.omradeNamn} · Fack ${post.fackKod}`
      : `${post.omradeNamn} · Inget fack`;
  }

  return post.plats ?? "-";
}

export function getStructuredLagerplatsDetails(post: StructuredLagerplats): {
  omrade: string;
  fack: string;
} {
  return {
    omrade: post.omradeNamn ?? post.plats ?? "-",
    fack: post.omradeNamn ? post.fackKod ?? "Inget fack" : "-",
  };
}

================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/lib/utils.ts ================

import { clsx, type ClassValue } from "clsx"
import { twMerge } from "tailwind-merge"

export function cn(...inputs: ClassValue[]) {
  return twMerge(clsx(inputs))
}

if ("serviceWorker" in navigator) {
  let reloadingForUpdate = false;
  navigator.serviceWorker.addEventListener("controllerchange", () => {
    if (reloadingForUpdate) return;
    reloadingForUpdate = true;
    window.location.reload();
  });
}

            få tillgång till systemet.

const TRASLAG_COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
  Gran: "hsl(142, 71%, 45%)",
  Fur:  "hsl(38, 92%, 50%)",
  Övrigt: "hsl(220, 14%, 60%)",

type TrendPoint = { manad: string; label: string; m3In: number; m3Hyvlat: number; m3Ut: number; m3HyvlatILager: number; antalIn: number; antalHyvlat: number; antalUt: number };
type LagertrendPoint = { datum: string; label: string; m3: number };
type RecentSpec = { id: number; kund: string | null; status: string; skapadAt: string; bekraftadAt: string | null; paketCount: number };

function ProgressBar({ andel, color }: { andel: number; color: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="w-full h-2 bg-muted rounded-full overflow-hidden">
      <div
        className="h-full rounded-full transition-all"
        style={{ width: `${Math.min(andel, 100)}%`, backgroundColor: color }}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

function KpiCard({ title, value, sub, icon: Icon, accent, accentBlue, accentGreen, onClick }: {
  title: string;
  value: string;
  sub: string;
  icon: React.ComponentType<{ className?: string }>;
  accent?: boolean;
  accentBlue?: boolean;
  accentGreen?: boolean;
  onClick?: () => void;
}) {
  const cardCls = accent
    ? "border-green-200 bg-green-50/40"
    : accentBlue
    ? "border-blue-200 bg-blue-50/40"
    : accentGreen
    ? "border-emerald-200 bg-emerald-50/40"
    : "";
  const iconCls = accent
    ? "text-green-600"
    : accentBlue
    ? "text-blue-600"
    : accentGreen
    ? "text-emerald-600"
    : "text-muted-foreground";
  const valueCls = accent ? "text-green-700" : accentBlue ? "text-blue-700" : accentGreen ? "text-emerald-700" : "";
  return (
    <Card
      className={`${cardCls} ${onClick ? "cursor-pointer hover:shadow-md transition-shadow" : ""}`}
      onClick={onClick}
    >
      <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0 pb-2">
        <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium">{title}</CardTitle>
        <Icon className={`h-4 w-4 ${iconCls}`} />
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent>
        <div className={`text-2xl font-bold ${valueCls}`}>{value}</div>
        <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{sub}{onClick ? " — klicka för lista" : ""}</p>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

type FlodeTyp = "sagat_in" | "hyvlat_in" | "levererat_ut";

const FLODE_META: Record<FlodeTyp, { title: string; datumLabel: string; color: string }> = {

  hyvlat_in:     { title: "Hyvlat in (30 dagar)", datumLabel: "Datum in", color: "text-emerald-700" },
  levererat_ut:  { title: "Levererat ut (30 dagar)", datumLabel: "Datum ut", color: "text-green-700" },
};

function TrendTooltip({ active, payload, label }: { active?: boolean; payload?: { value: number; name: string }[]; label?: string }) {
  if (!active || !payload?.length) return null;
  return (
    <div className="rounded-lg border bg-background p-3 shadow-sm text-sm">
      <div className="font-semibold mb-1">{label}</div>
      {payload.map((p, i) => (
        <div key={i} className="text-muted-foreground">{p.name}: <span className="text-foreground font-medium">{p.value}</span></div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function Dashboard() {
  const [flodeOpen, setFlodeOpen] = useState<FlodeTyp | null>(null);

  const { data: flodePaket = [], isFetching: flodeLoading } = useQuery<any[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/lager/flode", flodeOpen],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<any[]>(`/api/lager/flode?typ=${flodeOpen}`),
    enabled: !!flodeOpen,
    staleTime: 30_000,
  });

  const { data: statistik, isLoading, error } = useGetLagerStatistik({
    query: {
      queryKey: getGetLagerStatistikQueryKey(),
      refetchInterval: 60_000,
      staleTime: 30_000,
      refetchOnWindowFocus: true,
    }
  });

  const { data: larmTriggered = [] } = useQuery<{ id: number; namn: string; currentM3: number; minM3: number }[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/larm/status"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<{ id: number; namn: string; currentM3: number; minM3: number }[]>("/api/larm/status"),
    refetchInterval: 60_000,
    staleTime: 30_000,
  });

  const { data: leveranstrend = [] } = useQuery<TrendPoint[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/lager/leveranstrend"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<TrendPoint[]>("/api/lager/leveranstrend"),
    staleTime: 30_000,
    refetchInterval: 60_000,
    refetchOnWindowFocus: true,
  });

  const { data: lagertrend = [] } = useQuery<LagertrendPoint[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/lager/lagertrend"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<LagertrendPoint[]>("/api/lager/lagertrend"),
    staleTime: 30_000,
    refetchInterval: 60_000,
    refetchOnWindowFocus: true,
  });

  const { data: recentSpecs = [] } = useQuery<RecentSpec[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/spec"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<RecentSpec[]>("/api/spec"),
    staleTime: 30_000,
  });

  const senasteFemSpecs = recentSpecs.slice(0, 5);

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <div className="space-y-6">
        <div>
          <Skeleton className="h-8 w-48 mb-2" />
          <Skeleton className="h-4 w-64" />
        </div>
        <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-5">
          {[...Array(5)].map((_, i) => (
            <Skeleton key={i} className="h-32 rounded-xl" />
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          <Skeleton className="h-48 rounded-xl" />
          <Skeleton className="h-48 rounded-xl" />
          <Skeleton className="h-48 rounded-xl" />
        </div>
        <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
          <Skeleton className="h-[360px] rounded-xl" />
          <Skeleton className="h-[360px] rounded-xl" />
        </div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (error || !statistik) {
    return (
      <Alert variant="destructive">
        <AlertCircle className="h-4 w-4" />
        <AlertTitle>Ett fel uppstod</AlertTitle>
        <AlertDescription>
          Kunde inte hämta lagerstatistik. Vänligen försök igen senare.
        </AlertDescription>
      </Alert>
    );
  }

  const varuslagColors = [
    "hsl(var(--chart-1))",
    "hsl(var(--chart-2))",
    "hsl(var(--chart-3))",
    "hsl(var(--chart-4))",
    "hsl(var(--chart-5))",
  ];

  const svh = statistik.sagadVsHyvlat;
  const topDim = statistik.topSagadeDimensioner ?? [];
  const tradslag = statistik.tradslagFordelning ?? [];

  // Max value for the trend chart for nice Y axis
  const maxTrend = Math.max(...leveranstrend.map(d => Math.max(d.m3In, d.m3Ut, d.m3HyvlatILager)), 1);

  const utkastSpecs = recentSpecs.filter(s => s.status === "utkast").length;
  const bekraftadeSpecs = recentSpecs.filter(s => s.status === "bekraftad").length;

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight">Översikt</h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground">
          Sammanfattning av lagerstatus och nyckeltal för virkeshanteringen.
        </p>
      </div>

      <DashboardAlerts />

      {larmTriggered.length > 0 && (
        <div className="rounded-lg border-2 border-destructive/50 bg-destructive/5 p-4 space-y-2">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 font-semibold text-destructive">
              <BellRing className="h-5 w-5" />
              {larmTriggered.length} larm aktiv{larmTriggered.length > 1 ? "a" : "t"}
            </div>

          </div>
          {larmTriggered.map(t => (

              <span className="font-medium">{t.namn}</span>
              {" — "}

                {t.currentM3.toFixed(2)} m³ i lager (gräns: {t.minM3} m³)

              : "–"

          value={`${statistik.totalLpm.toFixed(0)} m`}
          sub="Total längd i lager"
          icon={Ruler}
        />
        <KpiCard
          title="Hyvlat i lager"

          icon={Package}

          title="Sågat in (30 dagar)"
          value={`${(statistik as { m3In30dagar?: number }).m3In30dagar?.toFixed(1) ?? "0"} m³`}

          accentBlue
