          icon={TrendingUp}
          accentGreen
          onClick={() => setFlodeOpen("hyvlat_in")}
        />
        <KpiCard
          title="Levererat ut (30 dagar)"
          value={`${(statistik as { m3Ut30dagar?: number }).m3Ut30dagar?.toFixed(1) ?? "0"} m³`}
          sub={`${(statistik as { posterUt30dagar?: number }).posterUt30dagar ?? 0} poster till kund`}
          icon={TrendingDown}
          accent
          onClick={() => setFlodeOpen("levererat_ut")}
        />
      </div>

      {/* Lagertrenddiagram — 30 dagar */}
      {lagertrend.length > 0 && (() => {
        const first = lagertrend[0].m3;
        const last = lagertrend[lagertrend.length - 1].m3;
        const delta = last - first;
        const deltaSign = delta >= 0 ? "+" : "";
        const pct = first > 0 ? ((delta / first) * 100).toFixed(1) : "0";
        const isUp = delta >= 0;
        const trendColor = isUp ? "hsl(0, 84%, 55%)" : "hsl(142, 71%, 45%)";
        const gradientId = isUp ? "trendRedGrad" : "trendGreenGrad";
        const stopColor = isUp ? "#ef4444" : "#22c55e";
        // Show every 5th label to avoid crowding
        const tickSet = new Set(lagertrend.filter((_, i) => i % 5 === 0 || i === lagertrend.length - 1).map(p => p.label));
        const yMin = Math.floor(Math.min(...lagertrend.map(p => p.m3)) * 0.97);
        const yMax = Math.ceil(Math.max(...lagertrend.map(p => p.m3)) * 1.03);
        return (
          <Card>
            <CardHeader className="pb-2">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                  <CardTitle>Lagernivå senaste 30 dagarna</CardTitle>
                  <CardDescription>Totalt m³ i lager per dag</CardDescription>
                </div>
                <div className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-sm font-semibold ${isUp ? "bg-red-100 text-red-700" : "bg-green-100 text-green-700"}`}>
                  {isUp ? <TrendingUp className="h-4 w-4" /> : <TrendingDown className="h-4 w-4" />}
                  {deltaSign}{delta.toFixed(1)} m³ ({deltaSign}{pct} %)
                </div>
              </div>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent>
              <div className="h-[180px]">
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <AreaChart data={lagertrend} margin={{ top: 4, right: 8, left: -16, bottom: 0 }}>
                    <defs>
                      <linearGradient id={gradientId} x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
                        <stop offset="5%" stopColor={stopColor} stopOpacity={0.25} />
                        <stop offset="95%" stopColor={stopColor} stopOpacity={0.02} />
                      </linearGradient>
                    </defs>
                    <XAxis
                      dataKey="label"
                      stroke="#888888"
                      fontSize={11}
                      tickLine={false}
                      axisLine={false}
                      interval={0}
                      tick={({ x, y, payload }) => tickSet.has(payload.value)
                        ? <text x={x} y={y + 12} textAnchor="middle" fill="#888" fontSize={11}>{payload.value}</text>
                        : <g />}
                    />
                    <YAxis
                      stroke="#888888"
                      fontSize={11}
                      tickLine={false}
                      axisLine={false}
                      domain={[yMin, yMax]}
                      tickFormatter={(v) => `${v}`}
                      width={50}
                    />
                    <Tooltip
                      content={({ active, payload, label }) =>
                        active && payload?.length ? (
                          <div className="rounded-lg border bg-background p-2.5 shadow-sm text-sm">
                            <div className="font-semibold mb-0.5">{label}</div>
                            <div className="text-muted-foreground">
                              Lager: <span className="text-foreground font-medium">{payload[0].value} m³</span>
                            </div>
                          </div>
                        ) : null
                      }
                    />
                    <Area
                      type="monotone"
                      dataKey="m3"
                      stroke={trendColor}
                      strokeWidth={2}
                      fill={`url(#${gradientId})`}
                      dot={false}
                      activeDot={{ r: 4, strokeWidth: 0 }}
                    />
                  </AreaChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        );
      })()}

      {/* Rad 2: Sågat/Hyvlat + Träslag + Sammansättning */}
      <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
        {svh && (
          <Card>
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle>Sågat vs Hyvlat</CardTitle>
              <CardDescription>Volymandel av aktivt lager</CardDescription>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent className="space-y-4">
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <div className="flex justify-between text-sm">
                  <span className="font-medium">Sågat</span>
                  <span className="text-muted-foreground">
                    {svh.sagat.andel}% · {svh.sagat.m3.toFixed(1)} m³
                  </span>
                </div>
                <ProgressBar andel={svh.sagat.andel} color="hsl(217, 91%, 60%)" />
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1">
                <div className="flex justify-between text-sm">
                  <span className="font-medium">Hyvlat/Annat</span>
                  <span className="text-muted-foreground">
                    {svh.hyvlat.andel}% · {svh.hyvlat.m3.toFixed(1)} m³
                  </span>
                </div>
                <ProgressBar andel={svh.hyvlat.andel} color="hsl(280, 65%, 60%)" />
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        )}

        <Card>
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle>Träslag</CardTitle>
            <CardDescription>Volymandel per träslag i lager</CardDescription>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="space-y-4">
            {tradslag.map((t) => (
              <div key={t.traslag} className="space-y-1">
                <div className="flex justify-between text-sm">
                  <span className="font-medium">{t.traslag}</span>
                  <span className="text-muted-foreground">
                    {t.andel}% · {t.totalM3.toFixed(1)} m³
                  </span>
                </div>
                <ProgressBar
                  andel={t.andel}
                  color={TRASLAG_COLORS[t.traslag] ?? "hsl(220,14%,60%)"}
                />
              </div>
            ))}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle>Sammansättning</CardTitle>
            <CardDescription>Lagerfördelning i korthet</CardDescription>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <div className="space-y-3 text-sm">
              {tradslag.map((t) => (
                <div key={t.traslag} className="flex items-center justify-between border-b pb-2 last:border-0 last:pb-0">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <div
                      className="w-3 h-3 rounded-full"
                      style={{ backgroundColor: TRASLAG_COLORS[t.traslag] ?? "hsl(220,14%,60%)" }}
                    />
                    <span>{t.traslag}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right">
                    <div className="font-semibold">{t.andel} %</div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{t.antal} poster</div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
              {svh && (
                <>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b pb-2 pt-1">
                    <span className="text-muted-foreground">varav sågat</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold">{svh.sagat.andel} %</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span className="text-muted-foreground">varav hyvlat</span>
                    <span className="font-semibold">{svh.hyvlat.andel} %</span>
                  </div>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {/* Leveranstrend + Senaste specifikationer */}
      <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
        <Card>
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle>Flöde och lagernivå per månad</CardTitle>
            <CardDescription>m³ sågat in · levererat ut · hyvlat i lager vid månadsslutet</CardDescription>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground flex-wrap">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="inline-block w-3 h-3 rounded-sm" style={{ background: "hsl(217, 91%, 60%)" }} />
                Sågat in
              </span>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="inline-block w-3 h-3 rounded-sm" style={{ background: "hsl(142, 71%, 45%)" }} />
                Levererat ut
              </span>
              <span className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                <span className="inline-block w-8 h-0.5 rounded" style={{ background: "hsl(38, 92%, 50%)" }} />
                Hyvlat i lager
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="h-[230px]">
              {leveranstrend.length > 0 ? (
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <ComposedChart data={leveranstrend} barCategoryGap="30%" barGap={2} margin={{ top: 4, right: 8, left: -20, bottom: 0 }}>
                    <XAxis
                      dataKey="label"
                      stroke="#888888"
                      fontSize={11}
                      tickLine={false}
                      axisLine={false}
                    />
                    <YAxis
                      stroke="#888888"
                      fontSize={11}
                      tickLine={false}
                      axisLine={false}
                      tickFormatter={(v) => `${v}`}
                      domain={[0, Math.ceil(maxTrend * 1.15)]}
                    />
                    <Tooltip content={<TrendTooltip />} />
                    <Bar dataKey="m3In" name="Sågat in (råvara)" fill="hsl(217, 91%, 60%)" radius={[3, 3, 0, 0]} />
                    <Bar dataKey="m3Ut" name="Levererat ut (till kund)" fill="hsl(142, 71%, 45%)" radius={[3, 3, 0, 0]} />
                    <Line
                      dataKey="m3HyvlatILager"
                      name="Hyvlat i lager (månadsslutet)"
                      type="monotone"
                      stroke="hsl(38, 92%, 50%)"
                      strokeWidth={2}
                      dot={{ r: 3, fill: "hsl(38, 92%, 50%)" }}
                      activeDot={{ r: 5 }}
                    />
                  </ComposedChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              ) : (
                <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground text-sm">
                  Ingen data tillgänglig
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0">
            <div>
              <CardTitle>Specifikationer</CardTitle>
              <CardDescription>Senaste dokument och status</CardDescription>
            </div>
            <Link href="/spec" className="text-xs text-primary hover:underline flex items-center gap-1">
              <FileText className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              Visa alla
            </Link>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <div className="flex gap-4 mb-4 text-sm">
              <div className="text-center">
                <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-amber-600">{utkastSpecs}</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">utkast</div>
              </div>
              <div className="text-center">
                <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-green-600">{bekraftadeSpecs}</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">bekräftade</div>
              </div>
              <div className="text-center">
                <div className="text-2xl font-bold">{recentSpecs.length}</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">totalt</div>
              </div>
            </div>
            {senasteFemSpecs.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Inga specifikationer skapade ännu.</p>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-2">
                {senasteFemSpecs.map(s => (
                  <Link key={s.id} href={`/spec/${s.id}`} className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-md hover:bg-muted/50 px-2 py-1.5 transition-colors group">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-0">
                      <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground font-mono w-12 shrink-0">#{s.id}</span>
                      <span className="text-sm font-medium truncate">{s.kund || "—"}</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 shrink-0">
                      <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{s.paketCount} pkt</span>
                      <Badge
                        variant="outline"
                        className={
                          s.status === "bekraftad"
                            ? "bg-green-100 text-green-800 border-green-200 text-xs"
                            : "text-xs"
                        }
                      >
                        {s.status === "bekraftad" ? "Bekräftad" : "Utkast"}
                      </Badge>
                    </div>
                  </Link>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {/* Top 10 sågade dimensioner */}
      {topDim.length > 0 && (
        <Card>
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle>Top 10 sågade dimensioner</CardTitle>
            <CardDescription>
              Största volymerna bland sågade varor — andel av total sågad volym ({(svh?.sagat.m3 ?? 0).toFixed(1)} m³)
            </CardDescription>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {topDim.map((d, i) => (
                <div key={d.dimension} className="space-y-1">
                  <div className="flex justify-between text-sm">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                      <span className="text-muted-foreground w-5 text-right">{i + 1}.</span>
                      <span className="font-medium font-mono">{d.dimension} mm</span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-muted-foreground">
                      <span>{d.antal} poster</span>
                      <span className="font-semibold text-foreground w-20 text-right">
                        {d.totalM3.toFixed(1)} m³
                      </span>
                      <span className="w-12 text-right font-semibold text-foreground">
                        {d.andel} %
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <ProgressBar andel={d.andel} color="hsl(217, 91%, 60%)" />
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      {/* Leverantörer */}
      {(() => {
        const levs = (statistik as { leverantorFordelning?: { leverantor: string; antal: number; totalM3: number }[] }).leverantorFordelning ?? [];
        if (levs.length === 0) return null;
        const maxM3 = levs[0].totalM3;
        return (
          <Card>
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle>Topp leverantörer</CardTitle>
              <CardDescription>Volym per leverantör — aktivt i lager</CardDescription>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent>
              <div className="space-y-3">
                {levs.map((lev, i) => (
                  <div key={lev.leverantor} className="space-y-1">
                    <div className="flex justify-between text-sm">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="text-muted-foreground w-5 text-right">{i + 1}.</span>
                        <span className="font-medium">{lev.leverantor}</span>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 text-muted-foreground">
                        <span>{lev.antal} poster</span>
                        <span className="font-semibold text-foreground w-20 text-right">{lev.totalM3.toFixed(1)} m³</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <ProgressBar andel={maxM3 > 0 ? (lev.totalM3 / maxM3) * 100 : 0} color="hsl(38, 92%, 50%)" />
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        );
      })()}

      {/* Diagram: Profil + Plats */}
      <div className="grid gap-4 md:grid-cols-2">
        <Card>
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle>Volym per profil</CardTitle>
            <CardDescription>Fördelning av m³ per produkttyp (sågat, regel, stolpe m.m.)</CardDescription>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="h-[300px]">
            {statistik.varuslagFordelning.length > 0 ? (
              <ChartContainer config={{}}>
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <PieChart>
                    <Pie
                      data={statistik.varuslagFordelning}
                      cx="50%"
                      cy="50%"
                      innerRadius={60}
                      outerRadius={80}
                      paddingAngle={2}
                      dataKey="totalM3"
                      nameKey="varuslag"
                    >
                      {statistik.varuslagFordelning.map((_, index) => (
                        <Cell key={`cell-${index}`} fill={varuslagColors[index % varuslagColors.length]} />
                      ))}
                    </Pie>
                    <ChartTooltip 
                      content={<ChartTooltipContent 
                        formatter={(value) => `${Number(value).toFixed(2)} m³`}
                      />} 
                    />
                  </PieChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </ChartContainer>
            ) : (
              <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground text-sm">
                Ingen data tillgänglig
              </div>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>

        <Card>
          <CardHeader>
            <CardTitle>Poster per lagerplats</CardTitle>
            <CardDescription>Antal unika poster fördelat på lagerplatser</CardDescription>
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent className="h-[300px]">
            {statistik.platsFordelning.length > 0 ? (
              <ChartContainer config={{}}>
                <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                  <BarChart data={statistik.platsFordelning}>
                    <XAxis 
                      dataKey="plats" 
                      stroke="#888888" 
                      fontSize={12} 
                      tickLine={false} 
                      axisLine={false}
                      tickFormatter={(value) => value || 'Okänd'}
                    />
                    <YAxis 
                      stroke="#888888" 
                      fontSize={12} 
                      tickLine={false} 
                      axisLine={false} 
                      tickFormatter={(value) => `${value}`} 
                    />
                    <ChartTooltip content={<ChartTooltipContent />} />
                    <Bar 
                      dataKey="antal" 
                      fill="hsl(var(--primary))" 
                      radius={[4, 4, 0, 0]} 
                      name="Antal poster"
                    />
                  </BarChart>
                </ResponsiveContainer>
              </ChartContainer>
            ) : (
              <div className="h-full flex items-center justify-center text-muted-foreground text-sm">
                Ingen data tillgänglig
              </div>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {/* Flödes-lista Sheet */}
      <Sheet open={!!flodeOpen} onOpenChange={(o) => { if (!o) setFlodeOpen(null); }}>
        <SheetContent side="right" className="w-full sm:max-w-3xl overflow-y-auto">
          {flodeOpen && (() => {
            const meta = FLODE_META[flodeOpen];
            const totM3 = flodePaket.reduce((s: number, p: any) => s + (p.m3 ?? 0), 0);
            return (
              <>
                <SheetHeader className="mb-4">
                  <SheetTitle className={`text-lg ${meta.color}`}>{meta.title}</SheetTitle>
                  <SheetDescription>
                    {flodeLoading
                      ? "Hämtar paket…"
                      : `${flodePaket.length} paket · Totalt ${totM3.toFixed(2)} m³`}
                  </SheetDescription>
                </SheetHeader>
                {flodeLoading ? (
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    {[...Array(8)].map((_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-10 w-full" />)}
                  </div>
                ) : flodePaket.length === 0 ? (
                  <div className="text-muted-foreground text-sm py-8 text-center">Inga paket hittades</div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                    <table className="w-full text-sm border-collapse">
                      <thead>
                        <tr className="border-b text-left text-muted-foreground text-xs uppercase tracking-wider">
                          <th className="py-2 pr-3 font-medium">Pkt Nr</th>
                          <th className="py-2 pr-3 font-medium">{meta.datumLabel}</th>
                          <th className="py-2 pr-3 font-medium">Träslag</th>
                          <th className="py-2 pr-3 font-medium">Profil</th>
                          <th className="py-2 pr-3 font-medium">Dim (nom)</th>
                          <th className="py-2 pr-3 font-medium">Kvalitet</th>
                          <th className="py-2 pr-3 text-right font-medium">Antal</th>
                          <th className="py-2 pr-3 text-right font-medium">m³</th>
                          <th className="py-2 font-medium">Plats</th>
                          <th className="py-2"></th>
                        </tr>
                      </thead>
                      <tbody className="divide-y">
                        {flodePaket.map((p: any) => (
                          <tr key={p.id} className="hover:bg-muted/40 transition-colors">
                            <td className="py-2 pr-3 font-mono text-xs">{p.pktNr || "–"}</td>
                            <td className="py-2 pr-3 text-muted-foreground">
                              {(flodeOpen === "levererat_ut" ? p.datumUt : p.datumIn) || "–"}
                            </td>
                            <td className="py-2 pr-3">{p.traslag || "–"}</td>
                            <td className="py-2 pr-3">{p.profil || "–"}</td>
                            <td className="py-2 pr-3 text-xs">
                              {p.nomTjocklek && p.nomBredd ? `${p.nomTjocklek}×${p.nomBredd}` : "–"}
                            </td>
                            <td className="py-2 pr-3 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{p.kvalitet || "–"}</td>
                            <td className="py-2 pr-3 text-right tabular-nums">{p.antal ?? "–"}</td>
                            <td className="py-2 pr-3 text-right tabular-nums font-medium">
                              {p.m3 != null ? p.m3.toFixed(3) : "–"}
                            </td>
                            <td className="py-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">{p.plats || "–"}</td>
                            <td className="py-2 pl-2">
                              <Link href={`/lager/${p.id}`} onClick={() => setFlodeOpen(null)}>
                                <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-muted-foreground hover:text-primary" />
                              </Link>
                            </td>
                          </tr>
                        ))}
                      </tbody>
                      <tfoot>
                        <tr className="border-t font-semibold">
                          <td colSpan={7} className="py-2 pr-3 text-right text-xs text-muted-foreground">Totalt</td>
                          <td className="py-2 pr-3 text-right tabular-nums">{totM3.toFixed(3)}</td>
                          <td colSpan={2}></td>
                        </tr>
                      </tfoot>
                    </table>
                  </div>
                )}
              </>
            );
          })()}
        </SheetContent>
      </Sheet>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState, useMemo } from "react";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { useLocation } from "wouter";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Sheet, SheetContent, SheetHeader, SheetTitle, SheetDescription } from "@/components/ui/sheet";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import {
  BarChart,
  Bar,
  XAxis,
  YAxis,
  CartesianGrid,
  Tooltip,
  ResponsiveContainer,
  Cell,
} from "recharts";
import { TrendingUp, Users, Package, Search, X, ChevronDown, ChevronRight, ShieldCheck, ArrowRight, ExternalLink } from "lucide-react";

type ProduktKundSpec = {
  specId: number;
  specNr: number | null;
  bekraftadAt: string | null;
  antalPaket: number;
  totalM3: number;
};

type ProduktKund = {
  kund: string | null;
  antalPaket: number;
  totalM3: number;
  specs: ProduktKundSpec[];
};

type Produkt = {
  varuslag: string | null;
  profil: string | null;
  traslag: string | null;
  nomTjocklek: string | null;
  nomBredd: string | null;
  actTjocklek: string | null;
  actBredd: string | null;
  langdDm: string | null;
  antalOrdrar: number;
  antalPaket: number;
  totalM3: number;
  andelProcent: number;
  kunder: ProduktKund[];
};

type Kund = {
  kund: string | null;
  antalOrdrar: number;
  antalPaket: number;
  totalM3: number;
};

type KundSpec = {
  specId: number;
  specNr: number | null;
  bekraftadAt: string | null;
  trailerNr: string | null;
  orderNr: string | null;
  antalPaket: number;
  m3: number;
};

type KundSpecarResponse = {
  kund: string;
  specs: KundSpec[];
};

type CertVolym = {
  cert: string;
  totalM3: number;
  antalOrdrar: number;
};

const CERT_LABELS: Record<string, { label: string; nr: string }> = {
  pefc: { label: "PEFC",                 nr: "PBN-PEFC-COC-078374" },
  fsc:  { label: "FSC Controlled Wood",  nr: "PBN-CW-078374" },
  kd:   { label: "KD/ISPM 15",           nr: "SE-H7883" },
};

type StatistikData = {
  manad: number;
  grandTotalM3: number;
  produkter: Produkt[];
  kunder: Kund[];
  certifikatVolym: CertVolym[];
};

const PERIODS = [
  { label: "3 mån", value: 3 },
  { label: "6 mån", value: 6 },
  { label: "12 mån", value: 12 },
  { label: "24 mån", value: 24 },
  { label: "Allt", value: 0 },
];

const BAR_COLORS = [
  "#2d5a27", "#3a7034", "#4a8c42", "#5aa050", "#6ab45e",
  "#7ac86c", "#4e7a49", "#3d6138", "#2c4827", "#5e9456",
];

function dimStr(t: string | null, b: string | null): string {
  const tn = t ? parseFloat(t) : null;
  const bn = b ? parseFloat(b) : null;
  if (!tn && !bn) return "-";
  return `${tn ?? "-"}×${bn ?? "-"}`;
}

function langdStr(dm: string | null): string {
  if (!dm) return "-";
  const n = parseFloat(dm);
  return isNaN(n) ? "-" : `${(n / 10).toFixed(2).replace(".", ",")} m`;
}

function produktLabel(p: Produkt): string {
  const dim = dimStr(p.nomTjocklek, p.nomBredd);
  const langd = langdStr(p.langdDm);
  const profil = p.profil ?? p.varuslag ?? "Okänd";
  return `${profil} ${dim} ${langd}`;
}

export default function ForsaljningsstatistikPage() {
  const [manad, setManad] = useState(12);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [expandedRow, setExpandedRow] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [expandedKund, setExpandedKund] = useState<string | null>(null); // "produktIdx|kundNamn"
  const [selectedKund, setSelectedKund] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();

  const { data, isLoading } = useQuery<StatistikData>({
    queryKey: ["/api/lager/forsaljningsstatistik", manad],
    queryFn: () =>
      customFetch<StatistikData>(
        `/api/lager/forsaljningsstatistik?manad=${manad}`
      ),
    staleTime: 60_000,
  });

  const { data: kundSpecar, isLoading: kundSpecarLoading } = useQuery<KundSpecarResponse>({
    queryKey: ["/api/rapport/kund-specar", selectedKund, manad],
    queryFn: () =>
      customFetch<KundSpecarResponse>(
        `/api/rapport/kund-specar?kund=${encodeURIComponent(selectedKund!)}&manad=${manad}`
      ),
    enabled: !!selectedKund,
    staleTime: 30_000,
  });

  const filteredProdukter = useMemo(() => {
    const all = data?.produkter ?? [];
    if (!search.trim()) return all.slice(0, 20);
    const normalize = (s: string) => s.toLowerCase().replace(/×/g, "x").replace(/[,\s]+/g, " ").trim();
    const tokens = normalize(search).split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    return all.filter(p => {
      const haystack = [
        p.varuslag ?? "",
        p.profil ?? "",
        p.traslag ?? "",
        dimStr(p.nomTjocklek, p.nomBredd),
        dimStr(p.actTjocklek, p.actBredd),
        langdStr(p.langdDm),
      ].map(normalize).join(" ");
      return tokens.every(tok => haystack.includes(tok));
    });
  }, [data?.produkter, search]);

  const chartData = (data?.produkter ?? []).slice(0, 10).map(p => ({
    name: produktLabel(p),
    m3: p.totalM3,
    andel: p.andelProcent,
  }));

  function toggleRow(i: number) {
    setExpandedRow(prev => prev === i ? null : i);
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 pb-10">
      <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold tracking-tight">Försäljningsstatistik</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">
            Baserat på bekräftade leveransspecifikationer
          </p>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1 bg-muted rounded-lg p-1">
          {PERIODS.map(p => (
            <button
              key={p.value}
              onClick={() => setManad(p.value)}
              className={`px-3 py-1.5 text-sm font-medium rounded-md transition-colors ${
                manad === p.value
                  ? "bg-background shadow text-foreground"
                  : "text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
              }`}
            >
              {p.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Summary cards */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-3">
        <Card>
          <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0 pb-2">
            <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium">Total m³ sålt</CardTitle>
            <TrendingUp className="h-4 w-4 text-[#2d5a27]" />
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            {isLoading ? (
              <Skeleton className="h-8 w-24" />
            ) : (
              <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-[#2d5a27]">
                {(data?.grandTotalM3 ?? 0).toFixed(1)} m³
              </div>
            )}
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">
              {manad === 0 ? "Totalt historiskt" : `Senaste ${manad} månader`}
            </p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
        <Card>
          <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0 pb-2">
            <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium">Antal produkter</CardTitle>
            <Package className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            {isLoading ? (
              <Skeleton className="h-8 w-16" />
            ) : (
              <div className="text-2xl font-bold">{data?.produkter.length ?? 0}</div>
            )}
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">Unika dim/profil-kombinationer</p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
        <Card className="col-span-2 sm:col-span-1">
          <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0 pb-2">
            <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium">Antal kunder</CardTitle>
            <Users className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            {isLoading ? (
              <Skeleton className="h-8 w-16" />
            ) : (
              <div className="text-2xl font-bold">{data?.kunder.length ?? 0}</div>
            )}
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">Med minst ett bekräftat köp</p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      </div>

      {/* Certifikat-volymer */}
      {(data?.certifikatVolym ?? []).length > 0 && (
        <Card>
          <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between space-y-0 pb-3">
            <CardTitle className="text-sm font-medium">Certifierad volym</CardTitle>
            <ShieldCheck className="h-4 w-4 text-[#2d5a27]" />
          </CardHeader>
          <CardContent>
            <div className="space-y-3">
              {(data?.certifikatVolym ?? []).map(cv => {
                const meta = CERT_LABELS[cv.cert] ?? { label: cv.cert.toUpperCase(), nr: "" };
                const pct = data && data.grandTotalM3 > 0
                  ? Math.round((cv.totalM3 / data.grandTotalM3) * 1000) / 10
                  : 0;
                return (
                  <div key={cv.cert}>
                    <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between mb-1">
                      <div>
                        <span className="font-semibold text-sm">{meta.label}</span>
                        {meta.nr && <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground ml-2">{meta.nr}</span>}
                      </div>
                      <div className="text-right">
                        <span className="font-bold text-[#2d5a27]">{cv.totalM3.toFixed(1)} m³</span>
                        <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground ml-2">({pct} % av total)</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="h-1.5 bg-muted rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                      <div
                        className="h-full rounded-full bg-[#2d5a27]"
                        style={{ width: `${pct}%` }}
                      />
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">{cv.antalOrdrar} leveranser</p>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-4 border-t pt-3">
              Baserat på bekräftade leveranser med certifikat markerade på specan.
              En leverans kan räknas under flera certifikat.
            </p>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      {/* Bar chart */}
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="text-base">Topp 10 produkter — m³ sålt</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          {isLoading ? (
            <Skeleton className="h-64 w-full" />
          ) : chartData.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center py-12">
              Inga bekräftade leveranser under perioden
            </p>
          ) : (
            <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height={280}>
              <BarChart
                data={chartData}
                layout="vertical"
                margin={{ top: 0, right: 60, left: 8, bottom: 0 }}
              >
                <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" horizontal={false} />
                <XAxis
                  type="number"
                  tickFormatter={v => `${v} m³`}
                  tick={{ fontSize: 11 }}
                />
                <YAxis
                  type="category"
                  dataKey="name"
                  width={160}
                  tick={{ fontSize: 10 }}
                  tickLine={false}
                />
                <Tooltip
                  formatter={(v: number, _name: string, entry) => [
                    `${v.toFixed(3)} m³ (${entry.payload.andel} %)`,
                    "Volym",
                  ]}
                  contentStyle={{ fontSize: 12 }}
                />
                <Bar dataKey="m3" radius={[0, 3, 3, 0]}>
                  {chartData.map((_entry, i) => (
                    <Cell key={i} fill={BAR_COLORS[i % BAR_COLORS.length]} />
                  ))}
                </Bar>
              </BarChart>
            </ResponsiveContainer>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* Products table */}
      <Card>
        <CardHeader className="flex flex-row items-center justify-between gap-3 flex-wrap">
          <CardTitle className="text-base">
            Produkter
            {search.trim() && filteredProdukter.length > 0 && (
              <span className="ml-2 text-sm font-normal text-muted-foreground">
                {filteredProdukter.length} träff{filteredProdukter.length !== 1 ? "ar" : ""}
              </span>
            )}
          </CardTitle>
          <div className="relative w-full sm:w-64">
            <Search className="absolute left-2.5 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 h-3.5 w-3.5 text-muted-foreground" />
            <Input
              className="pl-8 pr-8 h-8 text-sm"
              placeholder="Sök dim, profil, längd…"
              value={search}
              onChange={e => { setSearch(e.target.value); setExpandedRow(null); }}
            />
            {search && (
              <button
                onClick={() => setSearch("")}
                className="absolute right-2 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
              >
                <X className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="p-0">
          {isLoading ? (
            <div className="p-6 space-y-2">
              {[...Array(5)].map((_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-8 w-full" />)}
            </div>
          ) : (data?.produkter.length ?? 0) === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center py-12">
              Inga bekräftade leveranser under perioden
            </p>
          ) : filteredProdukter.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center py-10">
              Ingen produkt matchar "<span className="font-medium">{search}</span>"
            </p>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-sm">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="border-b bg-muted/30">
                    <th className="py-2 pl-4 pr-2 w-6" />
                    <th className="py-2 pr-3 text-left font-medium text-muted-foreground w-8">#</th>
                    <th className="py-2 pr-3 text-left font-medium text-muted-foreground">Produkt</th>
                    <th className="py-2 pr-3 text-right font-medium text-muted-foreground">Längd</th>
                    <th className="py-2 pr-3 text-right font-medium text-muted-foreground">Paket</th>
                    <th className="py-2 pr-3 text-right font-medium text-muted-foreground">Ordrar</th>
                    <th className="py-2 pr-3 text-right font-medium text-muted-foreground">m³</th>
                    <th className="py-2 pr-4 text-right font-medium text-muted-foreground">Andel</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {filteredProdukter.map((p, i) => {
                    const isExpanded = expandedRow === i;
                    const hasKunder = p.kunder && p.kunder.length > 0;
                    return (
                      <>
                        <tr
                          key={i}
                          className={`border-b border-border/40 transition-colors ${hasKunder ? "cursor-pointer hover:bg-muted/30" : "hover:bg-muted/20"} ${isExpanded ? "bg-muted/20" : ""}`}
                          onClick={() => hasKunder && toggleRow(i)}
                        >
                          <td className="py-2.5 pl-4 pr-2 text-muted-foreground">
                            {hasKunder && (
                              isExpanded
                                ? <ChevronDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                                : <ChevronRight className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                            )}
                          </td>
                          <td className="py-2.5 pr-3 text-muted-foreground tabular-nums">{i + 1}</td>
                          <td className="py-2.5 pr-3">
                            <div className="font-medium">{[p.profil, p.varuslag].filter(Boolean).join(" · ") || "Okänd"}</div>
                            <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{dimStr(p.nomTjocklek, p.nomBredd)}{p.traslag ? ` · ${p.traslag}` : ""}</div>
                          </td>
                          <td className="py-2.5 pr-3 text-right tabular-nums whitespace-nowrap">{langdStr(p.langdDm)}</td>
                          <td className="py-2.5 pr-3 text-right tabular-nums">{p.antalPaket}</td>
                          <td className="py-2.5 pr-3 text-right tabular-nums">{p.antalOrdrar}</td>
                          <td className="py-2.5 pr-3 text-right tabular-nums font-medium">{p.totalM3.toFixed(3)}</td>
                          <td className="py-2.5 pr-4">
                            <div className="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
                              <div className="w-16 h-1.5 bg-muted rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                                <div
                                  className="h-full bg-[#2d5a27] rounded-full"
                                  style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, p.andelProcent)}%` }}
                                />
                              </div>
                              <span className="tabular-nums text-xs w-10 text-right">{p.andelProcent.toFixed(1)} %</span>
                            </div>
                          </td>
                        </tr>
                        {isExpanded && hasKunder && (
                          <tr key={`${i}-kunder`} className="border-b border-border/40 bg-muted/10">
                            <td colSpan={8} className="py-2 pl-10 pr-4">
                              <table className="w-full text-xs">
                                <thead>
                                  <tr className="text-muted-foreground">
                                    <th className="text-left font-medium pb-1 pr-2 w-4" />
                                    <th className="text-left font-medium pb-1 pr-4">Kund</th>
                                    <th className="text-right font-medium pb-1 pr-4">Paket</th>
                                    <th className="text-right font-medium pb-1">m³</th>
                                  </tr>
                                </thead>
                                <tbody>
                                  {p.kunder.map((k, ki) => {
                                    const kundKey = `${i}|${k.kund ?? ""}`;
                                    const isKundExpanded = expandedKund === kundKey;
                                    const hasSpecs = k.specs && k.specs.length > 0;
                                    return (
                                      <>
                                        <tr
                                          key={ki}
                                          className={`border-t border-border/20 ${hasSpecs ? "cursor-pointer hover:bg-muted/30" : ""} ${isKundExpanded ? "bg-muted/20" : ""}`}
                                          onClick={() => hasSpecs && setExpandedKund(isKundExpanded ? null : kundKey)}
                                        >
                                          <td className="py-1 pr-2 text-muted-foreground">
                                            {hasSpecs && (isKundExpanded
                                              ? <ChevronDown className="h-3 w-3" />
                                              : <ChevronRight className="h-3 w-3" />
                                            )}
                                          </td>
                                          <td className="py-1 pr-4 font-medium">{k.kund || <span className="text-muted-foreground italic">Okänd kund</span>}</td>
                                          <td className="py-1 pr-4 text-right tabular-nums">{k.antalPaket}</td>
                                          <td className="py-1 text-right tabular-nums">{k.totalM3.toFixed(3)}</td>
                                        </tr>
                                        {isKundExpanded && hasSpecs && k.specs.map((s, si) => (
                                          <tr
                                            key={`${ki}-spec-${si}`}
                                            className="border-t border-border/10 bg-background/60 cursor-pointer hover:bg-muted/20"
                                            onClick={() => navigate(`/spec/${s.specId}`)}
                                          >
                                            <td className="py-1 pl-4 pr-2" />
                                            <td className="py-1 pr-4 text-muted-foreground">
                                              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                                <ExternalLink className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0" />
                                                {s.specNr ? `#${s.specNr}` : `Spec ${s.specId}`}
                                                {s.bekraftadAt && (
                                                  <span className="text-muted-foreground/60">
                                                    · {new Date(s.bekraftadAt).toLocaleDateString("sv-SE", { day: "numeric", month: "short" })}
                                                  </span>
                                                )}
                                              </span>
                                            </td>
                                            <td className="py-1 pr-4 text-right tabular-nums text-muted-foreground">{s.antalPaket}</td>
                                            <td className="py-1 text-right tabular-nums text-muted-foreground">{s.totalM3.toFixed(3)}</td>
                                          </tr>
                                        ))}
                                      </>
                                    );
                                  })}
                                </tbody>
                              </table>
                            </td>
                          </tr>
                        )}
                      </>
                    );
                  })}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* Customer breakdown */}
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="text-base">Kunder — totalt</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="p-0">
          {isLoading ? (
            <div className="p-6 space-y-2">
              {[...Array(4)].map((_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-8 w-full" />)}
            </div>
          ) : (data?.kunder.length ?? 0) === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center py-12">Inga kunder</p>
          ) : (
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-sm">
                <thead>
                  <tr className="border-b bg-muted/30">
                    <th className="py-2 pl-4 pr-3 text-left font-medium text-muted-foreground">Kund</th>
                    <th className="py-2 pr-3 text-right font-medium text-muted-foreground">Ordrar</th>
                    <th className="py-2 pr-3 text-right font-medium text-muted-foreground">Paket</th>
                    <th className="py-2 pr-4 text-right font-medium text-muted-foreground">m³</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {data?.kunder.map((k, i) => (
                    <tr
                      key={i}
                      className="border-b border-border/40 hover:bg-muted/30 cursor-pointer group transition-colors"
                      onClick={() => setSelectedKund(k.kund || "Okänd")}
                    >
                      <td className="py-2.5 pl-4 pr-3 font-medium">
                        <span className="group-hover:text-[#2d5a27] transition-colors">
                          {k.kund || <span className="text-muted-foreground italic">Okänd kund</span>}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-2.5 pr-3 text-right tabular-nums text-muted-foreground">{k.antalOrdrar}</td>
                      <td className="py-2.5 pr-3 text-right tabular-nums text-muted-foreground">{k.antalPaket}</td>
                      <td className="py-2.5 pr-4 text-right tabular-nums font-medium">
                        <span className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1.5">
                          {k.totalM3.toFixed(3)}
                          <ArrowRight className="h-3 w-3 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity" />
                        </span>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* Kund-spec Sheet */}
      <Sheet open={!!selectedKund} onOpenChange={open => { if (!open) setSelectedKund(null); }}>
        <SheetContent className="w-full sm:max-w-lg flex flex-col gap-0 p-0">
          <SheetHeader className="px-6 pt-6 pb-4 border-b">
            <SheetTitle className="text-lg font-semibold leading-tight">
              {selectedKund}
            </SheetTitle>
            <SheetDescription className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              {manad === 0 ? "Alla bekräftade leveranser" : `Bekräftade leveranser — senaste ${manad} månader`}
            </SheetDescription>
          </SheetHeader>

          <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
            {kundSpecarLoading ? (
              <div className="p-6 space-y-3">
                {[...Array(4)].map((_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-12 w-full" />)}
              </div>
            ) : !kundSpecar || kundSpecar.specs.length === 0 ? (
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center py-16">Inga specar hittades</p>
            ) : (
              <>
                {/* Summering */}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-px bg-border mx-6 mt-5 mb-4 rounded-lg overflow-hidden">
                  <div className="bg-background text-center py-3 px-2">
                    <div className="text-xl font-bold text-[#2d5a27]">{kundSpecar.specs.length}</div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">Specar</div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="bg-background text-center py-3 px-2">
                    <div className="text-xl font-bold">{kundSpecar.specs.reduce((s, x) => s + x.antalPaket, 0)}</div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">Paket</div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="bg-background text-center py-3 px-2">
                    <div className="text-xl font-bold">{kundSpecar.specs.reduce((s, x) => s + x.m3, 0).toFixed(1)}</div>
                    <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">m³</div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {/* Spec-lista */}
                <div className="divide-y divide-border/50 mx-4">
                  {kundSpecar.specs.map(s => {
                    const datum = s.bekraftadAt
                      ? new Date(s.bekraftadAt).toLocaleDateString("sv-SE", { day: "numeric", month: "short", year: "numeric" })
                      : "—";
                    return (
                      <button
                        key={s.specId}
                        onClick={() => { setSelectedKund(null); navigate(`/spec/${s.specId}`); }}
                        className="w-full text-left py-3.5 px-2 rounded-md hover:bg-muted/50 transition-colors group flex items-start justify-between gap-3"
                      >
                        <div className="min-w-0">
                          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                            {s.specNr && (
                              <Badge variant="outline" className="text-xs font-mono shrink-0">
                                #{s.specNr}
                              </Badge>
                            )}
                            <span className="text-sm font-medium">{datum}</span>
                            {s.trailerNr && (
                              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Trailer {s.trailerNr}</span>
                            )}
                            {s.orderNr && (
                              <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Order {s.orderNr}</span>
                            )}
                          </div>
                          <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-1">
                            {s.antalPaket} paket · {s.m3.toFixed(3)} m³
                          </div>
                        </div>
                        <ExternalLink className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity shrink-0 mt-0.5" />
                      </button>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
                <div className="h-6" />
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </SheetContent>
      </Sheet>
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/historik/HistorikPage.tsx ================

import { useState } from "react";
import { format } from "date-fns";
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Link } from "wouter";
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { History, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, ExternalLink } from "lucide-react";

interface HistorikPost {
  id: number;
  lagerId: number;
  action: string;
  andringar: Record<string, unknown> | null;
  changedBy: string | null;
  skapadAt: string;
  pktNr: string | null;
  varuslag: string | null;
  profil: string | null;
}

interface HistorikResponse {
  poster: HistorikPost[];
  total: number;
  page: number;
  limit: number;
}

const actionLabels: Record<string, { label: string; variant: "default" | "secondary" | "destructive" | "outline" }> = {
  create: { label: "Skapad", variant: "default" },
  update: { label: "Uppdaterad", variant: "secondary" },
  delete: { label: "Raderad", variant: "destructive" },
  batch_update: { label: "Batch-uppdatering", variant: "secondary" },
  avrapportera: { label: "Avrapporterad", variant: "outline" },
  ateroppen: { label: "Återöppnad", variant: "secondary" },
};

interface ClerkUserMin {
  id: string;
  email: string | null;
  firstName: string | null;
  lastName: string | null;
}

function resolveUser(id: string | null, users: ClerkUserMin[]): string | null {
  if (!id) return null;
  const u = users.find((u) => u.id === id);
  if (!u) return id;
  const name = [u.firstName, u.lastName].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
  return name || u.email || id;
}

export default function HistorikPage() {
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [from, setFrom] = useState("");
  const [to, setTo] = useState("");
  const [action, setAction] = useState("alla");

  const params = new URLSearchParams({ page: String(page), limit: "50" });
  if (from) params.set("from", from);
  if (to) params.set("to", to);
  if (action && action !== "alla") params.set("action", action);

  const { data, isLoading } = useQuery<HistorikResponse>({
    queryKey: ["/api/lager/historik", page, from, to, action],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<HistorikResponse>(`/api/lager/historik?${params}`),
  });

  const { data: clerkUsers = [] } = useQuery<ClerkUserMin[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/admin/users"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<ClerkUserMin[]>("/api/admin/users"),
    retry: false,
    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
  });

  const totalPages = data ? Math.ceil(data.total / 50) : 1;

  function resetFilters() {
    setFrom("");
    setTo("");
    setAction("alla");
    setPage(1);
  }

  return (
    <div className="p-6 space-y-6 max-w-5xl mx-auto">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight flex items-center gap-3">
          <History className="h-8 w-8 text-primary" />
          Ändringshistorik
        </h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-1">Alla händelser i lagersystemet, sorterade senast först.</p>
      </div>

      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="text-base">Filtrera</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 items-end">
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <label className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider">Från datum</label>
              <Input
                type="date"
                value={from}
                onChange={(e) => { setFrom(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}
                className="w-40"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <label className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider">Till datum</label>
              <Input
                type="date"
                value={to}
                onChange={(e) => { setTo(e.target.value); setPage(1); }}
                className="w-40"
              />
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
              <label className="text-xs font-medium text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wider">Händelsetyp</label>
              <Select value={action} onValueChange={(v) => { setAction(v); setPage(1); }}>
                <SelectTrigger className="w-44">
                  <SelectValue />
                </SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  <SelectItem value="alla">Alla händelser</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="create">Skapad</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="update">Uppdaterad</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="delete">Raderad</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="batch_update">Batch-uppdatering</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="avrapportera">Avrapporterad</SelectItem>
                  <SelectItem value="ateroppen">Återöppnad</SelectItem>
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
            {(from || to || action !== "alla") && (
              <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={resetFilters}>
                Rensa filter
              </Button>
            )}
            {data && (
              <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground ml-auto">
                {data.total} händelser totalt
              </span>
            )}
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Card>
        <CardContent className="p-0">
          {isLoading ? (
            <div className="p-6 space-y-3">
              {Array.from({ length: 10 }).map((_, i) => (
                <Skeleton key={i} className="h-12 w-full" />
              ))}
            </div>
          ) : !data || data.poster.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-12 text-center text-muted-foreground">
              <History className="h-10 w-10 mx-auto mb-3 opacity-30" />
              <p>Inga händelser hittades.</p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="divide-y">
              {data.poster.map((h) => {
                const a = actionLabels[h.action] ?? { label: h.action, variant: "outline" as const };
                const andringarEntries = h.andringar
                  ? Object.entries(h.andringar).filter(([k]) => !["uppdateradAt"].includes(k))
                  : [];
                return (
                  <div key={h.id} className="flex items-start gap-4 px-6 py-3 hover:bg-muted/30 transition-colors">
                    <div className="pt-0.5">
                      <Badge variant={a.variant} className="text-xs whitespace-nowrap">{a.label}</Badge>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                        <span className="font-medium text-sm">
                          {h.pktNr ? `Pkt ${h.pktNr}` : `Post #${h.lagerId}`}
                        </span>
                        {h.varuslag && (
                          <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{h.varuslag}</span>
                        )}
                        {h.profil && (
                          <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{h.profil}</span>
                        )}
                        {h.action !== "delete" && (
                          <Link href={`/lager/${h.lagerId}`}>
                            <ExternalLink className="h-3 w-3 text-muted-foreground hover:text-primary" />
                          </Link>
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mt-0.5">
                        {andringarEntries.length > 0 && (
                          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground truncate">
                            {andringarEntries.map(([k, v]) => `${k}: ${v}`).join(" · ")}
                          </p>
                        )}
                        {h.changedBy && (
                          <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground/70 whitespace-nowrap shrink-0">
                            av {resolveUser(h.changedBy, clerkUsers) ?? h.changedBy}
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground whitespace-nowrap">
                      {format(new Date(h.skapadAt), "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm")}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {totalPages > 1 && (
        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-3">
          <Button
            variant="outline"
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => setPage((p) => Math.max(1, p - 1))}
            disabled={page === 1}
          >
            <ChevronLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
            Föregående
          </Button>
          <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Sida {page} av {totalPages}
          </span>
          <Button
            variant="outline"
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => setPage((p) => Math.min(totalPages, p + 1))}
            disabled={page === totalPages}
          >
            Nästa
            <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4" />
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useLocation } from "wouter";
import { customFetch, ApiError } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Plus, FlaskConical, Trash2, ChevronRight, Search, X } from "lucide-react";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { useUser } from "@clerk/react";
import { AlertDialog, AlertDialogAction, AlertDialogCancel, AlertDialogContent, AlertDialogDescription, AlertDialogFooter, AlertDialogHeader, AlertDialogTitle } from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";

type TreatmentType = "IMPREGNERING" | "GRUNDMALNING";
type Treatment = {
  id: number; typ: string; kund: string | null; utforare?: string | null; behandlingTyp?: TreatmentType | null;
  status: string; skapadAt: string; antalPaket: number; totalM3: number; totalActM3?: number | null;
};
const typeLabel = (type?: TreatmentType | null) => type === "GRUNDMALNING" ? "Grundmålning" : "Impregnering";
const typeBadge = (type?: TreatmentType | null) => <Badge variant="secondary" className="font-normal">{typeLabel(type)}</Badge>;
const statusBadge = (status: string) => status === "bekraftad"
  ? <Badge className="bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 border-blue-200">Hos behandling</Badge>
  : status === "returnerad" ? <Badge className="bg-green-100 text-green-800 border-green-200">Returnerad</Badge> : <Badge variant="outline">Utkast</Badge>;
const formatDate = (iso: string) => new Date(iso).toLocaleDateString("sv-SE", { day: "numeric", month: "short", year: "numeric" });
const volume = (v?: number | null) => v != null && Number(v) > 0 ? `${Number(v).toFixed(2)} m³` : "–";

export default function ImpregneringList() {
  const [specs, setSpecs] = useState<Treatment[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [deleteTarget, setDeleteTarget] = useState<Treatment | null>(null);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState(""); const [status, setStatus] = useState("alla");
  const [type, setType] = useState<"alla" | TreatmentType>("alla"); const [dateFrom, setDateFrom] = useState(""); const [dateTo, setDateTo] = useState("");
  const { toast } = useToast(); const { user } = useUser(); const [, navigate] = useLocation();
  const isAdmin = (user?.publicMetadata?.role as string) !== "anvandare";
  async function load() {
    setLoading(true);
    try { setSpecs(await customFetch<Treatment[]>("/api/spec?typ=impregnering")); }
    catch (e) { toast({ title: "Kunde inte ladda behandlingar", description: e instanceof Error ? e.message : undefined, variant: "destructive" }); }
    finally { setLoading(false); }
  }
  useEffect(() => { load(); }, []);
  const filtered = useMemo(() => specs.filter(s => {
    const performer = s.utforare ?? s.kund ?? "";
    const treatmentType = s.behandlingTyp ?? "IMPREGNERING";
    return (!search || performer.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())) &&
      (status === "alla" || s.status === status) && (type === "alla" || treatmentType === type) &&
      (!dateFrom || s.skapadAt >= dateFrom) && (!dateTo || s.skapadAt <= `${dateTo}T23:59:59`);
  }), [specs, search, status, type, dateFrom, dateTo]);
  const hasFilter = !!(search || status !== "alla" || type !== "alla" || dateFrom || dateTo);
  async function confirmDelete() {
    if (!deleteTarget) return;
    try { await customFetch(`/api/spec/${deleteTarget.id}`, { method: "DELETE" }); toast({ title: `Behandling #${deleteTarget.id} raderad` }); setDeleteTarget(null); load(); }
    catch (e) { toast({ title: "Kunde inte radera behandlingen", description: e instanceof ApiError ? e.message : undefined, variant: "destructive" }); }
  }
  const reset = () => { setSearch(""); setStatus("alla"); setType("alla"); setDateFrom(""); setDateTo(""); };
  return <div className="space-y-6">
    <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3"><div><h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight flex items-center gap-3"><FlaskConical className="h-7 w-7 text-primary" />Behandlingar</h1><p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm hidden sm:block">Paket hos extern behandling finns kvar i lagret tills de returneras.</p></div>
      <Link href="/behandlingar/ny"><Button size="sm" className="gap-1.5"><Plus className="h-4 w-4" />Ny behandling</Button></Link></div>
    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2 items-center">
      <div className="flex rounded-md border p-0.5 shrink-0" aria-label="Filtrera behandlingstyp">
        {([["alla","Alla"],["IMPREGNERING","Impregnering"],["GRUNDMALNING","Grundmålning"]] as const).map(([value,label]) => <Button key={value} type="button" size="sm" variant={type === value ? "secondary" : "ghost"} onClick={() => setType(value)}>{label}</Button>)}
      </div>
      <div className="relative flex-1 min-w-[180px]"><Search className="absolute left-2.5 top-2.5 h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" /><Input className="pl-9" placeholder="Sök utförare / anläggning..." value={search} onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)} /></div>
      <Select value={status} onValueChange={setStatus}><SelectTrigger className="w-full sm:w-[150px]"><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger><SelectContent><SelectItem value="alla">Alla status</SelectItem><SelectItem value="utkast">Utkast</SelectItem><SelectItem value="bekraftad">Hos behandling</SelectItem><SelectItem value="returnerad">Returnerad</SelectItem></SelectContent></Select>
      <Input type="date" className="w-[145px] max-w-full" value={dateFrom} onChange={e => setDateFrom(e.target.value)} title="Från datum" /><Input type="date" className="w-[145px] max-w-full" value={dateTo} onChange={e => setDateTo(e.target.value)} title="Till datum" />
      {hasFilter && <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={reset} className="gap-1"><X className="h-4 w-4" />Rensa</Button>}
    </div>
    {loading ? <div className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">Laddar...</div> : filtered.length === 0 ? <div className="text-center py-20 text-muted-foreground"><FlaskConical className="h-12 w-12 mx-auto mb-3 opacity-30" /><p className="font-medium">{specs.length ? "Inga behandlingar matchar filtret" : "Inga behandlingar ännu"}</p></div> : <>
      <div className="sm:hidden space-y-2">{filtered.map(s => <div key={s.id} className="border rounded-lg p-3 bg-card cursor-pointer" onClick={() => navigate(`/behandlingar/${s.id}`)}><div className="flex justify-between gap-2"><div className="min-w-0"><div className="flex gap-1 flex-wrap mb-1">{typeBadge(s.behandlingTyp)}{statusBadge(s.status)}</div><p className="font-medium truncate">{s.utforare ?? s.kund ?? "–"}</p><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{formatDate(s.skapadAt)} · {s.antalPaket} paket · Nom {volume(s.totalM3)} · Act {volume(s.totalActM3)}</p></div><div className="flex items-center"><span className="font-mono text-xs">#{s.id}</span>{isAdmin && <button onClick={e => { e.stopPropagation(); setDeleteTarget(s); }} className="p-1.5 text-destructive"><Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /></button>}<ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4" /></div></div></div>)}</div>
      <div className="hidden sm:block rounded-lg border overflow-x-auto"><table className="w-full text-sm whitespace-nowrap"><thead className="bg-muted/50"><tr><th className="text-left px-4 py-3">#</th><th className="text-left px-4 py-3">Typ</th><th className="text-left px-4 py-3">Utförare / anläggning</th><th className="text-right px-4 py-3">Paket</th><th className="text-right px-4 py-3">Nom. m³</th><th className="text-right px-4 py-3">Act. m³</th><th className="text-left px-4 py-3">Datum</th><th className="text-left px-4 py-3">Status</th><th /></tr></thead><tbody>{filtered.map(s => <tr key={s.id} className="border-t hover:bg-muted/30 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => navigate(`/behandlingar/${s.id}`)}><td className="px-4 py-3 font-mono">{s.id}</td><td className="px-4 py-3">{typeBadge(s.behandlingTyp)}</td><td className="px-4 py-3 font-medium">{s.utforare ?? s.kund ?? "–"}</td><td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">{s.antalPaket}</td><td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">{volume(s.totalM3)}</td><td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">{volume(s.totalActM3)}</td><td className="px-4 py-3">{formatDate(s.skapadAt)}</td><td className="px-4 py-3">{statusBadge(s.status)}</td><td>{isAdmin && <button onClick={e => { e.stopPropagation(); setDeleteTarget(s); }} className="p-1.5 text-destructive"><Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /></button>}<ChevronRight className="inline h-4 w-4" /></td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div>
    </>}
    <AlertDialog open={!!deleteTarget} onOpenChange={open => !open && setDeleteTarget(null)}><AlertDialogContent><AlertDialogHeader><AlertDialogTitle>Radera behandling #{deleteTarget?.id}?</AlertDialogTitle><AlertDialogDescription>{deleteTarget?.status === "bekraftad" ? "Behandlingen är bekräftad — återöppna den först." : "Behandlingen raderas permanent. Paketen påverkas inte."}</AlertDialogDescription></AlertDialogHeader><AlertDialogFooter><AlertDialogCancel>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>{deleteTarget?.status !== "bekraftad" && <AlertDialogAction className="bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground" onClick={confirmDelete}>Radera</AlertDialogAction>}</AlertDialogFooter></AlertDialogContent></AlertDialog>
  </div>;
}

  customFetch,
  ApiError,
  useListLagerPoster,
  getListLagerPosterQueryKey,
