type TreatmentType = "IMPREGNERING" | "GRUNDMALNING";
type Ntr = "NTR_A" | "NTR_AB";
type Pkg = {
  id: number;
  pktNr: string | null;
  varuslag: string | null;
  traslag: string | null;
  profil: string | null;
  nomTjocklek: number | null;
  nomBredd: number | null;
  actTjocklek: number | null;
  actBredd: number | null;
  langdDm: number | null;
  antal: number | null;
  lpm: number | null;
  m3: number | null;
  actM3?: number | null;
  plats: string | null;
  ntrKlass?: Ntr | null;
};
type SavedSpec = {
  id: number;
  kund: string | null;
  utforare?: string | null;
  orderNr: string | null;
  status: string;
  paket: Pkg[];
  behandlingTyp?: TreatmentType | null;
  ntrKlass?: Ntr | null;
  olikaNtrPerPaket?: boolean;
  kulor?: string | null;
  ncsRalKod?: string | null;
  antalStrykningar?: number | null;
  maladeSidor?: number | null;
  behandlingsInstruktion?: string | null;
  showCertificationOnDocument?: boolean;
  documentSenderSnapshot?: Branding | null;
};
const ntrLabel = (n?: Ntr | null) =>
  n === "NTR_A" ? "NTR A" : n === "NTR_AB" ? "NTR AB" : "Ej angiven";
const typeLabel = (t: TreatmentType) =>
  t === "IMPREGNERING" ? "Impregnering" : "Grundmålning";
const dim = (a: number | null, b: number | null) =>
  !a && !b ? "–" : `${a ?? "–"}×${b ?? "–"}`;
const nom = (p: Pkg) =>
  p.nomTjocklek && p.nomBredd && p.lpm
    ? (((p.nomTjocklek / 1000) * p.nomBredd) / 1000) * p.lpm
    : p.m3;
const act = (p: Pkg) =>
  p.actM3 ??
  (p.actTjocklek && p.actBredd && p.lpm
    ? (((p.actTjocklek / 1000) * p.actBredd) / 1000) * p.lpm
    : null);

export default function ImpregneringPage() {
  const branding = useBranding();
  const { id } = useParams<{ id?: string }>();
  const specId = id && id !== "ny" ? Number(id) : null;
  const [, navigate] = useLocation();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const qc = useQueryClient();
  const [type, setType] = useState<TreatmentType>("IMPREGNERING"),
    [utforare, setUtforare] = useState(""),
    [orderNr, setOrderNr] = useState(""),
    [status, setStatus] = useState("utkast");
  const [ntr, setNtr] = useState<Ntr | null>(null),
    [different, setDifferent] = useState(false),
    [packageNtr, setPackageNtr] = useState<Map<number, Ntr>>(new Map());
  const [kulor, setKulor] = useState(""),
    [ncsRal, setNcsRal] = useState(""),
    [coats, setCoats] = useState(""),
    [sides, setSides] = useState(""),
    [instruction, setInstruction] = useState("");
  const [showCertificationOnDocument, setShowCertificationOnDocument] = useState(false);
  const [documentSenderSnapshot, setDocumentSenderSnapshot] = useState<Branding | null>(null);
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Map<number, Pkg>>(new Map()),
    [search, setSearch] = useState(""),
    [scanner, setScanner] = useState(false),
    [loading, setLoading] = useState(!!specId),
    [saving, setSaving] = useState(false),
    [busy, setBusy] = useState(false),
    [disableNtrDialog, setDisableNtrDialog] = useState(false),
    [pdfLoading, setPdfLoading] = useState(false);
  const locked = status === "bekraftad" || status === "returnerad";
  const impregnation = type === "IMPREGNERING";
  const searchArgs = {
    search: search || undefined,
    status: "i_lager" as const,
    limit: 200,
  };
  const { data: lagerData } = useListLagerPoster(searchArgs, {
    query: {
      queryKey: getListLagerPosterQueryKey(searchArgs),
      enabled: !!search && !locked,
    },
  });
  const searchPkgs = ((lagerData?.data ?? []) as unknown as Pkg[]).filter(
    (p) => !selected.has(p.id),
  );
  const list = [...selected.values()];
  const [packageSort, setPackageSort] = useState<TreatmentSort[]>([]);
  const sortedList = useMemo(
    () => sortTreatmentPackages(list, packageSort, {
      ntrLabel: (p) => packageNtr.get(p.id) ?? ntr,
      nomM3: nom,
      actM3: act,
    }),
    [list, packageSort, packageNtr, ntr],
  );
  const togglePackageSort = (key: TreatmentSortKey) => {
    setPackageSort((current) => {
      const active = current.find((entry) => entry.key === key);
      return [{ key, dir: active?.dir === "asc" ? "desc" : "asc" }];
    });
  };
  const sortableHeader = (key: TreatmentSortKey, label: string, align = "text-left") => {
    const active = packageSort.find((entry) => entry.key === key);
    const Icon = active?.dir === "asc" ? ChevronUp : active?.dir === "desc" ? ChevronDown : ChevronsUpDown;
    return (
      <th className={align}>
        <button type="button" className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 py-2 font-semibold" onClick={() => togglePackageSort(key)}>
          {label}<Icon className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
        </button>
      </th>
    );
  };
  const totals = useMemo(
    () => ({
      nom: list.reduce((x, p) => x + (nom(p) ?? 0), 0),
      act: list.reduce((x, p) => x + (act(p) ?? 0), 0),
      pieces: list.reduce((x, p) => x + (p.antal ?? 0), 0),
    }),
    [list],
  );
  const ntrValues = new Set(
    list
      .map((p) => packageNtr.get(p.id) ?? ntr)
      .filter((value): value is Ntr => value === "NTR_A" || value === "NTR_AB"),
  );
  const mixed = different && ntrValues.has("NTR_A") && ntrValues.has("NTR_AB");
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!specId) return;
    setLoading(true);
    customFetch<SavedSpec>(`/api/spec/${specId}`)
      .then((s) => {
        setType(s.behandlingTyp ?? "IMPREGNERING");
        setUtforare(s.utforare ?? s.kund ?? "");
        setOrderNr(s.orderNr ?? "");
        setStatus(s.status);
        setNtr(s.ntrKlass ?? null);
        setDifferent(!!s.olikaNtrPerPaket);
        setKulor(s.kulor ?? "");
        setNcsRal(s.ncsRalKod ?? "");
        setCoats(s.antalStrykningar?.toString() ?? "");
        setSides(s.maladeSidor?.toString() ?? "");
        setInstruction(s.behandlingsInstruktion ?? "");
        setShowCertificationOnDocument(s.showCertificationOnDocument === true);
        setDocumentSenderSnapshot(s.documentSenderSnapshot ? normalizeBranding(s.documentSenderSnapshot) : null);
        setSelected(new Map(s.paket.map((p) => [p.id, p])));
        // Old treatment records can have only a spec-wide class. Seed their
        // rows from that value so package-specific controls are never blank.
        setPackageNtr(
          new Map(
            s.paket
              .map((p) => [p.id, p.ntrKlass ?? s.ntrKlass] as const)
              .filter(
                (entry): entry is readonly [number, Ntr] => entry[1] !== null,
              ),
          ),
        );
      })
      .catch((e) =>
        toast({
          title: "Kunde inte ladda behandlingen",
          description: e instanceof Error ? e.message : undefined,
          variant: "destructive",
        }),
      )
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, [specId]);
  function changeType(value: TreatmentType) {
    if (value === type) return;
    const populated =
      type === "IMPREGNERING"
        ? !!ntr || different || packageNtr.size > 0
        : !!(kulor || ncsRal || coats || sides || instruction);
    if (
      populated &&
      !window.confirm("Typens fält kommer att rensas. Vill du fortsätta?")
    )
      return;
    if (value === "IMPREGNERING") {
      setKulor("");
      setNcsRal("");
      setCoats("");
      setSides("");
      setInstruction("");
    } else {
      setNtr(null);
      setDifferent(false);
      setPackageNtr(new Map());
    }
    setType(value);
  }
  function add(p: Pkg) {
    setSelected((v) => new Map(v).set(p.id, p));
    if (different && ntr) setPackageNtr((v) => new Map(v).set(p.id, ntr));
  }
  function addPackageRows(rows: Pkg[]): KameraScanResult {
    const newRows = rows.filter((p) => !selected.has(p.id));
    if (!newRows.length)
      return { kind: "duplicate", selectedCount: list.length };
    setSelected((old) => {
      const next = new Map(old);
      newRows.forEach((p) => next.set(p.id, p));
      return next;
    });
    if (different && ntr)
      setPackageNtr((old) => {
        const next = new Map(old);
        newRows.forEach((p) => next.set(p.id, ntr));
        return next;
      });
    toast({
      title: `Paket ${newRows[0].pktNr ?? ""} tillagt`,
      description: `${newRows.length} lagerposter lades till.`,
    });
    return { kind: "added", selectedCount: list.length + newRows.length };
  }
  async function addByPktNr(pktNr: string): Promise<KameraScanResult> {
    try {
      const result = await customFetch<{ rader: Pkg[] }>(
        `/api/lager/paket/${encodeURIComponent(pktNr)}`,
      );
      if (!result.rader.length)
        return { kind: "not-found", selectedCount: list.length };
      return addPackageRows(result.rader);
    } catch (e) {
      toast({
        title: `Paket ${pktNr} hittades inte`,
        description: apiError(e, "Paketet saknas i lagret."),
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return { kind: "not-found", selectedCount: list.length };
    }
  }
  function remove(pid: number) {
    setSelected((v) => {
      const n = new Map(v);
      n.delete(pid);
      return n;
    });
    setPackageNtr((v) => {
      const n = new Map(v);
      n.delete(pid);
      return n;
    });
  }
  function apiError(e: unknown, fallback: string) {
    return e instanceof ApiError || e instanceof Error ? e.message : fallback;
  }
  function validate() {
    if (!list.length) {
      toast({ title: "Lägg till minst ett paket", variant: "destructive" });
      return false;
    }
    if (
      impregnation &&
      (!ntr || (different && list.some((p) => !packageNtr.get(p.id))))
    ) {
      toast({
        title: "Välj NTR-klass",
        description: different
          ? "Välj klass för varje paket."
          : "NTR-klass krävs för impregnering.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return false;
    }
    if (!impregnation && !kulor.trim()) {
      toast({ title: "Kulör krävs för grundmålning", variant: "destructive" });
      return false;
    }
    if (coats && (!Number.isInteger(Number(coats)) || Number(coats) <= 0)) {
      toast({
        title: "Antal strykningar måste vara ett positivt heltal",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return false;
    }
    if (
      sides &&
      (!Number.isInteger(Number(sides)) ||
        Number(sides) < 1 ||
        Number(sides) > 4)
    ) {
      toast({
        title: "Målade sidor måste vara mellan 1 och 4",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return false;
    }
    return true;
  }
  const body = () => ({
    typ: "impregnering",
    behandlingTyp: type,
    kund: utforare || null,
    utforare: utforare || null,
    orderNr: orderNr || null,
    paketIds: list.map((p) => p.id),
    ntrKlass: impregnation ? ntr : null,
    olikaNtrPerPaket: impregnation && different,
    ntrByPaket:
      impregnation && different
        ? Object.fromEntries(
            list.map((p) => [p.id, packageNtr.get(p.id) ?? ntr]),
          )
        : {},
    kulor: !impregnation ? kulor.trim() : null,
    ncsRalKod: !impregnation ? ncsRal.trim() || null : null,
    antalStrykningar: !impregnation && coats ? Number(coats) : null,
    maladeSidor: !impregnation && sides ? Number(sides) : null,
    behandlingsInstruktion: !impregnation ? instruction.trim() || null : null,
    showCertificationOnDocument,
  });
  async function save(check = true) {
    if (check && !validate()) return null;
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      if (specId) {
        await customFetch(`/api/spec/${specId}`, {
          method: "PUT",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify(body()),
        });
        toast({ title: "Behandling sparad" });
        return specId;
      }
      const result = await customFetch<{ id: number }>("/api/spec", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify(body()),
      });
      toast({ title: "Behandling sparad som utkast" });
      navigate(`/behandlingar/${result.id}`);
      return result.id;
    } catch (e) {
      toast({
        title: "Fel vid sparning",
        description: apiError(e, "Kunde inte spara"),
        variant: "destructive",
      });
      return null;
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }
  const invalidate = () => {
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: getGetLagerStatistikQueryKey() });
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager"] });
    qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/spec"] });
  };
  async function confirm() {
    const spec = await save();
    if (!spec) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await customFetch(`/api/spec/${spec}/bekrafta`, { method: "POST" });
      setStatus("bekraftad");
      invalidate();
      toast({
        title: `Behandling #${spec} bekräftad — paketen är hos behandling.`,
      });
      setTimeout(print, 150);
    } catch (e) {
      toast({
        title: "Fel vid bekräftelse",
        description: apiError(e, "Kunde inte bekräfta"),
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }
  async function action(endpoint: string, next: string, message: string) {
    if (!specId) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await customFetch(`/api/spec/${specId}/${endpoint}`, { method: "POST" });
      setStatus(next);
      invalidate();
      toast({ title: message });
    } catch (e) {
      toast({
        title: "Åtgärden misslyckades",
        description: apiError(e, "Okänt fel"),
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }
  async function deleteSpec() {
    if (!specId) return;
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await customFetch(`/api/spec/${specId}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      invalidate();
      navigate("/behandlingar");
    } catch (e) {
      toast({
        title: "Kunde inte radera",
        description: apiError(e, "Okänt fel"),
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }
  function print() {
    const source = document.getElementById("treatment-print");
    if (!source) return;
    const clone = source.cloneNode(true) as HTMLElement;
    clone.id = "treatment-print-portal";
    document.body.appendChild(clone);
    const style = document.createElement("style");
    style.textContent =
      "@media print{@page{size:A4 landscape;margin:8mm}body>*{display:none!important}#treatment-print-portal{display:block!important;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;color:#111;font-size:9px}#treatment-print-portal thead{display:table-header-group}#treatment-print-portal tr{break-inside:avoid}}";
    document.head.append(style);
    window.addEventListener(
      "afterprint",
      () => {
        clone.remove();
        style.remove();
      },
      { once: true },
    );
    window.print();
  }
  async function pdf() {
    const source = document.getElementById("treatment-print");
    if (!source) return;
    setPdfLoading(true);
    const original = source.getAttribute("style") ?? "";
    source.setAttribute(
      "style",
      "display:block;position:fixed;left:-9999px;top:0;width:277mm;padding:8mm;box-sizing:border-box;background:#fff;font-family:Arial,sans-serif;font-size:9px;color:#111",
    );
    try {
      await new Promise<void>((resolve) =>
        requestAnimationFrame(() => requestAnimationFrame(() => resolve())),
      );
      const [{ default: html2canvas }, { default: jsPDF }] = await Promise.all([
        import("html2canvas"),
        import("jspdf"),
      ]);
      const canvas = await html2canvas(source, {
        scale: 2,
        useCORS: true,
        backgroundColor: "#ffffff",
        logging: false,
      });
      const pdfDocument = new jsPDF({
        orientation: "landscape",
        unit: "mm",
        format: "a4",
      });
      const width = pdfDocument.internal.pageSize.getWidth(),
        height = pdfDocument.internal.pageSize.getHeight(),
        imageHeight = (width * canvas.height) / canvas.width;
      for (
        let y = 0, remaining = imageHeight;
        remaining > 0;
        y -= height, remaining -= height
      ) {
        pdfDocument.addImage(
          canvas.toDataURL("image/jpeg", 0.95),
          "JPEG",
          0,
          y,
          width,
          imageHeight,
        );
        if (remaining > height) pdfDocument.addPage();
      }
      pdfDocument.save(`${typeLabel(type)}-${specId ?? "ny"}.pdf`);
    } catch (e) {
      toast({
        title: "PDF-export misslyckades",
        description: apiError(e, "Okänt fel"),
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      source.setAttribute("style", original);
      setPdfLoading(false);
    }
  }
  const setAllNtr = (value: Ntr) => {
    setNtr(value);
    setPackageNtr(new Map(list.map((p) => [p.id, value])));
    setDifferent(false);
    setDisableNtrDialog(false);
  };
  if (loading)
    return (
      <div className="flex gap-2 text-muted-foreground">
        <Loader2 className="animate-spin h-4 w-4" />
        Laddar…
      </div>
    );
  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <SkannaDialog
        open={scanner}
        onClose={() => setScanner(false)}
        onFound={(p) => {
          add(p as Pkg);
          setScanner(false);
        }}
        onPktNrFunnet={addByPktNr}
        selectedCount={list.length}
      />
      <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-between gap-3">
        <div className="flex gap-3">
          <Button
            variant="ghost"
            size="icon"
            onClick={() => navigate("/behandlingar")}
          >
            <ArrowLeft />
          </Button>
          <div>
            <div className="flex gap-2 items-center">
              <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold">
                {specId ? `Behandling #${specId}` : "Ny behandling"}
              </h1>
              {status === "bekraftad" ? (
                <Badge>Hos behandling</Badge>
              ) : status === "returnerad" ? (
                <Badge className="bg-green-100 text-green-800">
                  Returnerad
                </Badge>
              ) : (
                <Badge variant="outline">Utkast</Badge>
              )}
            </div>
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              {locked
                ? "Paketens behandling är avslutad eller pågår."
                : "Välj behandlingstyp, uppgifter och paket."}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
        {specId && (
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
            {status === "bekraftad" && (
              <Button
                onClick={() =>
                  action(
                    "returnera",
                    "returnerad",
                    "Paket returnerade från behandling",
                  )
                }
                disabled={busy}
              >
                <PackageCheck />
                Registrera retur
              </Button>
            )}
            {status === "bekraftad" && (
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                onClick={() =>
                  action("ateroppen", "utkast", "Behandling återöppnad")
                }
                disabled={busy}
              >
                <RotateCcw />
                Återöppna
              </Button>
            )}
            {!locked && (
              <Button
                variant="outline"
                className="text-destructive"
                onClick={() => {
                  if (window.confirm("Radera behandlingen?")) deleteSpec();
                }}
              >
                <Trash2 />
                Radera
              </Button>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      <Card>
        <CardHeader className="pb-2">
          <CardTitle className="text-base">Behandlingsuppgifter</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Behandlingstyp</label>
            <Select
              value={type}
              onValueChange={(v) => changeType(v as TreatmentType)}
              disabled={locked}
            >
              <SelectTrigger>
                <SelectValue />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="IMPREGNERING">Impregnering</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="GRUNDMALNING">Grundmålning</SelectItem>
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Utförare / anläggning</label>
            <Input
              value={utforare}
              onChange={(e) => setUtforare(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="Ex. Bitus, Korsberga"
              disabled={locked}
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">
              Referens / ordernummer
            </label>
            <Input
              value={orderNr}
              onChange={(e) => setOrderNr(e.target.value)}
              disabled={locked}
            />
          </div>
          {impregnation ? (
            <>
              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">
                  NTR-klass <span className="text-destructive">*</span>
                </label>
                <Select
                  value={ntr ?? "none"}
                  onValueChange={(v) =>
                    setNtr(v === "none" ? null : (v as Ntr))
                  }
                  disabled={locked}
                >
                  <SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectValue />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent>
                    {!ntr && specId && (
                      <SelectItem value="none">Ej angiven</SelectItem>
                    )}
                    <SelectItem value="NTR_AB">NTR AB</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="NTR_A">NTR A</SelectItem>
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-end gap-2 pb-2">
                <Checkbox
                  id="different-ntr"
                  checked={different}
                  disabled={locked}
                  onCheckedChange={(v) => {
                    if (!v && different && mixed) setDisableNtrDialog(true);
                    else if (v) {
                      setDifferent(true);
                      setPackageNtr(
                        new Map(
                          list
                            .map((p) => [p.id, ntr])
                            .filter(
                              (entry): entry is [number, Ntr] =>
                                entry[1] !== null,
                            ),
                        ),
                      );
                    } else setDifferent(false);
                  }}
                />
                <label htmlFor="different-ntr" className="text-sm">
                  Olika NTR-klasser per paket
                </label>
              </div>
            </>
          ) : (
            <>
              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">
                  Kulör <span className="text-destructive">*</span>
                </label>
                <Input
                  value={kulor}
                  onChange={(e) => setKulor(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={locked}
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">NCS/RAL-kod</label>
                <Input
                  value={ncsRal}
                  onChange={(e) => setNcsRal(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={locked}
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">Antal strykningar</label>
                <Input
                  type="number"
                  min="1"
                  step="1"
                  value={coats}
                  onChange={(e) => setCoats(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={locked}
                />
              </div>
              <div>
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">Målade sidor</label>
                <Select
                  value={sides || "none"}
                  onValueChange={(v) => setSides(v === "none" ? "" : v)}
                  disabled={locked}
                >
                  <SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectValue placeholder="Välj antal" />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent>
                    <SelectItem value="none">Ej angivet</SelectItem>
                    {[1, 2, 3, 4].map((x) => (
                      <SelectItem key={x} value={`${x}`}>
                        {x}
                      </SelectItem>
                    ))}
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </div>
              <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">
                  Instruktion / anteckning
                </label>
                <Textarea
                  value={instruction}
                  onChange={(e) => setInstruction(e.target.value)}
                  disabled={locked}
                />
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
      <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
        <div className="space-y-3">
          <Card>
            <CardContent className="pt-4">
              <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium">
                <Checkbox
                  checked={showCertificationOnDocument}
                  onCheckedChange={(checked) => setShowCertificationOnDocument(checked === true)}
                  disabled={locked}
                />
                Visa certifieringsuppgifter på dokumentet
              </label>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
          {!locked && (
            <Card>
              <CardHeader className="pb-2">
                <CardTitle className="text-base">Lägg till paket</CardTitle>
              </CardHeader>
              <CardContent className="space-y-2">
                <Button
                  variant="outline"
                  className="w-full"
                  onClick={() => setScanner(true)}
                >
                  <Search />
                  Skanna paketlapp
                </Button>
                <div className="relative">
                  <Search className="absolute left-2 top-2.5 h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
                  <Input
                    className="pl-8"
                    value={search}
                    onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
                    placeholder="Sök paket…"
                  />
                </div>
                {searchPkgs.map((p) => (
                  <div
                    key={p.id}
                    className="flex justify-between gap-2 text-xs border rounded p-2"
                  >
                    <span>
                      {p.pktNr} · {dim(p.nomTjocklek, p.nomBredd)}
                    </span>
                    <Button size="sm" onClick={() => add(p)}>
                      <Plus />
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="lg:col-span-2">
          <Card>
            <CardHeader className="flex flex-row flex-wrap justify-between gap-2 pb-2">
              <CardTitle className="text-base">
                Valda paket <Badge variant="secondary">{list.length}</Badge>
              </CardTitle>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                {locked ? (
                  <>
                    <Button
                      variant="outline"
                      onClick={pdf}
                      disabled={pdfLoading}
                    >
                      <FileDown />
                      Ladda ner PDF
                    </Button>
                    <Button variant="outline" onClick={print}>
                      <Printer />
                      Skriv ut
                    </Button>
                  </>
                ) : (
                  <>
                    <Button
                      variant="outline"
                      onClick={() => save()}
                      disabled={saving}
                    >
                      <Save />
                      Spara utkast
                    </Button>
                    <Button onClick={confirm} disabled={saving || busy}>
                      <FlaskConical />
                      Bekräfta & skriv ut
                    </Button>
                  </>
                )}
              </div>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent>
              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full text-sm">
                  <thead>
                    <tr className="border-b">
                      {sortableHeader("pktNr", "Pkt-nr")}
                      {sortableHeader("produkt", "Produkt")}
                      {sortableHeader("nomDim", "Nom dim", "text-center")}
                      {sortableHeader("antal", "Antal", "text-center")}
                      {impregnation && different && sortableHeader("ntrKlass", "NTR-klass", "text-center")}
                      {sortableHeader("nomM3", "Nom m³", "text-right")}
                      {sortableHeader("actM3", "Act m³", "text-right")}
                      {!locked && <th />}
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {sortedList.map((p) => (
                      <tr className="border-b" key={p.id}>
                        <td className="py-2 font-mono">{p.pktNr}</td>
                        <td>
                          {[p.traslag, p.profil].filter(Boolean).join(" / ") ||
                            "–"}
                        </td>
                        <td>{dim(p.nomTjocklek, p.nomBredd)}</td>
                        <td className="text-center">{p.antal ?? "–"}</td>
                        {impregnation && different && (
                          <td>
                            {locked ? (
                              ntrLabel(packageNtr.get(p.id) ?? ntr)
                            ) : (
                              <Select
                                value={packageNtr.get(p.id) ?? "none"}
                                onValueChange={(v) =>
                                  setPackageNtr((old) =>
                                    new Map(old).set(p.id, v as Ntr),
                                  )
                                }
                              >
                                <SelectTrigger className="h-8 w-28">
                                  <SelectValue />
                                </SelectTrigger>
                                <SelectContent>
                                  <SelectItem value="NTR_AB">NTR AB</SelectItem>
                                  <SelectItem value="NTR_A">NTR A</SelectItem>
                                </SelectContent>
                              </Select>
                            )}
                          </td>
                        )}
                        <td className="text-right">
                          {nom(p)?.toFixed(3) ?? "–"}
                        </td>
                        <td className="text-right">
                          {act(p)?.toFixed(3) ?? "–"}
                        </td>
                        {!locked && (
                          <td>
                            <Button
                              variant="ghost"
                              size="icon"
                              onClick={() => remove(p.id)}
                            >
                              <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                            </Button>
                          </td>
                        )}
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                  <tfoot>
                    <tr className="font-bold">
                      <td colSpan={impregnation && different ? 4 : 4}>
                        Summa ({list.length} paket)
                      </td>
                      {impregnation && different && <td />}
                      <td className="text-right">{totals.nom.toFixed(3)}</td>
                      <td className="text-right">{totals.act.toFixed(3)}</td>
                      {!locked && <td />}
                    </tr>
                  </tfoot>
                </table>
              </div>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </div>
      </div>
      <AlertDialog open={disableNtrDialog} onOpenChange={setDisableNtrDialog}>
        <AlertDialogContent>
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>Välj NTR-klass för alla paket</AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>
              Paketen har olika klasser. Välj vilken klass som ska användas när
              paketvisa val stängs av.
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <AlertDialogCancel>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
            <AlertDialogAction onClick={() => setAllNtr("NTR_AB")}>
              Använd NTR AB för alla
            </AlertDialogAction>
            <AlertDialogAction onClick={() => setAllNtr("NTR_A")}>
              Använd NTR A för alla
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>
      <TreatmentPrint
        id={specId}
        type={type}
        utforare={utforare}
        orderNr={orderNr}
        ntr={ntr}
        different={different}
        packageNtr={packageNtr}
        list={sortedList}
        totals={totals}
        kulor={kulor}
        ncsRal={ncsRal}
        coats={coats}
        sides={sides}
        instruction={instruction}
        showCertificationOnDocument={showCertificationOnDocument}
        documentSender={documentSenderSnapshot ?? branding}
      />
    </div>
  );
}
function TreatmentPrint(props: {
  id: number | null;
  type: TreatmentType;
  utforare: string;
  orderNr: string;
  ntr: Ntr | null;
  different: boolean;
  packageNtr: Map<number, Ntr>;
  list: Pkg[];
  totals: { nom: number; act: number; pieces: number };
  kulor: string;
  ncsRal: string;
  coats: string;
  sides: string;
  instruction: string;
  showCertificationOnDocument: boolean;
  documentSender: Branding;
}) {
  const { type, list, packageNtr, ntr } = props;
  const values = new Set(list.map((p) => packageNtr.get(p.id) ?? ntr));
  const mixed = props.different && values.has("NTR_A") && values.has("NTR_AB");
  const cell = { border: "1px solid #ccc", padding: 3 };
  const headers = [
    "Pkt-nr",
    "Träslag / profil",
    "Nom dim",
    "Act dim",
    "Längd (dm)",
    "St",
    "Lpm",
    ...(mixed ? ["NTR-klass"] : []),
    "Nom m³",
    "Act m³",
  ];
  return (
    <div id="treatment-print" style={{ display: "none" }}>
      <div
        style={{
          borderTop: "4px solid #2d5a27",
          paddingTop: 10,
          marginBottom: 10,
          display: "flex",
          justifyContent: "space-between",
        }}
      >
        <div>
          {props.documentSender.logoUrl && <img src={props.documentSender.logoUrl} alt={props.documentSender.companyName || "Logotyp"} style={{ height: 30 }} />}
          <b style={{ display: "block", color: "#2d5a27" }}>
            {props.documentSender.companyName}
          </b>
        </div>
        <div style={{ textAlign: "center" }}>
          <b style={{ fontSize: 15, letterSpacing: 1 }}>
            {type === "IMPREGNERING"
              ? "IMPREGNERINGSSPECIFIKATION"
              : "GRUNDMÅLNINGSSPECIFIKATION"}
          </b>
          <div>
            #{props.id ?? "—"} · {new Date().toLocaleDateString("sv-SE")}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div>
          Utförare: <b>{props.utforare || "—"}</b>
          <br />
          Ref: <b>{props.orderNr || "—"}</b>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>
        Behandlingstyp: <b>{typeLabel(type)}</b>
        {type === "IMPREGNERING" ? (
          <>
            {" "}
            · NTR-klass: <b>{mixed ? "Blandad – se paket" : ntrLabel(ntr)}</b>
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            {props.kulor && (
              <>
                {" "}
                · Kulör: <b>{props.kulor}</b>
              </>
            )}
            {props.ncsRal && (
              <>
                {" "}
                · NCS/RAL: <b>{props.ncsRal}</b>
              </>
            )}
            {props.coats && (
              <>
                {" "}
                · Strykningar: <b>{props.coats}</b>
              </>
            )}
            {props.sides && (
              <>
                {" "}
                · Målade sidor: <b>{props.sides}</b>
              </>
            )}
            {props.instruction && <div>Instruktion: {props.instruction}</div>}
          </>
        )}
      </div>
      <table style={{ borderCollapse: "collapse", width: "100%" }}>
        <thead>
          <tr>
            {headers.map((x) => (
              <th
                key={x}
                style={{
                  textAlign: "left",
                  border: "1px solid #aaa",
                  padding: 3,
                  background: "#edf3ea",
                }}
              >
                {x}
              </th>
            ))}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {list.map((p) => (
            <tr key={p.id}>
              <td style={cell}>{p.pktNr}</td>
              <td style={cell}>
                {[p.traslag, p.profil].filter(Boolean).join(" / ")}
              </td>
              <td style={cell}>{dim(p.nomTjocklek, p.nomBredd)}</td>
              <td style={cell}>{dim(p.actTjocklek, p.actBredd)}</td>
              <td style={cell}>{p.langdDm ?? "–"}</td>
              <td style={cell}>{p.antal ?? "–"}</td>
              <td style={cell}>{p.lpm ?? "–"}</td>
              {mixed && (
                <td style={cell}>{ntrLabel(packageNtr.get(p.id) ?? ntr)}</td>
              )}
              <td style={cell}>{nom(p)?.toFixed(3) ?? "–"}</td>
              <td style={cell}>{act(p)?.toFixed(3) ?? "–"}</td>
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
        <tfoot>
          <tr>
            <td colSpan={mixed ? 8 : 7} style={{ padding: 3 }}>
              <b>
                Summa ({list.length} paket / {props.totals.pieces} st)
              </b>
            </td>
            <td>
              <b>{props.totals.nom.toFixed(3)}</b>
            </td>
            <td>
              <b>{props.totals.act.toFixed(3)}</b>
            </td>
          </tr>
        </tfoot>
      </table>
      <DocumentCertificationBlock
        show={props.showCertificationOnDocument}
        certificationText={props.documentSender.certificationText}
        certificationNumber={props.documentSender.certificationNumber}
      />
      <DocumentCompanyFooter profile={props.documentSender} />
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/impregnering/treatmentPackageSort.test.ts ================

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { sortTreatmentPackages, type SortableTreatmentPackage, type TreatmentSortKey } from "./treatmentPackageSort";

type Pkg = SortableTreatmentPackage & { ntr?: string | null; calculatedNom?: number | null };
const pkg = (id: number, values: Partial<Pkg> = {}): Pkg => ({
  id, pktNr: null, traslag: null, profil: null, nomTjocklek: null, nomBredd: null,
  antal: null, m3: null, actM3: null, ...values,
});
const context = {
  ntrLabel: (p: Pkg) => p.ntr ?? null,
  nomM3: (p: Pkg) => p.calculatedNom ?? p.m3,
  actM3: (p: Pkg) => p.actM3 ?? null,
};
const order = (items: Pkg[], key: TreatmentSortKey, dir: "asc" | "desc" = "asc") =>
  sortTreatmentPackages(items, [{ key, dir }], context).map(p => p.id);

describe("sortering av valda behandlingspaket", () => {
  it("sorterar paketnummer, antal och volymer numeriskt med tomma sist", () => {
    const items = [pkg(1, { pktNr: "10", antal: 10, calculatedNom: 2, actM3: 3 }), pkg(2), pkg(3, { pktNr: "2", antal: 2, calculatedNom: 1, actM3: 1 })];
    expect(order(items, "pktNr")).toEqual([3, 1, 2]);
    expect(order(items, "antal", "desc")).toEqual([1, 3, 2]);
    expect(order(items, "nomM3")).toEqual([3, 1, 2]);
    expect(order(items, "actM3", "desc")).toEqual([1, 3, 2]);
  });

  it("sorterar svensk produkttext och NTR-klass", () => {
    const items = [pkg(1, { traslag: "Övrigt", ntr: "NTR AB" }), pkg(2, { traslag: "Asp", ntr: "NTR A" }), pkg(3)];
    expect(order(items, "produkt")).toEqual([2, 1, 3]);
    expect(order(items, "ntrKlass")).toEqual([2, 1, 3]);
  });

  it("sorterar dimensioner naturligt och använder samma stabila ordning för alla utdata", () => {
    const items = [pkg(1, { nomTjocklek: 45, nomBredd: 70 }), pkg(2, { nomTjocklek: 22, nomBredd: 145 }), pkg(3, { nomTjocklek: 22, nomBredd: 95 }), pkg(4)];
    const sorted = sortTreatmentPackages(items, [{ key: "nomDim", dir: "asc" }], context);
    expect(sorted.map(p => p.id)).toEqual([3, 2, 1, 4]);
    expect([...sorted].map(p => p.id)).toEqual(sorted.map(p => p.id));
  });
});

================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/impregnering/treatmentPackageSort.ts ================

export type TreatmentSortKey =
  | "pktNr"
  | "produkt"
  | "nomDim"
  | "antal"
  | "ntrKlass"
  | "nomM3"
  | "actM3";

export type TreatmentSort = { key: TreatmentSortKey; dir: "asc" | "desc" };

export type SortableTreatmentPackage = {
  id: number;
  pktNr: string | null;
  traslag: string | null;
  profil: string | null;
  nomTjocklek: number | null;
  nomBredd: number | null;
  antal: number | null;
  m3: number | null;
  actM3?: number | null;
};

type SortContext<T extends SortableTreatmentPackage> = {
  ntrLabel: (pkg: T) => string | null;
  nomM3: (pkg: T) => number | null;
  actM3: (pkg: T) => number | null;
};

const text = (value: string | null | undefined) => value?.trim() || null;
const compareText = (a: string, b: string) =>
  a.localeCompare(b, "sv", { sensitivity: "base", numeric: true });

function compareValue<T extends SortableTreatmentPackage>(
  a: T,
  b: T,
  key: TreatmentSortKey,
  context: SortContext<T>,
): number | null {
  if (key === "pktNr") {
    const av = text(a.pktNr), bv = text(b.pktNr);
    return av === null || bv === null ? null : compareText(av, bv);
  }
  if (key === "produkt") {
    const av = text([a.traslag, a.profil].filter(Boolean).join(" / "));
    const bv = text([b.traslag, b.profil].filter(Boolean).join(" / "));
    return av === null || bv === null ? null : compareText(av, bv);
  }
  if (key === "nomDim") {
    if (a.nomTjocklek == null || a.nomBredd == null || b.nomTjocklek == null || b.nomBredd == null) return null;
    return a.nomTjocklek - b.nomTjocklek || a.nomBredd - b.nomBredd;
  }
  const av = key === "antal" ? a.antal
    : key === "ntrKlass" ? text(context.ntrLabel(a))
    : key === "nomM3" ? context.nomM3(a)
    : context.actM3(a);
  const bv = key === "antal" ? b.antal
    : key === "ntrKlass" ? text(context.ntrLabel(b))
    : key === "nomM3" ? context.nomM3(b)
    : context.actM3(b);
  if (av == null || bv == null) return null;
  return typeof av === "string" && typeof bv === "string" ? compareText(av, bv) : Number(av) - Number(bv);
}

function isEmpty<T extends SortableTreatmentPackage>(
  pkg: T,
  key: TreatmentSortKey,
  context: SortContext<T>,
) {
  if (key === "pktNr") return text(pkg.pktNr) === null;
  if (key === "produkt") return text([pkg.traslag, pkg.profil].filter(Boolean).join(" / ")) === null;
  if (key === "nomDim") return pkg.nomTjocklek == null || pkg.nomBredd == null;
  if (key === "antal") return pkg.antal == null;
  if (key === "ntrKlass") return text(context.ntrLabel(pkg)) === null;
  return (key === "nomM3" ? context.nomM3(pkg) : context.actM3(pkg)) == null;
}

export function sortTreatmentPackages<T extends SortableTreatmentPackage>(
  packages: T[],
  sorts: TreatmentSort[],
  context: SortContext<T>,
): T[] {
  if (sorts.length === 0) return packages;
  const originalIndex = new Map(packages.map((pkg, index) => [pkg.id, index]));
  return [...packages].sort((a, b) => {
    for (const sort of sorts) {
      const aEmpty = isEmpty(a, sort.key, context);
      const bEmpty = isEmpty(b, sort.key, context);
      if (aEmpty !== bEmpty) return aEmpty ? 1 : -1;
      if (aEmpty) continue;
      const comparison = compareValue(a, b, sort.key, context) ?? 0;
      if (comparison !== 0) return sort.dir === "asc" ? comparison : -comparison;
    }
    return (originalIndex.get(a.id) ?? 0) - (originalIndex.get(b.id) ?? 0);
  });
}

type ImpMigrationResult = {
  status: "ready" | "completed" | "already_completed";
  enabled: boolean;
  confirmationPhrase: string;
  wouldChange: number;
  changed: number;
  skipped: number;
  errors: number;
  executedAt: string | null;
  records: Array<{
    id: number;
    pktNr: string | null;
    profil: string | null;
    nomTjocklek: number | null;
    nomBredd: number | null;

function ImpTillFuruMigrationSection() {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [result, setResult] = useState<ImpMigrationResult | null>(null);
  const [confirmation, setConfirmation] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function dryRun() {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      setResult(await customFetch<ImpMigrationResult>("/api/admin/data-migrations/imp-to-furu"));
      setConfirmation("");
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Dry-run misslyckades", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function execute() {
    if (!result) return;
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const next = await customFetch<ImpMigrationResult>("/api/admin/data-migrations/imp-to-furu", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ confirmation }),
      });
      setResult(next);
      setConfirmation("");
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager"] });
      toast({ title: `${next.changed} poster korrigerades` });
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Migreringen misslyckades och återställdes", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Card className="border-amber-300">
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Database className="h-5 w-5 text-amber-700" />
          Historisk korrigering: Imp → Furu
        </CardTitle>
        <CardDescription>
          Adminlåst engångsåtgärd. Kör alltid dry-run först. Endast träslag och tom/null behandling ändras;
          dimensioner och alla andra paketfält lämnas orörda.
        </CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="space-y-4">
        <Button variant="outline" onClick={dryRun} disabled={loading}>
          {loading ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : null}
          Kör dry-run
        </Button>

        {result && (
          <>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3">
              <div className="rounded-md bg-muted p-3"><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Påverkas</p><p className="text-xl font-semibold">{result.wouldChange}</p></div>
              <div className="rounded-md bg-muted p-3"><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Ändrade</p><p className="text-xl font-semibold">{result.changed}</p></div>
              <div className="rounded-md bg-muted p-3"><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Hoppade</p><p className="text-xl font-semibold">{result.skipped}</p></div>
              <div className="rounded-md bg-muted p-3"><p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Fel</p><p className="text-xl font-semibold">{result.errors}</p></div>
            </div>

            {!result.enabled && (
              <div className="rounded-md border border-green-200 bg-green-50 p-3 text-sm text-green-900">
                Engångsåtgärden är slutförd och inaktiverad
                {result.executedAt ? ` sedan ${new Date(result.executedAt).toLocaleString("sv-SE")}` : ""}.
              </div>
            )}

            {result.records.length > 0 && (
              <div className="max-h-72 overflow-auto rounded-md border">
                <table className="w-full text-xs">
                  <thead className="sticky top-0 bg-background">
                    <tr><th className="p-2 text-left">ID</th><th className="p-2 text-left">Pkt Nr</th><th className="p-2 text-left">Profil</th><th className="p-2 text-left">Nom. mått</th><th className="p-2 text-left">Behandling före</th></tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {result.records.map((row) => (
                      <tr key={row.id} className="border-t">
                        <td className="p-2">{row.id}</td>
                        <td className="p-2 font-mono">{row.pktNr ?? "–"}</td>
                        <td className="p-2">{row.profil ?? "–"}</td>
                        <td className="p-2">{row.nomTjocklek ?? "–"}×{row.nomBredd ?? "–"}</td>
                        <td className="p-2">{row.behandling || "Tom"}</td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            )}

            {result.enabled && result.wouldChange > 0 && (
              <AlertDialog>
                <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
                  <Button variant="destructive">Fortsätt till bekräftelse</Button>
                </AlertDialogTrigger>
                <AlertDialogContent>
                  <AlertDialogHeader>
                    <AlertDialogTitle>Korrigera {result.wouldChange} poster?</AlertDialogTitle>
                    <AlertDialogDescription>
                      Skriv exakt <strong>{result.confirmationPhrase}</strong>. Ändringen körs i en databastransaktion,
                      revisionsloggas och kan bara slutföras en gång.
                    </AlertDialogDescription>
                  </AlertDialogHeader>
                  <Input value={confirmation} onChange={(e) => setConfirmation(e.target.value)} />
                  <AlertDialogFooter>
                    <AlertDialogCancel>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
                    <AlertDialogAction
                      onClick={execute}
                      disabled={confirmation !== result.confirmationPhrase || loading}
                    >
                      Kör engångsåtgärden
                    </AlertDialogAction>
                  </AlertDialogFooter>
                </AlertDialogContent>
              </AlertDialog>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

function guessMapping(headers: string[]): MappingState {
  const normalise = (s: string) => s.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-zåäö0-9]/g, "");
  const guesses: Record<string, string[]> = {
    datumIn: ["datumin", "indat", "datumind", "ankomsdat"],
    datumUt: ["datumut", "utdat", "levdat", "leveransdat"],
    mottaget: ["mottaget", "fran", "from", "avsandare"],
    plats: ["plats", "lagerplats", "lagplats", "location"],
    pktNr: ["pktnr", "paketnr", "paket", "pktnummer"],
    produktion: ["produktion", "prod"],
    profil: ["profil", "dimension", "dim"],
    varuslag: ["varuslag", "vara", "sort"],
    traslag: ["traslag", "tra"],
    kvalitet: ["kvalitet", "kval"],
    avrapp: ["avrapp", "avrapportering"],
    nomTjocklek: ["nomtjocklek", "tjocklek", "thickness", "tjock"],
    nomBredd: ["nombredd", "bredd", "width"],
    actTjocklek: ["acttjocklek", "akttjocklek"],
    actBredd: ["actbredd", "aktbredd"],
    antal: ["antal", "st", "count"],
    langdDm: ["langddm", "langd", "length", "dm"],
    m3: ["m3", "volym", "volume"],
    m3Pris: ["m3pris", "pris", "price"],
    m3Sum: ["m3sum", "summa", "sum"],
    avrappM3: ["avrapp_m3", "avrappvolym", "avrakknadm3"],
    lpm: ["lpm", "lopmeter", "lopande"],
    actM3: ["actm3", "aktm3", "aktuelvolym"],
    anmarkNing: ["anmarkning", "artikel", "artikelanm"],
    anteckning: ["anteckning", "notat", "kommentar", "note"],
  };

  const mapping: MappingState = {};
  for (const header of headers) {
    const norm = normalise(header);
    let found = "ignorera";
    for (const [field, patterns] of Object.entries(guesses)) {
      if (patterns.some((p) => norm.includes(p) || p.includes(norm))) {
        found = field;
        break;
      }
    }
    mapping[header] = found;
  }
  return mapping;
}

type ClerkUser = {

  email: string | null;
  firstName: string | null;
  lastName: string | null;
  lastSignInAt: number | null;
  lastActiveAt: number | null;
  createdAt: number;
  banned: boolean;
  role: string | null;
};

function AnvandarSection() {

  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const [inviteRol, setInviteRol] = useState("anvandare");
  const [inviting, setInviting] = useState(false);

  const { data: users = [], isLoading } = useQuery<ClerkUser[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/admin/users"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<ClerkUser[]>("/api/admin/users"),
  });

  async function invite() {
    if (!inviteEmail.includes("@")) return;
    setInviting(true);
    try {
      await customFetch("/api/admin/invite", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ email: inviteEmail, rol: inviteRol }),
      });
      toast({
        title: "E-post godkänd",
        description: `${inviteEmail} kan nu skapa ett konto. Dela appens länk med personen.`,
      });
      setInviteEmail("");
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/admin/allowlist"] });
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      toast({ title: "Kunde inte godkänna", description: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Fel", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setInviting(false);
    }
  }

  async function deleteUser(userId: string, email: string | null) {
    try {
      await customFetch(`/api/admin/users/${userId}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      toast({ title: "Användare borttagen", description: email ?? userId });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/admin/users"] });
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Kunde inte ta bort användare", variant: "destructive" });
    }
  }

  async function changeRole(userId: string, role: string) {
    try {
      await customFetch(`/api/admin/users/${userId}/role`, {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ role }),
      });
      toast({ title: "Roll uppdaterad" });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/admin/users"] });
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Kunde inte uppdatera roll", variant: "destructive" });
    }
  }

  const fmt = (ts: number | null) =>
    ts ? new Date(ts).toLocaleDateString("sv-SE") : "Aldrig";

  const ACTIVE_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000;
  const isActiveNow = (ts: number | null) =>
    ts != null && Date.now() - ts < ACTIVE_MS;

  return (
    <Card>
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Users className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />
          Användare
        </CardTitle>
        <CardDescription>
          Hantera vem som har tillgång till systemet.
        </CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="space-y-5">
        {/* Invite form */}
        <div className="space-y-1.5">
          <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            Lägg till en e-postadress för att tillåta att personen skapar ett konto. Dela sedan appens länk med personen.
          </p>
          <div className="flex gap-2 flex-wrap sm:flex-nowrap">
            <div className="relative flex-1 min-w-0">
              <Mail className="absolute left-2.5 top-2.5 h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
              <Input
                className="pl-9"
                type="email"
                placeholder="person@foretag.se"
                value={inviteEmail}
                onChange={(e) => setInviteEmail(e.target.value)}
                onKeyDown={(e) => e.key === "Enter" && invite()}
              />
            </div>
            <Select value={inviteRol} onValueChange={setInviteRol}>
              <SelectTrigger className="w-[140px] shrink-0">
                <SelectValue />
              </SelectTrigger>
              <SelectContent>
                <SelectItem value="anvandare">Användare</SelectItem>
                <SelectItem value="admin">Administratör</SelectItem>
              </SelectContent>
            </Select>
            <Button onClick={invite} disabled={inviting || !inviteEmail.includes("@")} className="gap-2 shrink-0">
              {inviting ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <UserPlus className="h-4 w-4" />}
              Godkänn
            </Button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* User list */}
        {isLoading ? (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-muted-foreground py-4">
            <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> Laddar användare...
          </div>
        ) : users.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground py-4 text-center">Inga användare registrerade ännu.</p>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-2">
            {users.map((u) => (
              <div key={u.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg border bg-card">
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                    <span className="font-medium text-sm truncate">{u.email ?? "—"}</span>
                    {u.banned && (
                      <Badge variant="destructive" className="text-xs gap-1">
                        <ShieldAlert className="h-3 w-3" /> Bannad
                      </Badge>
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap">
                    {isActiveNow(u.lastActiveAt) && (
                      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-green-600 font-medium">
                        <span className="h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-green-500 inline-block" />
                        Aktiv nu
                      </span>
                    )}
                    <span>{[u.firstName, u.lastName].filter(Boolean).join(" ") || "Inget namn"}</span>
                    <span>·</span>
                    <span>Skapades {fmt(u.createdAt)}</span>
                    <span>·</span>
                    <span>Senast aktiv {fmt(u.lastActiveAt ?? u.lastSignInAt)}</span>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <Select value={u.role ?? "admin"} onValueChange={(val) => changeRole(u.id, val)}>
                  <SelectTrigger className="w-[130px] h-8 text-xs shrink-0">
                    <SelectValue />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent>
                    <SelectItem value="admin">Administratör</SelectItem>
                    <SelectItem value="anvandare">Användare</SelectItem>
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
                <AlertDialog>
                  <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
                    <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="h-8 w-8 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive">
                      <Trash2 className="h-4 w-4" />
                    </Button>
                  </AlertDialogTrigger>
                  <AlertDialogContent>
                    <AlertDialogHeader>
                      <AlertDialogTitle>Ta bort användare?</AlertDialogTitle>
                      <AlertDialogDescription>
                        {u.email ?? u.id} tas bort permanent och förlorar all åtkomst. Åtgärden kan inte ångras.
                      </AlertDialogDescription>
                    </AlertDialogHeader>
                    <AlertDialogFooter>
                      <AlertDialogCancel>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
                      <AlertDialogAction
                        className="bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground hover:bg-destructive/90"
                        onClick={() => deleteUser(u.id, u.email)}
                      >
                        Ta bort
                      </AlertDialogAction>
                    </AlertDialogFooter>
                  </AlertDialogContent>
                </AlertDialog>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

function BrandingSection() {
  const branding = useBranding();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const [companyName, setCompanyName] = useState(branding.companyName);
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState(() => ({
    streetAddress: branding.streetAddress,
    postalCode: branding.postalCode,
    city: branding.city,
    country: branding.country,
    phone: branding.phone,
    email: branding.email,
    organizationNumber: branding.organizationNumber,
    vatNumber: branding.vatNumber,
    website: branding.website,
    certificationText: branding.certificationText,
    certificationNumber: branding.certificationNumber,
    invoiceDetails: branding.invoiceDetails,
    approvedForFTax: branding.approvedForFTax,
  }));
  const [logoPath, setLogoPath] = useState(branding.logoPath);
  const [logoPreview, setLogoPreview] = useState(branding.logoUrl);
  const [file, setFile] = useState<File | null>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setCompanyName(branding.companyName);
    setProfile({
      streetAddress: branding.streetAddress,
      postalCode: branding.postalCode,
      city: branding.city,
      country: branding.country,
      phone: branding.phone,
      email: branding.email,
      organizationNumber: branding.organizationNumber,
      vatNumber: branding.vatNumber,
      website: branding.website,
      certificationText: branding.certificationText,
      certificationNumber: branding.certificationNumber,
      invoiceDetails: branding.invoiceDetails,
      approvedForFTax: branding.approvedForFTax,
    });
    setLogoPath(branding.logoPath);
    setLogoPreview(branding.logoUrl);
  }, [branding]);

  function chooseFile(next: File | undefined) {
    if (!next) return;
    if (!["image/png", "image/jpeg", "image/webp", "image/svg+xml"].includes(next.type)) {
      toast({ title: "Fel filtyp", description: "Välj PNG, JPG, WebP eller SVG.", variant: "destructive" });
      return;
    }
    if (next.size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) {
      toast({ title: "Filen är för stor", description: "Logotypen får vara högst 5 MB.", variant: "destructive" });
      return;
    }
    setFile(next);
    setLogoPreview(URL.createObjectURL(next));
  }

  async function save() {
    const trimmedName = companyName.trim();
    if (!trimmedName) {
      toast({ title: "Företagsnamn saknas", variant: "destructive" });
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    try {
      let nextLogoPath = logoPath;
      if (file) {
        const upload = await customFetch<{ uploadURL: string; objectPath: string }>("/api/storage/uploads/request-url", {
          method: "POST",
          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
          body: JSON.stringify({ name: file.name, size: file.size, contentType: file.type }),
        });
        const uploadResponse = await fetch(upload.uploadURL, {
          method: "PUT",
          headers: { "Content-Type": file.type },
          body: file,
        });
        if (!uploadResponse.ok) throw new Error("Uppladdningen misslyckades.");
        nextLogoPath = upload.objectPath;
      }

      const saved = await customFetch<typeof branding>("/api/branding", {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ companyName: trimmedName, logoPath: nextLogoPath, ...profile }),
      });
      setFile(null);
      setLogoPath(saved.logoPath);
      setLogoPreview(saved.logoUrl);
      await queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/branding"] });
      toast({ title: "Företagsprofil sparad", description: "Ändringen syns för alla användare." });
    } catch (err) {
      toast({
        title: "Kunde inte spara",
        description: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Försök igen.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Card>
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Building2 className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />
          Företagsuppgifter och dokument
        </CardTitle>
        <CardDescription>
          Uppgifter och logotyp används i systemets sidhuvud, dokument och utskrifter.
        </CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="space-y-5">
        <div className="grid gap-5 md:grid-cols-[1fr_auto] md:items-end">
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <label htmlFor="company-name" className="text-sm font-medium">Företagsnamn</label>
            <Input
              id="company-name"
              value={companyName}
              onChange={(event) => setCompanyName(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="Företagsnamn"
              maxLength={120}
            />
          </div>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border bg-muted/30 p-3 min-w-64">
            <div className="flex h-14 w-14 items-center justify-center rounded-md border bg-background overflow-hidden">
              {logoPreview ? (
                <img src={logoPreview} alt="Förhandsvisning av logotyp" className="h-full w-full object-contain p-1" />
              ) : (
                <ImageIcon className="h-7 w-7 text-muted-foreground" />
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="min-w-0">
              <p className="text-sm font-medium">Logotyp</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">PNG, JPG, WebP eller SVG · max 5 MB</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
          {([
            ["streetAddress", "Adress"],
            ["postalCode", "Postnummer"],
            ["city", "Ort"],
            ["country", "Land"],
            ["phone", "Telefon"],
            ["email", "E-post", "email"],
            ["organizationNumber", "Organisationsnummer"],
            ["vatNumber", "VAT-nummer"],
            ["website", "Webbplats", "url"],
            ["certificationText", "Certifieringstext"],
            ["certificationNumber", "Certifieringsnummer"],
          ] as const).map(([field, label, type]) => (
            <div className="space-y-2" key={field}>
              <label htmlFor={`branding-${field}`} className="text-sm font-medium">{label}</label>
              <Input
                id={`branding-${field}`}
                type={type ?? "text"}
                value={profile[field]}
                onChange={(event) => setProfile((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: event.target.value }))}
              />
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-2">
          <label htmlFor="branding-invoice-details" className="text-sm font-medium">Fakturauppgifter</label>
          <Textarea
            id="branding-invoice-details"
            value={profile.invoiceDetails}
            onChange={(event) => setProfile((current) => ({ ...current, invoiceDetails: event.target.value }))}
            placeholder="Exempelvis fakturaadress, referens eller betalningsuppgifter"
            rows={3}
          />
        </div>
        <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium">
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={profile.approvedForFTax}
            onChange={(event) => setProfile((current) => ({ ...current, approvedForFTax: event.target.checked }))}
          />
          Godkänd för F-skatt
        </label>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Button type="button" variant="outline" onClick={() => document.getElementById("branding-logo")?.click()}>
            <Upload className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />
            Välj logotyp
          </Button>
          <input
            id="branding-logo"
            type="file"
            accept="image/png,image/jpeg,image/webp,image/svg+xml"
            className="hidden"
            onChange={(event) => chooseFile(event.target.files?.[0])}
          />
          {logoPath && (
            <Button
              type="button"
              variant="ghost"
              onClick={() => {
                setFile(null);
                setLogoPath(null);
                setLogoPreview(null);
              }}
            >
              Använd standardlogotyp
            </Button>
          )}
          <Button type="button" onClick={save} disabled={saving}>
            {saving ? <Loader2 className="mr-2 h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Save className="mr-2 h-4 w-4" />}
            {saving ? "Sparar..." : "Spara företagsprofil"}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

type AllowlistEntry = { id: number; email: string; rol: string; lagtTillAt: string; lagtTillAv: string | null };

function AtkomstlistaSection() {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const { data: entries = [], isLoading } = useQuery<AllowlistEntry[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/admin/allowlist"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<AllowlistEntry[]>("/api/admin/allowlist"),
  });

  const canonicalOrigin = import.meta.env.VITE_APP_URL || window.location.origin;
  const appUrl = canonicalOrigin + import.meta.env.BASE_URL;

  function copyLink() {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(appUrl);
    toast({ title: "Länk kopierad", description: appUrl });
  }

  async function removeEmail(email: string) {
    try {
      await customFetch(`/api/admin/allowlist/${encodeURIComponent(email)}`, { method: "DELETE" });
      toast({ title: "E-post borttagen från listan" });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/admin/allowlist"] });
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Kunde inte ta bort", variant: "destructive" });
    }
  }

  return (
    <Card>
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <ShieldCheck className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />
          Godkända e-postadresser
        </CardTitle>
        <CardDescription>
          Endast dessa adresser kan skapa konto i systemet. Listan uppdateras när du godkänner en e-post ovan.
        </CardDescription>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent className="space-y-4">
        {entries.length === 0 && !isLoading && (
          <div className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-lg border border-orange-200 bg-orange-50 p-3 text-sm text-orange-800">
            <ShieldOff className="h-4 w-4 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
            <span>
              Listan är tom — alla som skapar ett konto kan logga in. Lägg till e-postadresser för att begränsa åtkomsten.
            </span>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border bg-muted/40 px-3 py-2 text-xs text-muted-foreground">
          <span className="flex-1 truncate font-mono">{appUrl}</span>
          <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="h-6 w-6 shrink-0" onClick={copyLink}>
            <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </Button>
        </div>

        {isLoading ? (
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-muted-foreground py-2">
            <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> Laddar…
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-1.5">
            {entries.map((e) => (
              <div key={e.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3 rounded-md border px-3 py-2 bg-card">
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 flex-wrap">
                    <span className="text-sm font-mono truncate">{e.email}</span>
                    <Badge variant={e.rol === "admin" ? "default" : "secondary"} className="text-xs shrink-0">
                      {e.rol === "admin" ? "Administratör" : "Användare"}
                    </Badge>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <Button
                  variant="ghost"
                  size="icon"
                  className="h-7 w-7 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive"
                  onClick={() => removeEmail(e.email)}
                >
                  <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                </Button>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

type Kund = { id: number; namn: string };

type Dubblett = {
  id_a: number;
  namn_a: string;
  id_b: number;
  namn_b: string;
  likhet: number;
};

function KunderSection() {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [nyKund, setNyKund] = useState("");
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [mergingPair, setMergingPair] = useState<Dubblett | null>(null);
  const [mergeKanonikId, setMergeKanonikId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [merging, setMerging] = useState(false);
  const [ignoringPair, setIgnoringPair] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const { data: kunder = [], isLoading, refetch } = useQuery<Kund[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/kunder"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<Kund[]>("/api/kunder"),
  });

  const { data: dubbletter = [], isLoading: loadingDubbletter, refetch: refetchDubbletter } = useQuery<Dubblett[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/kunder/dubbletter"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<Dubblett[]>("/api/kunder/dubbletter"),
  });

  async function addKund() {
    if (!nyKund.trim()) return;
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      await customFetch("/api/kunder", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ namn: nyKund.trim() }),
      });
      setNyKund("");
      refetch();
      refetchDubbletter();
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      toast({ title: "Fel", description: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Fel", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function deleteKund(id: number) {
    await customFetch(`/api/kunder/${id}`, { method: "DELETE" });
    refetch();
    refetchDubbletter();
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/kunder"] });
  }

  function openMergeDialog(pair: Dubblett) {
    setMergingPair(pair);
    setMergeKanonikId(pair.id_a); // default: keep the first (typically the older / lower id)
  }

  function closeMergeDialog() {
    setMergingPair(null);
    setMergeKanonikId(null);
  }

  async function confirmMerge() {
    if (!mergingPair || !mergeKanonikId) return;
    const dubblettId = mergeKanonikId === mergingPair.id_a ? mergingPair.id_b : mergingPair.id_a;
    setMerging(true);
    try {
      await customFetch("/api/kunder/merge", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ kanonikaId: mergeKanonikId, dubblettId }),
      });
      toast({ title: "Köpare sammanslagen", description: "Alla kopplingar har pekats om till den kanoniska raden." });
      closeMergeDialog();
      refetch();
      refetchDubbletter();
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/kunder"] });
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      toast({ title: "Fel vid sammanslagning", description: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Fel", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setMerging(false);
    }
  }

  async function ignoreDuplicate(pair: Dubblett) {
    const pairKey = `${pair.id_a}-${pair.id_b}`;
    setIgnoringPair(pairKey);
    try {
      await customFetch("/api/kunder/dubbletter/ignore", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ idA: pair.id_a, idB: pair.id_b }),
      });
      toast({
        title: "Förslag borttaget",
        description: "Ingen köpare eller lagerpost har ändrats.",
      });
      refetchDubbletter();
    } catch (err: unknown) {
      toast({
        title: "Kunde inte ta bort förslaget",
        description: err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Försök igen.",
        variant: "destructive",
      });
    } finally {
      setIgnoringPair(null);
    }
  }

  const dubblettNamnA = mergingPair ? (mergeKanonikId === mergingPair.id_a ? mergingPair.namn_a : mergingPair.namn_b) : "";
  const dubblettNamnB = mergingPair ? (mergeKanonikId === mergingPair.id_a ? mergingPair.namn_b : mergingPair.namn_a) : "";

  return (
    <>
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <Users className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />
            Sparade köpare
          </CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>
            Köpare som visas som förslag i spec-formuläret.
          </CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-4">
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <Input
              placeholder="Lägg till köpare..."
              value={nyKund}
              onChange={e => setNyKund(e.target.value)}
              onKeyDown={e => e.key === "Enter" && addKund()}
            />
            <Button onClick={addKund} disabled={saving || !nyKund.trim()} className="gap-2 shrink-0">
              {saving ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <UserPlus className="h-4 w-4" />}
              Lägg till
            </Button>
          </div>
          {isLoading ? (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-muted-foreground text-sm py-2">
              <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> Laddar...
            </div>
          ) : kunder.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground text-center py-4">Inga sparade köpare ännu.</p>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-1">
              {kunder.map(k => (
                <div key={k.id} className="flex items-center justify-between px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border bg-card">
                  <span className="text-sm">{k.namn}</span>
                  <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" className="h-7 w-7 text-muted-foreground hover:text-destructive" onClick={() => deleteKund(k.id)}>
                    <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                  </Button>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Duplicate detection */}
          {loadingDubbletter ? null : dubbletter.length > 0 && (
            <div className="space-y-2 pt-2 border-t">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4 text-amber-500 shrink-0" />
                <p className="text-sm font-medium text-amber-700">
                  {dubbletter.length === 1 ? "Möjlig dubblett hittad" : `${dubbletter.length} möjliga dubbletter hittade`}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                {dubbletter.map((d) => (
                  <div
                    key={`${d.id_a}-${d.id_b}`}
                    className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2"
                  >
                    <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 flex items-center gap-1.5 flex-wrap text-sm">
                      <span className="font-medium truncate">{d.namn_a}</span>
                      <ChevronRight className="h-3 w-3 text-muted-foreground shrink-0" />
                      <span className="font-medium truncate">{d.namn_b}</span>
