                      <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground ml-1">
                        ({Math.round(Number(d.likhet) * 100)}% likhet)
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <Button
                      size="sm"
                      variant="outline"
                      className="gap-1.5 shrink-0 h-7 text-xs border-amber-300 hover:bg-amber-100"
                      onClick={() => openMergeDialog(d)}
                    >
                      <Merge className="h-3 w-3" />
                      Slå ihop
                    </Button>
                    <Button
                      size="icon"
                      variant="ghost"
                      className="h-7 w-7 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground hover:bg-amber-100 hover:text-destructive"
                      onClick={() => ignoreDuplicate(d)}
                      disabled={ignoringPair === `${d.id_a}-${d.id_b}`}
                      title="Ta bort förslaget"
                      aria-label={`Ta bort dubblettförslaget ${d.namn_a} och ${d.namn_b}`}
                    >
                      {ignoringPair === `${d.id_a}-${d.id_b}` ? (
                        <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin" />
                      ) : (
                        <Trash2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
                      )}
                    </Button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {/* Merge confirmation dialog */}
      <AlertDialog open={!!mergingPair} onOpenChange={(open) => !open && closeMergeDialog()}>
        <AlertDialogContent>
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Merge className="h-5 w-5" />
              Slå ihop köpare
            </AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription asChild>
              <div className="space-y-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                <p>Välj vilken rad som ska vara den kanoniska (bevarade) köparen. Den andra tas bort och alla specifikationer, lego-jobb och lagerposter pekas om till den valda raden.</p>
                {mergingPair && (
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    {[
                      { id: mergingPair.id_a, namn: mergingPair.namn_a },
                      { id: mergingPair.id_b, namn: mergingPair.namn_b },
                    ].map((opt) => (
                      <button
                        key={opt.id}
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setMergeKanonikId(opt.id)}
                        className={`w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-lg border text-left transition-colors ${
                          mergeKanonikId === opt.id
                            ? "border-primary bg-primary/5 text-foreground"
                            : "border-border bg-card hover:bg-muted/50 text-foreground"
                        }`}
                      >
                        <div className={`h-4 w-4 rounded-full border-2 shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center ${
                          mergeKanonikId === opt.id ? "border-primary" : "border-muted-foreground"
                        }`}>
                          {mergeKanonikId === opt.id && (
                            <div className="h-2 w-2 rounded-full bg-primary" />
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <span className="font-medium">{opt.namn}</span>
                        {mergeKanonikId === opt.id && (
                          <span className="ml-auto text-xs text-primary font-medium">Behålls</span>
                        )}
                      </button>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
                {mergeKanonikId && mergingPair && (
                  <p className="text-xs rounded-md bg-muted px-3 py-2">
                    <span className="font-medium text-destructive">"{dubblettNamnB}"</span> tas bort.
                    Alla poster som refererar till det namnet pekas om till{" "}
                    <span className="font-medium text-foreground">"{dubblettNamnA}"</span>.
                  </p>
                )}
              </div>
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <AlertDialogCancel onClick={closeMergeDialog}>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
            <AlertDialogAction
              disabled={!mergeKanonikId || merging}
              onClick={confirmMerge}
              className="gap-2"
            >
              {merging ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin" /> : <Merge className="h-4 w-4" />}
              Slå ihop
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>
    </>
  );
}

export default function SysteminstallningarPage() {
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const fileRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);

  const [headers, setHeaders] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<Record<string, unknown>[]>([]);
  const [mapping, setMapping] = useState<MappingState>({});
  const [importing, setImporting] = useState(false);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<ImportResult | null>(null);
  const [fileName, setFileName] = useState("");

  function handleFile(e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;
    setFileName(file.name);
    setResult(null);

    const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload = (ev) => {
      const data = new Uint8Array(ev.target!.result as ArrayBuffer);
      const wb = XLSX.read(data, { type: "array" });
      const ws = wb.Sheets[wb.SheetNames[0]];
      const jsonRows = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json<Record<string, unknown>>(ws, {
        defval: "",
        raw: false,
      });
      if (jsonRows.length === 0) {
        toast({ title: "Tom fil", description: "Filen innehöll inga rader.", variant: "destructive" });
        return;
      }
      const hdrs = Object.keys(jsonRows[0]);
      setHeaders(hdrs);
      setRows(jsonRows);
      setMapping(guessMapping(hdrs));
    };
    reader.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
  }

  async function handleImport() {
    const poster = rows.map((row) => {
      const post: Record<string, unknown> = {};
      for (const [header, field] of Object.entries(mapping)) {
        if (field === "ignorera") continue;
        const val = row[header];
        if (val === "" || val === null || val === undefined) continue;
        const numFields = [
          "nomTjocklek", "nomBredd", "actTjocklek", "actBredd",
          "langdDm", "m3", "m3Pris", "m3Sum", "avrappM3", "lpm", "actM3",
        ];
        if (numFields.includes(field)) {
          const n = parseFloat(String(val).replace(",", "."));
          if (!isNaN(n)) post[field] = n;
        } else if (field === "antal") {
          const n = parseInt(String(val));
          if (!isNaN(n)) post[field] = n;
        } else {
          post[field] = String(val);
        }
      }
      return post;
    });

    setImporting(true);
    try {
      const data = await customFetch<ImportResult>("/api/lager/import", {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ poster }),
      });
      setResult(data);
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager"] });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/lager/statistik"] });
      toast({
        title: "Import klar",
        description: `${data.importerade} poster importerade, ${data.fel} fel.`,
      });
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Importfel", description: "Något gick fel vid importen.", variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setImporting(false);
    }
  }

  function reset() {
    setHeaders([]);
    setRows([]);
    setMapping({});
    setResult(null);
    setFileName("");
    if (fileRef.current) fileRef.current.value = "";
  }

  const mappedCount = Object.values(mapping).filter((v) => v !== "ignorera").length;

  return (
    <div className="p-6 space-y-6 max-w-4xl mx-auto">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight flex items-center gap-3">
          <Settings className="h-8 w-8 text-primary" />
          Systeminställningar
        </h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground mt-1">Importera data och konfigurera systemet.</p>
      </div>

      <BrandingSection />

      <AnvandarSection />

      <AtkomstlistaSection />

      <KunderSection />

      <ImpTillFuruMigrationSection />

      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <FileSpreadsheet className="h-5 w-5 text-primary" />
            Importera från Excel
          </CardTitle>
          <CardDescription>
            Ladda upp en .xlsx- eller .xls-fil. Systemet försöker gissa kolumnmappning automatiskt — du kan justera innan du importerar.
          </CardDescription>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="space-y-6">
          {!headers.length ? (
            <div
              className="border-2 border-dashed rounded-lg p-10 text-center cursor-pointer hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-muted/30 transition-colors"
              onClick={() => fileRef.current?.click()}
            >
              <Upload className="h-10 w-10 mx-auto mb-3 text-muted-foreground" />
              <p className="font-medium">Klicka för att välja fil</p>
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground mt-1">.xlsx eller .xls</p>
              <input
                ref={fileRef}
                type="file"
                accept=".xlsx,.xls"
                className="hidden"
                onChange={handleFile}
              />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-5">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <FileSpreadsheet className="h-4 w-4 text-primary" />
                  <span className="font-medium text-sm">{fileName}</span>
                  <Badge variant="secondary">{rows.length} rader</Badge>
                  <Badge variant="outline">{mappedCount} kolumner mappade</Badge>
                </div>
                <Button variant="ghost" size="sm" onClick={reset}>
                  <X className="h-4 w-4" />
                  Rensa
                </Button>
              </div>

              <div>
                <p className="text-sm font-medium mb-3">Kolumnmappning</p>
                <div className="space-y-2 max-h-80 overflow-y-auto pr-1">
                  {headers.map((header) => (
                    <div key={header} className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <span className="text-sm w-48 truncate text-muted-foreground font-mono bg-muted px-2 py-1 rounded shrink-0">
                        {header}
                      </span>
                      <ArrowRight className="h-3 w-3 text-muted-foreground shrink-0" />
                      <Select
                        value={mapping[header] ?? "ignorera"}
                        onValueChange={(v) => setMapping((m) => ({ ...m, [header]: v }))}
                      >
                        <SelectTrigger className="flex-1">
                          <SelectValue />
                        </SelectTrigger>
                        <SelectContent>
                          {LAGER_FIELDS.map((f) => (
                            <SelectItem key={f.key} value={f.key}>
                              {f.label}
                            </SelectItem>
                          ))}
                        </SelectContent>
                      </Select>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="bg-muted/40 rounded-lg p-4">
                <p className="text-sm font-medium mb-2">Förhandsgranskning (3 första rader)</p>
                <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                  <table className="text-xs w-full">
                    <thead>
                      <tr>
                        {headers
                          .filter((h) => mapping[h] !== "ignorera")
                          .map((h) => (
                            <th key={h} className="text-left pr-4 pb-1 font-medium text-muted-foreground">
                              {LAGER_FIELDS.find((f) => f.key === mapping[h])?.label ?? h}
                            </th>
                          ))}
                      </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                      {rows.slice(0, 3).map((row, i) => (
                        <tr key={i}>
                          {headers
                            .filter((h) => mapping[h] !== "ignorera")
                            .map((h) => (
                              <td key={h} className="pr-4 py-0.5 text-foreground/80">
                                {String(row[h] ?? "").slice(0, 30)}
                              </td>
                            ))}
                        </tr>
                      ))}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </div>
              </div>

              {result ? (
                <div className={`flex items-center gap-3 p-4 rounded-lg border ${result.fel === 0 ? "bg-green-50 border-green-200" : "bg-yellow-50 border-yellow-200"}`}>
                  {result.fel === 0 ? (
                    <CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5 text-green-600 shrink-0" />
                  ) : (
                    <AlertTriangle className="h-5 w-5 text-yellow-600 shrink-0" />
                  )}
                  <div>
                    <p className="font-medium text-sm">Import klar</p>
                    <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                      {result.importerade} poster importerades.{result.fel > 0 ? ` ${result.fel} poster misslyckades.` : ""}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <Button onClick={handleImport} disabled={importing || mappedCount === 0} className="w-full">
                  {importing ? "Importerar..." : `Importera ${rows.length} poster`}
                </Button>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/inventering/InventeringDetaljPage.tsx ================

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useLocation, useRoute, useSearch } from "wouter";
import { useQuery, useQueryClient, useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
  AlertDialogTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import { ArrowLeft, ScanBarcode, Loader2, Wrench, FileText } from "lucide-react";
import { KLASS_LABEL, KLASS_COLOR, type SessionOversikt, type SkanningDetalj, type LangdBrytning } from "./types";
import { omradeskartaHref, sessionsHref } from "@/pages/lagerplatser/inventeringskarta";

function formatDatum(iso: string | null) {
  if (!iso) return "—";
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleString("sv-SE", { dateStyle: "short", timeStyle: "short" });
}

// Formatering för "Felplacerade paket"-listan (Task: felplacerade-UX) — se
// exemplet i produktkravet: "2,4 m × 12 st · 2,7 m × 39 st".
function formatLangdM(langdDm: number): string {
  const m = langdDm / 10;
  return m.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: Number.isInteger(m) ? 0 : 1, maximumFractionDigits: 1 });
}

// NOM m³ visas alltid med 3 decimaler (t.ex. "0,419 NOM m³") — aldrig ACT.
function formatNomM3(m3: number): string {
  return m3.toLocaleString("sv-SE", { minimumFractionDigits: 3, maximumFractionDigits: 3 });
}

function formatLangdBrytning(langdBrytning: LangdBrytning[]): string {
  if (langdBrytning.length === 0) return "—";
  return langdBrytning.map((l) => `${formatLangdM(l.langdDm)} m × ${l.styck} st`).join(" · ");
}

function formatDimension(tjocklek: string | null, bredd: string | null): string | null {
  if (!tjocklek || !bredd) return null;
  return `${tjocklek}×${bredd}`;
}

function formatKvalitetProfil(s: SkanningDetalj): string | null {
  const delar = [s.kvalitet, s.profil].filter((v): v is string => !!v);
  return delar.length > 0 ? delar.join(" · ") : null;
}

export default function InventeringDetaljPage() {
  const [, params] = useRoute("/inventering/:id");
  const id = params?.id;
  const searchString = useSearch();
  const searchParams = new URLSearchParams(searchString);
  const omgangId = Number(searchParams.get("omgangId")) || null;
  const kartOmrade = searchParams.get("kartOmrade") ?? undefined;
  const inventeringskartaHref = omgangId && kartOmrade ? omradeskartaHref(kartOmrade, omgangId) : null;
  const [, setLocation] = useLocation();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [valda, setValda] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [visaKorrigeraDialog, setVisaKorrigeraDialog] = useState(false);

  const { data: session, isLoading } = useQuery<SessionOversikt>({
    queryKey: ["/api/inventering", id],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<SessionOversikt>(`/api/inventering/${id}`),
    enabled: !!id,
  });

  const felplacerade = session ? session.skanningar.filter((s) => s.klassificering === "felplacerad") : [];

  // Ett paket som skannats i detta fack är fysiskt observerat här — därför
  // ska samtliga felplacerade paket vara FÖRMARKERADE som standard. Varje
  // gång den underliggande listan av felplacerade pktNr faktiskt ändras
  // (första laddningen, eller efter en korrigering som tar bort/tillför
  // rader) återställs markeringen till "alla markerade". Detta rör aldrig
  // korrigeringslogiken — det är bara startvärdet för kryssrutorna.
  const felplaceradeNycklar = felplacerade.map((s) => s.pktNr).sort().join(",");
  const foregaendeNycklarRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!session) return;
    if (foregaendeNycklarRef.current === felplaceradeNycklar) return;
    foregaendeNycklarRef.current = felplaceradeNycklar;
    setValda(new Set(felplacerade.map((s) => s.pktNr)));
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [felplaceradeNycklar, !!session]);

  const korrigeraMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (pktNrLista: string[]) =>
      customFetch<{ korrigerade: string[]; konflikter: { pktNr: string; anledning: string }[] }>(`/api/inventering/${id}/korrigera`, {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({ pktNrLista }),
      }),
    onSuccess: (result) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/inventering"] });
      if (result.konflikter.length > 0) {
        toast({
          title: `${result.korrigerade.length} korrigerade, ${result.konflikter.length} kunde inte korrigeras`,
          description: result.konflikter.map((k) => `${k.pktNr}: ${k.anledning}`).join(" · "),
          variant: "destructive",
        });
      } else {
        toast({ title: `${result.korrigerade.length} paket korrigerade till detta fack` });
      }
    },
    onError: () => toast({ title: "Kunde inte korrigera", variant: "destructive" }),
  });

  if (isLoading || !session) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-64">
        <Loader2 className="h-6 w-6 animate-spin text-muted-foreground" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const { raknare } = session;
  const ovrigaAvvikelser = session.skanningar.filter((s) => s.fortfarandeAvvikande && s.klassificering !== "felplacerad");
  const allaMarkerade = felplacerade.length > 0 && felplacerade.every((s) => valda.has(s.pktNr));

  function toggleVald(pktNr: string) {
    setValda((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(pktNr)) next.delete(pktNr); else next.add(pktNr);
      return next;
    });
  }

  function toggleAlla() {
    setValda(allaMarkerade ? new Set() : new Set(felplacerade.map((s) => s.pktNr)));
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={() => setLocation(inventeringskartaHref ?? "/inventering")}>
          <ArrowLeft className="h-5 w-5" />
        </Button>
        <div className="flex-1">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">{session.omradeNamn} · Fack {session.fackKod}</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Startad {formatDatum(session.startadAt)} av {session.startadAv}
            {session.avslutadAt && ` · Avslutad ${formatDatum(session.avslutadAt)} av ${session.avslutadAv}`}
          </p>
        </div>
        {inventeringskartaHref && (
          <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setLocation(inventeringskartaHref)}>
            Tillbaka till inventeringskartan
          </Button>
        )}
        <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setLocation(`/inventering/${session.id}/rapport`)}>
          <FileText className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
          Rapport & prissättning
        </Button>
        {session.status === "pagaende" && (
          <Button onClick={() => setLocation(sessionsHref("skanna", session.id, omgangId, kartOmrade))} className="bg-[#2d5a27] hover:bg-[#224420] text-white">
            <ScanBarcode className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
            Fortsätt skanna
          </Button>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        <Badge className="bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 border-blue-300 border">{raknare.hittade}/{raknare.forvantadeAntalPaket} stämde</Badge>
        {raknare.felplacerade > 0 && <Badge className="bg-orange-100 text-orange-800 border-orange-300 border">{raknare.felplacerade} felplacerade</Badge>}
        {raknare.saknade > 0 && <Badge className="bg-red-100 text-red-800 border-red-300 border">{raknare.saknade} saknas</Badge>}
        {raknare.okanda > 0 && <Badge variant="outline">{raknare.okanda} okända</Badge>}
        {raknare.otydliga > 0 && <Badge className="bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800 border-yellow-300 border">{raknare.otydliga} otydliga</Badge>}
      </div>

      {felplacerade.length > 0 && (
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="p-4 space-y-3">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <h2 className="font-semibold">Felplacerade paket</h2>
              <AlertDialog open={visaKorrigeraDialog} onOpenChange={setVisaKorrigeraDialog}>
                <AlertDialogTrigger asChild>
                  <Button size="sm" disabled={valda.size === 0 || korrigeraMutation.isPending}>
                    {korrigeraMutation.isPending ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 animate-spin" /> : <Wrench className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />}
                    Korrigera valda till detta fack ({valda.size})
                  </Button>
                </AlertDialogTrigger>
                <AlertDialogContent>
                  <AlertDialogHeader>
                    <AlertDialogTitle>Korrigera {valda.size} paket till {session.omradeNamn} · Fack {session.fackKod}?</AlertDialogTitle>
                    <AlertDialogDescription>
                      Detta flyttar de markerade paketen (och samtliga deras längdrader) till detta fack i lagret. Åtgärden kan inte ångras automatiskt.
                    </AlertDialogDescription>
                  </AlertDialogHeader>
                  <AlertDialogFooter>
                    <AlertDialogCancel>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
                    <AlertDialogAction onClick={() => korrigeraMutation.mutate(Array.from(valda))}>
                      Korrigera
                    </AlertDialogAction>
                  </AlertDialogFooter>
                </AlertDialogContent>
              </AlertDialog>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              Dessa paket skannades i detta fack men är registrerade på annan plats i systemet — de är därför förmarkerade för korrigering hit. Avmarkera de du inte vill flytta.
            </p>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 text-sm">
              <Button variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={toggleAlla}>
                {allaMarkerade ? "Avmarkera alla" : "Markera alla"}
              </Button>
              <span className="text-muted-foreground">{valda.size} av {felplacerade.length} markerade</span>
            </div>

            {/* Mobil: kompakt kortlista, ingen horisontell scroll. */}
            <div className="sm:hidden space-y-2">
              {felplacerade.map((s) => {
                const nom = formatDimension(s.nomTjocklek, s.nomBredd);
                const act = formatDimension(s.actTjocklek, s.actBredd);
                const kvalProfil = formatKvalitetProfil(s);
                return (
                  <div key={s.id} className="rounded-lg border p-3 space-y-1.5">
                    <div className="flex items-start gap-2.5">
                      <Checkbox className="mt-0.5" checked={valda.has(s.pktNr)} onCheckedChange={() => toggleVald(s.pktNr)} />
                      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 space-y-1">
                        <div className="font-mono font-medium">{s.pktNr}</div>
                        {(nom || act) && (
                          <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                            {nom && `NOM ${nom}`}{nom && act && " · "}{act && `ACT ${act}`}
                          </div>
                        )}
                        <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{formatLangdBrytning(s.langdBrytning)}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs font-medium">
                          Totalt {s.totalAntal} st · {formatNomM3(s.totalM3)} NOM m³
                        </div>
                        {kvalProfil && <div className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{kvalProfil}</div>}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>

            {/* Desktop: kolumner. */}
            <div className="hidden sm:block rounded-lg border overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-sm">
                <thead className="bg-muted/50">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="w-10 px-4 py-2.5" />
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Paket-ID</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Dimension (NOM · ACT)</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Längder</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Totalt / NOM m³</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {felplacerade.map((s) => {
                    const nom = formatDimension(s.nomTjocklek, s.nomBredd);
                    const act = formatDimension(s.actTjocklek, s.actBredd);
                    const kvalProfil = formatKvalitetProfil(s);
                    return (
                      <tr key={s.id} className="border-t align-top">
                        <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
                          <Checkbox checked={valda.has(s.pktNr)} onCheckedChange={() => toggleVald(s.pktNr)} />
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
                          <div className="font-mono font-medium">{s.pktNr}</div>
                          {kvalProfil && <div className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">{kvalProfil}</div>}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-2.5 whitespace-nowrap">
                          {nom ? `NOM ${nom}` : "—"}{act && <span className="text-muted-foreground"> · ACT {act}</span>}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-2.5">{formatLangdBrytning(s.langdBrytning)}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-2.5 whitespace-nowrap font-medium">
                          {s.totalAntal} st · {formatNomM3(s.totalM3)} NOM m³
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      {ovrigaAvvikelser.length > 0 && (
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="p-4 space-y-3">
            <h2 className="font-semibold">Övriga avvikelser</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Kräver manuell hantering — korrigeras inte automatiskt.</p>
            <div className="rounded-lg border overflow-hidden">
              <table className="w-full text-sm">
                <thead className="bg-muted/50">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Paket-ID</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Status</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {ovrigaAvvikelser.map((s) => (
                    <tr key={s.id} className="border-t">
                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5 font-mono font-medium">{s.pktNr}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5"><Badge className={cn("border", KLASS_COLOR[s.klassificering])}>{KLASS_LABEL[s.klassificering]}</Badge></td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      {session.saknadePaket.length > 0 && (
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="p-4 space-y-3">
            <h2 className="font-semibold">Saknade paket</h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              Fanns registrerade i facket vid inventeringens start men skannades aldrig. Produktdata nedan är sammansättningen VID
              INVENTERINGSSTART — den ändras aldrig av senare flyttar eller uttag i lagret.
            </p>
            <div className="rounded-lg border overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-sm">
                <thead className="bg-muted/50">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Paket-ID</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Träslag</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Profil</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Kvalitet</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Dimension</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Längder</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Antal / m³</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {session.saknadePaket.map((p) => (
                    <tr key={p.pktNr} className="border-t">
                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5 font-mono font-medium">{p.pktNr}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5">{p.traslag ?? "—"}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5">{p.profil ?? "—"}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5">{p.kvalitet ?? "—"}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
                        {p.nomTjocklek && p.nomBredd ? `${p.nomTjocklek}×${p.nomBredd}` : "—"}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5">{p.langder.length > 0 ? p.langder.join(", ") : "—"}</td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-2.5">{p.totalAntal} / {p.totalM3.toFixed(2)} m³</td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      {felplacerade.length === 0 && ovrigaAvvikelser.length === 0 && session.saknadePaket.length === 0 && (
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="p-8 text-center text-muted-foreground">
            Inga avvikelser — facket stämmer helt.
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/inventering/InventeringOversikt.tsx ================

import { useState } from "react";
import { useLocation } from "wouter";
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tabs";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { ClipboardCheck, MapPin, AlertTriangle, History, ScanBarcode, Loader2, Layers } from "lucide-react";
import type { SessionListItem, AvvikelseListItem, SessionOversikt } from "./types";

interface LagerOmradeSummary {
  id: number;
  namn: string;
  slug: string;
  fackAntal: number;
}

interface FackMedStats {
  id: number;
  kod: string;
  posterAntal: number;
  totalM3: number;
}

interface LagerOmradeDetalj {
  id: number;
  namn: string;
  fack: FackMedStats[];
}

function statusBadge(status: SessionListItem["status"]) {
  if (status === "pagaende") return <Badge className="bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 border-blue-300 border">Pågående</Badge>;
  if (status === "avslutad") return <Badge className="bg-green-100 text-green-800 border-green-300 border">Avslutad</Badge>;
  return <Badge variant="outline">Avbruten</Badge>;
}

function formatDatum(iso: string | null) {
  if (!iso) return "—";
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleString("sv-SE", { dateStyle: "short", timeStyle: "short" });
}

export default function InventeringOversikt() {
  const [, setLocation] = useLocation();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [valtOmradeId, setValtOmradeId] = useState<string>("");

  const { data: omraden = [] } = useQuery<LagerOmradeSummary[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/lager/omraden"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<LagerOmradeSummary[]>("/api/lager/omraden"),
  });

  const { data: omradeDetalj, isLoading: laddarFack } = useQuery<LagerOmradeDetalj>({
    queryKey: ["/api/lager/omraden", "detalj", valtOmradeId],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<LagerOmradeDetalj>(`/api/lager/omraden/${omraden.find((o) => String(o.id) === valtOmradeId)?.slug}`),
    enabled: !!valtOmradeId && omraden.length > 0,
  });

  const { data: pagaende = [], isLoading: laddarPagaende } = useQuery<SessionListItem[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/inventering", "pagaende"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<SessionListItem[]>("/api/inventering?status=pagaende"),
    refetchInterval: 15_000,
  });

  const { data: avvikelser = [], isLoading: laddarAvvikelser } = useQuery<AvvikelseListItem[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/inventering/avvikelser"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<AvvikelseListItem[]>("/api/inventering/avvikelser"),
  });

  const { data: historik = [], isLoading: laddarHistorik } = useQuery<SessionListItem[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/inventering", "historik"],
    queryFn: async () => {
      const [avslutade, avbrutna] = await Promise.all([
        customFetch<SessionListItem[]>("/api/inventering?status=avslutad"),
        customFetch<SessionListItem[]>("/api/inventering?status=avbruten"),
      ]);
      return [...avslutade, ...avbrutna].sort((a, b) => (a.startadAt < b.startadAt ? 1 : -1));
    },
  });

  const startaMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (fackId: number) =>
      customFetch<{ atertagen: boolean; session: SessionOversikt }>("/api/inventering/start", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({ omradeId: Number(valtOmradeId), fackId }),
      }),
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/inventering"] });
      if (data.atertagen) {
        toast({ title: "Återupptar pågående inventering", description: `Fack ${data.session.fackKod}` });
      }
      setLocation(`/inventering/skanna/${data.session.id}`);
    },
    onError: () => {
      toast({ title: "Kunde inte starta inventering", variant: "destructive" });
    },
  });

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-bold tracking-tight flex items-center gap-2">
          <ClipboardCheck className="h-7 w-7 text-[#2d5a27]" />
          Inventering
        </h1>
        <p className="text-muted-foreground text-sm">Inventera lagerfack, se pågående sessioner och avvikelser.</p>
      </div>

      <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setLocation("/inventering/omgangar")} className="w-fit">
        <Layers className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
        Inventeringsomgångar
      </Button>

      <Tabs defaultValue="starta">
        <TabsList>
          <TabsTrigger value="starta">Starta inventering</TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value="pagaende">
            Pågående
            {pagaende.length > 0 && <Badge variant="secondary" className="ml-2">{pagaende.length}</Badge>}
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value="avvikelser">
            Avvikelser
            {avvikelser.length > 0 && <Badge variant="destructive" className="ml-2">{avvikelser.length}</Badge>}
          </TabsTrigger>
          <TabsTrigger value="historik">Historik</TabsTrigger>
        </TabsList>

        <TabsContent value="starta" className="space-y-4">
          <Card>
            <CardContent className="p-4 space-y-4">
              <div className="max-w-sm space-y-1.5">
                <label className="text-sm font-medium">Område</label>
                <Select value={valtOmradeId} onValueChange={setValtOmradeId}>
                  <SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectValue placeholder="Välj område…" />
                  </SelectTrigger>
                  <SelectContent>
                    {omraden.map((o) => (
                      <SelectItem key={o.id} value={String(o.id)}>{o.namn}</SelectItem>
                    ))}
                  </SelectContent>
                </Select>
              </div>

              {valtOmradeId && laddarFack && (
                <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
                  {Array.from({ length: 6 }).map((_, i) => <Skeleton key={i} className="h-16 rounded-lg" />)}
                </div>
              )}

              {valtOmradeId && omradeDetalj && (
                <div className="grid gap-2 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
                  {omradeDetalj.fack.map((f) => (
                    <button
                      key={f.id}
                      type="button"
                      disabled={startaMutation.isPending}
                      onClick={() => startaMutation.mutate(f.id)}
                      className="min-h-16 rounded-lg border p-3 text-left hover:border-[#2d5a27] hover:shadow-sm transition-colors disabled:opacity-50 flex items-center justify-between gap-2"
                    >
                      <div>
                        <div className="font-semibold">{f.kod}</div>
                        <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{f.posterAntal} poster · {f.totalM3.toFixed(1)} m³</div>
                      </div>
                      {startaMutation.isPending && startaMutation.variables === f.id ? (
                        <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin text-muted-foreground shrink-0" />
                      ) : (
                        <ScanBarcode className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground shrink-0" />
                      )}
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value="pagaende" className="space-y-2">
          {laddarPagaende && <Skeleton className="h-20 rounded-lg" />}
          {!laddarPagaende && pagaende.length === 0 && (
            <EmptyState icon={MapPin} text="Inga pågående inventeringar." />
          )}
          {pagaende.map((s) => (
            <SessionRad key={s.id} s={s} onClick={() => setLocation(`/inventering/skanna/${s.id}`)} />
          ))}
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value="avvikelser" className="space-y-2">
          {laddarAvvikelser && <Skeleton className="h-20 rounded-lg" />}
          {!laddarAvvikelser && avvikelser.length === 0 && (
            <EmptyState icon={AlertTriangle} text="Inga olösta avvikelser." />
          )}
          {avvikelser.map((a) => (
            <Card key={a.id} className="cursor-pointer hover:shadow-sm transition-shadow" onClick={() => setLocation(`/inventering/${a.id}`)}>
              <CardContent className="p-4 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2">
                <div>
                  <div className="font-medium">{a.omradeNamn} · Fack {a.fackKod}</div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">Avslutad {formatDatum(a.avslutadAt)}</div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
                  {a.raknare.felplacerade > 0 && <Badge className="bg-orange-100 text-orange-800 border-orange-300 border">{a.raknare.felplacerade} felplacerade</Badge>}
                  {a.raknare.saknade > 0 && <Badge className="bg-red-100 text-red-800 border-red-300 border">{a.raknare.saknade} saknas</Badge>}
                  {a.raknare.okanda > 0 && <Badge variant="outline">{a.raknare.okanda} okända</Badge>}
                  {a.raknare.otydliga > 0 && <Badge className="bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-800 border-yellow-300 border">{a.raknare.otydliga} otydliga</Badge>}
                </div>
              </CardContent>
            </Card>
          ))}
        </TabsContent>

        <TabsContent value="historik" className="space-y-2">
          {laddarHistorik && <Skeleton className="h-20 rounded-lg" />}
          {!laddarHistorik && historik.length === 0 && (
            <EmptyState icon={History} text="Ingen inventeringshistorik ännu." />
          )}
          {historik.map((s) => (
            <SessionRad key={s.id} s={s} onClick={() => setLocation(`/inventering/${s.id}`)} />
          ))}
        </TabsContent>
      </Tabs>
    </div>
  );
}

function SessionRad({ s, onClick }: { s: SessionListItem; onClick: () => void }) {
  return (
    <Card className="cursor-pointer hover:shadow-sm transition-shadow" onClick={onClick}>
      <CardContent className="p-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <div>
          <div className="font-medium">{s.omradeNamn} · Fack {s.fackKod}</div>
          <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
            Startad {formatDatum(s.startadAt)}{s.avslutadAt ? ` · Avslutad ${formatDatum(s.avslutadAt)}` : ""}
          </div>
        </div>
        {statusBadge(s.status)}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  );
}

function EmptyState({ icon: Icon, text }: { icon: typeof MapPin; text: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-12 text-center text-muted-foreground gap-2">
      <Icon className="h-8 w-8 opacity-30" />
      <p className="text-sm">{text}</p>
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/inventering/InventeringRapportPage.tsx ================

import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { useLocation, useRoute } from "wouter";
import { useQuery, useQueryClient, useMutation } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
import { Switch } from "@/components/ui/switch";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogHeader,
  DialogTitle,
  DialogDescription,
  DialogFooter,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { ArrowLeft, Loader2, Printer, FileSpreadsheet, Tag, Ban, TrendingUp, TrendingDown, X } from "lucide-react";
import { KLASS_LABEL, KLASS_COLOR, type SessionOversikt, type RapportRad, type VardeSvar } from "./types";
import { printInventeringLista, type RapportFlatEntry } from "@/lib/printInventeringLista";
import { exportInventeringCsv } from "@/lib/exportInventeringCsv";

type GroupField = "dimension" | "kvalitet" | "profil" | "traslag" | "omrade" | "fack";
type SortField = GroupField | "pktNr";

/** Ordning som nya nivåer erbjuds i "Lägg till nivå"-väljaren. */
const GROUP_FIELDS: GroupField[] = ["dimension", "kvalitet", "profil", "traslag", "omrade", "fack"];

const SORT_LABEL: Record<SortField, string> = {
  pktNr: "Pkt-nr", dimension: "Dimension", kvalitet: "Kvalitet", profil: "Profil",
  traslag: "Träslag", omrade: "Område", fack: "Fack",
};
const GROUP_LABEL: Record<GroupField, string> = {
  dimension: "Dimension", kvalitet: "Kvalitet", profil: "Profil",
  traslag: "Träslag", omrade: "Område", fack: "Fack",
};

function sortValue(r: RapportRad, key: SortField): string | number {
  switch (key) {
    case "pktNr": return r.pktNr;
    case "dimension": return (Number(r.nomTjocklek) || 0) * 100000 + (Number(r.nomBredd) || 0);
    case "kvalitet": return r.kvalitet ?? "";
    case "profil": return r.profil ?? "";
    case "traslag": return r.traslag ?? "";
    case "omrade": return r.blandadPlacering ? "" : r.omradeNamn ?? "";
    case "fack": return r.blandadPlacering ? "" : r.fackKod ?? "";
  }
}

function groupLabel(r: RapportRad, key: GroupField): string {
  switch (key) {
    case "dimension": return r.nomTjocklek && r.nomBredd ? `${r.nomTjocklek}×${r.nomBredd}` : "Okänd dimension";
    case "kvalitet": return r.kvalitet ?? "Okänd kvalitet";
    case "profil": return r.profil ?? "Okänd profil";
    case "traslag": return r.traslag ?? "Okänt träslag";
    case "omrade": return r.blandadPlacering ? "Blandad placering" : r.omradeNamn ?? "Okänt område";
    case "fack": return r.blandadPlacering ? "Blandad placering" : r.fackKod ?? "Okänt fack";
  }
}

function compareRows(a: RapportRad, b: RapportRad, key: SortField): number {
  const va = sortValue(a, key);
  const vb = sortValue(b, key);
  if (typeof va === "number" && typeof vb === "number") return va - vb;
  return String(va).localeCompare(String(vb), "sv");
}

/**
 * Flernivågruppering: ett träd där varje nivå i `groupLevels` (i vald
 * ordning) delar upp föräldranodens rader i undergrupper. `rows` på en nod
 * innehåller alltid ALLA rader under noden (oavsett djup) — det gör att
 * delsumma-beräkning och "markera hela gruppen" fungerar identiskt på
 * varje nivå utan specialfall.
 */
interface GroupNode {
  level: number; // 0 = rot (orenderad), 1 = första grupperingsnivån, osv.
  label: string | null;
  rows: RapportRad[];
  children: GroupNode[] | null; // null = lägsta nivå, radera renderas direkt
}

function buildGroupTree(rows: RapportRad[], fields: GroupField[], level: number, label: string | null): GroupNode {
  if (fields.length === 0) {
    return { level, label, rows, children: null };
  }
  const [field, ...rest] = fields;
  const sorted = [...rows].sort((a, b) => compareRows(a, b, field) || compareRows(a, b, "pktNr"));
  const buckets: { label: string; rows: RapportRad[] }[] = [];
  for (const r of sorted) {
    const l = groupLabel(r, field);
    const last = buckets[buckets.length - 1];
    if (last && last.label === l) last.rows.push(r);
    else buckets.push({ label: l, rows: [r] });
  }
  return {
    level, label, rows,
    children: buckets.map((b) => buildGroupTree(b.rows, rest, level + 1, b.label)),
  };
}

interface FlatHeader {
  kind: "header";
  level: number;
  label: string;
  count: number;
  styck: number;
  nomM3: number;
  varde: number;
  node: GroupNode;
}
interface FlatRow {
  kind: "row";
  row: RapportRad;
}
type FlatEntry = FlatHeader | FlatRow;

/** Plattar ut trädet i renderingsordning: header-rad för varje grupp
 * (med delsumma direkt synlig, innan dess innehåll) följt av dess
 * underliggande grupper eller — på lägsta nivå — de sorterade paketraderna. */
function flattenGroupTree(node: GroupNode, sortBy: SortField): FlatEntry[] {
  const entries: FlatEntry[] = [];
  function walk(n: GroupNode) {
    if (n.label !== null) {
      entries.push({
        kind: "header",
        level: n.level,
        label: n.label,
        count: n.rows.length,
        styck: n.rows.reduce((s, r) => s + r.styck, 0),
        nomM3: n.rows.reduce((s, r) => s + r.nomM3, 0),
        varde: n.rows.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.pris?.lagervarde ?? 0), 0),
        node: n,
      });
    }
    if (n.children) {
      for (const child of n.children) walk(child);
    } else {
      const leafRows = [...n.rows].sort((a, b) => compareRows(a, b, sortBy) || compareRows(a, b, "pktNr"));
      for (const r of leafRows) entries.push({ kind: "row", row: r });
    }
  }
  walk(node);
  return entries;
}

function formatDatum(iso: string | null) {
  if (!iso) return "—";
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleString("sv-SE", { dateStyle: "short", timeStyle: "short" });
}

export default function InventeringRapportPage() {
  const [, params] = useRoute("/inventering/:id/rapport");
  const id = params?.id;
  const [, setLocation] = useLocation();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const [groupLevels, setGroupLevels] = useState<GroupField[]>([]);
  const [addLevelValue, setAddLevelValue] = useState<string>("");
  const [sortBy, setSortBy] = useState<SortField>("pktNr");
  const [visaPriser, setVisaPriser] = useState(false);
  const [valda, setValda] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [prisDialogOppen, setPrisDialogOppen] = useState(false);
  const [prisInput, setPrisInput] = useState("");

  const { data: session } = useQuery<SessionOversikt>({
    queryKey: ["/api/inventering", id],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<SessionOversikt>(`/api/inventering/${id}`),
    enabled: !!id,
  });

  const { data: rader, isLoading } = useQuery<RapportRad[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/inventering", id, "rader"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<RapportRad[]>(`/api/inventering/${id}/rader`),
    enabled: !!id,
  });

  const { data: varde } = useQuery<VardeSvar>({
    queryKey: ["/api/inventering", id, "varde"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<VardeSvar>(`/api/inventering/${id}/varde`),
    enabled: !!id,
  });

  function invalidera() {
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/inventering", id, "rader"] });
    queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/inventering", id, "varde"] });
  }

  const prissattMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (vars: { pktNrLista: string[]; prisPerNomM3: number }) =>
      customFetch<{ prissatta: any[]; fel: { pktNr: string; anledning: string }[] }>(`/api/inventering/${id}/prissatt`, {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify(vars),
      }),
    onSuccess: (result) => {
      invalidera();
      setPrisDialogOppen(false);
      setPrisInput("");
      setValda(new Set());
      if (result.fel.length > 0) {
        toast({
          title: `${result.prissatta.length} prissatta, ${result.fel.length} kunde inte prissättas`,
          description: result.fel.map((f) => `${f.pktNr}: ${f.anledning}`).join(" · "),
          variant: "destructive",
        });
      } else {
        toast({ title: `${result.prissatta.length} paket prissatta` });
      }
    },
    onError: () => toast({ title: "Kunde inte prissätta", variant: "destructive" }),
  });

  const taBortPrisMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: (pktNrLista: string[]) =>
      customFetch<{ borttagna: string[] }>(`/api/inventering/${id}/prissatt`, {
        method: "DELETE",
        body: JSON.stringify({ pktNrLista }),
      }),
    onSuccess: (result) => {
      invalidera();
      setValda(new Set());
      toast({ title: `Pris borttaget för ${result.borttagna.length} paket` });
    },
    onError: () => toast({ title: "Kunde inte ta bort pris", variant: "destructive" }),
  });

  const entries: FlatEntry[] = useMemo(() => {
    if (!rader) return [];
    const tree = buildGroupTree(rader, groupLevels, 0, null);
    return flattenGroupTree(tree, sortBy);
  }, [rader, groupLevels, sortBy]);

  const kolAntal = visaPriser ? 12 : 10; // datakolumner exkl. checkbox-kolumnen

  if (isLoading || !session || !rader) {
    return (
      <div className="flex items-center justify-center h-64">
        <Loader2 className="h-6 w-6 animate-spin text-muted-foreground" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  const sessionData: SessionOversikt = session;
  const raderData: RapportRad[] = rader;

  const totaltStyck = rader.reduce((s, r) => s + r.styck, 0);
  const totaltNomM3 = rader.reduce((s, r) => s + r.nomM3, 0);
  const totaltVarde = rader.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.pris?.lagervarde ?? 0), 0);

  const valdaRader = rader.filter((r) => valda.has(r.pktNr));
  const valdaNomM3 = valdaRader.reduce((s, r) => s + r.nomM3, 0);
  const naganValdHarPris = valdaRader.some((r) => r.pris);

  function toggleVald(pktNr: string) {
    setValda((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(pktNr)) next.delete(pktNr); else next.add(pktNr);
      return next;
    });
  }

  function toggleGrupp(node: GroupNode) {
    const allaValda = node.rows.every((r) => valda.has(r.pktNr));
    setValda((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      for (const r of node.rows) {
        if (allaValda) next.delete(r.pktNr); else next.add(r.pktNr);
      }
      return next;
    });
  }

  function laggTillNiva(field: string) {
    setGroupLevels((prev) => (prev.includes(field as GroupField) ? prev : [...prev, field as GroupField]));
    setAddLevelValue("");
  }

  function taBortNiva(field: GroupField) {
    setGroupLevels((prev) => prev.filter((f) => f !== field));
  }

  function oppnaPrisDialog() {
    if (valda.size === 0) {
      toast({ title: "Markera minst ett paket först", variant: "destructive" });
      return;
    }
    setPrisDialogOppen(true);
  }

  function skickaPris() {
    const pris = Number(prisInput.replace(",", "."));
    if (!Number.isFinite(pris) || pris < 0) {
      toast({ title: "Ange ett giltigt pris", variant: "destructive" });
      return;
    }
    prissattMutation.mutate({ pktNrLista: Array.from(valda), prisPerNomM3: pris });
  }

  // Samma platta post-lista (header/rad, i vald flernivågruppering och
  // sortering) matas in i både utskrift och CSV-export, så de alltid visar
  // exakt den gruppering användaren har valt på skärmen.
  const printEntries: RapportFlatEntry[] = entries.map((e) =>
    e.kind === "header"
      ? { kind: "header", level: e.level, label: e.label, count: e.count, styck: e.styck, nomM3: e.nomM3, varde: visaPriser ? e.varde : null }
      : { kind: "row", row: e.row },
  );

  function skrivUt() {
    printInventeringLista({
      titel: `Inventeringsrapport — ${sessionData.omradeNamn ?? ""} · Fack ${sessionData.fackKod ?? ""}`,
      undertitel: `Startad ${formatDatum(sessionData.startadAt)} av ${sessionData.startadAv}${sessionData.avslutadAt ? ` · Avslutad ${formatDatum(sessionData.avslutadAt)} av ${sessionData.avslutadAv}` : ""} · Utskrivet ${new Date().toLocaleString("sv-SE", { dateStyle: "short", timeStyle: "short" })}`,
      visaPriser,
      entries: printEntries,
      totaltStyck,
      totaltNomM3,
      totaltVarde: visaPriser ? totaltVarde : null,
    });
  }

  function exporteraCsv() {
    const namn = `inventering_${sessionData.fackKod ?? sessionData.id}_${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}${visaPriser ? "_med_priser" : ""}.csv`;
    exportInventeringCsv(namn, printEntries, visaPriser, { totaltStyck, totaltNomM3, totaltVarde: visaPriser ? totaltVarde : null });
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={() => setLocation(`/inventering/${session.id}`)}>
          <ArrowLeft className="h-5 w-5" />
        </Button>
        <div className="flex-1">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold">Rapport — {session.omradeNamn} · Fack {session.fackKod}</h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">{rader.length} skannade paket</p>
        </div>
        <Button variant="outline" onClick={exporteraCsv}>
          <FileSpreadsheet className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
          Exportera CSV
        </Button>
        <Button onClick={skrivUt} className="bg-[#2d5a27] hover:bg-[#224420] text-white">
          <Printer className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
          Skriv ut
        </Button>
      </div>

      {varde && (
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="p-4">
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-6">
              <Stat label="Totalt NOM m³" value={`${varde.totaltNomM3.toFixed(2)} m³`} />
              <Stat label="Prissatt NOM m³" value={`${varde.prissattNomM3.toFixed(2)} m³`} />
              <Stat label="Ej prissatt NOM m³" value={`${varde.ejPrissattNomM3.toFixed(2)} m³`} highlight={varde.ejPrissattNomM3 > 0} />
              <Stat label="Totalt lagervärde" value={`${varde.totaltLagervarde.toLocaleString("sv-SE")} kr`} />
            </div>
            {varde.jamforelse && (
              <div className="mt-3 pt-3 border-t flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                {varde.jamforelse.forandringNomM3 >= 0 ? <TrendingUp className="h-4 w-4 text-green-600" /> : <TrendingDown className="h-4 w-4 text-red-600" />}
                <span>
                  Jämfört med föregående inventering ({formatDatum(varde.jamforelse.foregaendeAvslutadAt)}):{" "}
                  {varde.jamforelse.forandringNomM3 >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{varde.jamforelse.forandringNomM3.toFixed(2)} m³,{" "}
                  {varde.jamforelse.forandringLagervarde >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{varde.jamforelse.forandringLagervarde.toLocaleString("sv-SE")} kr
                </span>
              </div>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      <Card>
        <CardContent className="p-4 space-y-3">
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
            <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground shrink-0">Gruppera efter (i vald ordning)</span>
            {groupLevels.map((field, i) => (
              <Badge key={field} variant="secondary" className="gap-1.5 pr-1">
                <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold opacity-60">{i + 1}</span>
                {GROUP_LABEL[field]}
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => taBortNiva(field)}
                  className="ml-0.5 rounded-full hover:bg-muted-foreground/20 p-0.5"
                  aria-label={`Ta bort nivå ${GROUP_LABEL[field]}`}
                >
                  <X className="h-3 w-3" />
                </button>
              </Badge>
            ))}
            {groupLevels.length < GROUP_FIELDS.length && (
              <Select value={addLevelValue} onValueChange={laggTillNiva}>
                <SelectTrigger className="w-40 h-8"><SelectValue placeholder="+ Lägg till nivå" /></SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  {GROUP_FIELDS.filter((f) => !groupLevels.includes(f)).map((f) => (
                    <SelectItem key={f} value={f}>{GROUP_LABEL[f]}</SelectItem>
                  ))}
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            )}
            {groupLevels.length > 0 && (
              <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setGroupLevels([])}>Rensa gruppering</Button>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                {groupLevels.length > 0 ? "Sortera inom lägsta nivå" : "Sortera efter"}
              </span>
              <Select value={sortBy} onValueChange={(v) => setSortBy(v as SortField)}>
                <SelectTrigger className="w-40"><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  {(Object.keys(SORT_LABEL) as SortField[]).map((k) => (
                    <SelectItem key={k} value={k}>{SORT_LABEL[k]}</SelectItem>
                  ))}
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 ml-auto">
              <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Visa priser</span>
              <Switch checked={visaPriser} onCheckedChange={setVisaPriser} />
            </div>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      {valda.size > 0 && (
        <Card className="border-[#2d5a27]/40">
          <CardContent className="p-3 flex items-center gap-3">
            <Badge variant="outline">{valda.size} valda · {valdaNomM3.toFixed(2)} m³</Badge>
            <Button size="sm" onClick={oppnaPrisDialog}>
              <Tag className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
              Sätt pris för valda
            </Button>
            {naganValdHarPris && (
              <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => taBortPrisMutation.mutate(Array.from(valda))} disabled={taBortPrisMutation.isPending}>
                {taBortPrisMutation.isPending ? <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 animate-spin" /> : <Ban className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />}
                Ta bort pris
              </Button>
            )}
            <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setValda(new Set())}>Avmarkera alla</Button>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      <Card>
        <CardContent className="p-0">
          <div className="overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-sm">
              <thead className="bg-muted/50">
                <tr>
                  <th className="w-10 px-3 py-2.5" />
                  <th className="text-left px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Pkt-nr</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Status</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Träslag</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Dimension</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Kvalitet</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Profil</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Längd(er) mm</th>
                  <th className="text-right px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Styck</th>
                  <th className="text-right px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">NOM m³</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Plats</th>
                  {visaPriser && <th className="text-right px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Pris kr/NOM m³</th>}
                  {visaPriser && <th className="text-right px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Paketvärde</th>}
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {entries.map((e, i) => {
                  if (e.kind === "header") {
                    return (
                      <tr key={`h-${i}`} className="border-t bg-muted/30">
                        <td className="px-3 py-2">
                          <Checkbox checked={e.node.rows.every((r) => valda.has(r.pktNr))} onCheckedChange={() => toggleGrupp(e.node)} />
                        </td>
                        <td colSpan={kolAntal} className="px-3 py-2 font-semibold" style={{ paddingLeft: `${(e.level - 1) * 20 + 12}px` }}>
                          {e.label} — {e.count} paket · {e.styck} st · {e.nomM3.toFixed(2)} m³
                          {visaPriser && <span> · {e.varde.toLocaleString("sv-SE")} kr</span>}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  }
                  const r = e.row;
                  return (
                    <tr key={r.pktNr} className="border-t">
                      <td className="px-3 py-2">
                        <Checkbox checked={valda.has(r.pktNr)} onCheckedChange={() => toggleVald(r.pktNr)} />
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2 font-mono font-medium">{r.pktNr}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2"><Badge className={`border text-xs ${KLASS_COLOR[r.klassificering]}`}>{KLASS_LABEL[r.klassificering]}</Badge></td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2">{r.traslag ?? "—"}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2">{r.nomTjocklek && r.nomBredd ? `${r.nomTjocklek}×${r.nomBredd}` : "—"}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2">{r.kvalitet ?? "—"}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2">{r.profil ?? "—"}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2">{r.langder.length ? r.langder.map((l) => l * 100).join(", ") : "—"}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2 text-right">{r.styck}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2 text-right">{r.nomM3.toFixed(3)}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2">{r.blandadPlacering ? "Blandad placering" : [r.omradeNamn, r.fackKod].filter(Boolean).join(" / ") || "—"}</td>
                      {visaPriser && (
                        <td className="px-3 py-2 text-right">{r.pris ? r.pris.prisPerNomM3.toLocaleString("sv-SE") : "—"}</td>
                      )}
                      {visaPriser && (
                        <td className="px-3 py-2 text-right font-medium">{r.pris ? r.pris.lagervarde.toLocaleString("sv-SE") : "—"}</td>
                      )}
                    </tr>
                  );
                })}
              </tbody>
              <tfoot>
                <tr className="border-t-2 font-semibold">
                  <td colSpan={8} className="px-3 py-2.5 text-right">Totalt</td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-right">{totaltStyck}</td>
                  <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-right">{totaltNomM3.toFixed(3)}</td>
                  <td />
                  {visaPriser && <td />}
                  {visaPriser && <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-right">{totaltVarde.toLocaleString("sv-SE")} kr</td>}
                </tr>
              </tfoot>
            </table>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>

      <Dialog open={prisDialogOppen} onOpenChange={setPrisDialogOppen}>
        <DialogContent>
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle>Sätt pris</DialogTitle>
            <DialogDescription>
              {valda.size} paket markerade · totalt {valdaNomM3.toFixed(2)} NOM m³. Priset gäller per NOM m³ och sparas som en frusen värdering för denna inventering.
            </DialogDescription>
          </DialogHeader>
          <div className="space-y-2">
            <label className="text-sm font-medium">Pris kr/NOM m³</label>
            <Input
              type="text"
              inputMode="decimal"
              value={prisInput}
              onChange={(e) => setPrisInput(e.target.value)}
              placeholder="t.ex. 2500"
              autoFocus
            />
            {prisInput && Number.isFinite(Number(prisInput.replace(",", "."))) && (
              <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">
                Totalt värde: {(Number(prisInput.replace(",", ".")) * valdaNomM3).toLocaleString("sv-SE", { maximumFractionDigits: 0 })} kr
              </p>
            )}
          </div>
          <DialogFooter>
            <Button variant="outline" onClick={() => setPrisDialogOppen(false)}>Avbryt</Button>
            <Button onClick={skickaPris} disabled={prissattMutation.isPending}>
              {prissattMutation.isPending && <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />}
              Spara pris
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </DialogContent>
      </Dialog>
    </div>
  );
}

function Stat({ label, value, highlight }: { label: string; value: string; highlight?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground uppercase tracking-wide">{label}</div>
      <div className={`text-lg font-semibold ${highlight ? "text-orange-600" : ""}`}>{value}</div>
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/inventering/InventeringSkanningPage.tsx ================

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useLocation, useRoute, useSearch } from "wouter";
import { useQuery, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { useUser } from "@clerk/react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { useDebounce } from "@/hooks/use-debounce";
import { cn } from "@/lib/utils";
import {
  AlertDialog,
  AlertDialogAction,
  AlertDialogCancel,
  AlertDialogContent,
  AlertDialogDescription,
  AlertDialogFooter,
  AlertDialogHeader,
  AlertDialogTitle,
  AlertDialogTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/alert-dialog";
import { ScanBarcode, Camera, CheckCircle2, AlertCircle, Loader2, ArrowLeft, Flag, X } from "lucide-react";
import { KLASS_LABEL, KLASS_COLOR, type SessionOversikt, type Klassificering } from "./types";
import KameraSkanner from "./KameraSkanner";
import { omradeskartaHref, sessionsHref } from "@/pages/lagerplatser/inventeringskarta";

type Feedback = { kind: "ok" | "warn" | "error"; text: string } | null;

// En träff i paketsökningen — grupperad per pktNr (GET /api/inventering/sok-paket).
// Ett fysiskt paket kan ligga som flera lager_poster-rader (en per längd);
// backend slår ihop dem till EN träff här, se lagerFilters.ts.
type PaketSokTraff = {
  pktNr: string;
  traslag: string | null;
  profil: string | null;
  kvalitet: string | null;
  nomTjocklek: number | null;
  nomBredd: number | null;
  langder: number[];
  antalRader: number;
  totalAntal: number;
  totalM3: number;
  omradeId: number | null;
  fackId: number | null;
  omradeNamn: string | null;
  fackKod: string | null;
  blandadPlacering: boolean;
  deregistrerad: boolean;
  kanAterstallas: boolean;
  restaureringsAnledning: string | null;
};

type DeregistreratPaket = {
  pktNr: string;
  kanAterstallas: boolean;
  restaureringsAnledning: string | null;
  metod: "skanner" | "manuell" | "kamera";
  package: {
    traslag: string | null;
    profil: string | null;
    kvalitet: string | null;
    nomTjocklek: string | null;
    nomBredd: string | null;
    langder: number[];
    styck: number;
    nomM3: number;
    blandadPlacering: boolean;
  };
};

function dimStr(t: number | null, b: number | null) {
  if (!t && !b) return null;
  return `${t ?? "-"}×${b ?? "-"}`;
}

export default function InventeringSkanningPage() {
  const { user } = useUser();
  const isAdmin = user?.publicMetadata?.role === "admin";
  const [, params] = useRoute("/inventering/skanna/:id");
  const id = params?.id;
  const searchString = useSearch();
  const searchParams = new URLSearchParams(searchString);
  const omgangId = Number(searchParams.get("omgangId")) || null;
  const kartOmrade = searchParams.get("kartOmrade") ?? undefined;
  const inventeringskartaHref = omgangId && kartOmrade ? omradeskartaHref(kartOmrade, omgangId) : null;
  const [, setLocation] = useLocation();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();

  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const [inputValue, setInputValue] = useState("");
  const [metod, setMetod] = useState<"skanner" | "manuell" | "kamera">("skanner");
  const [scanning, setScanning] = useState(false);
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState<Feedback>(null);
  const [avslutar, setAvslutar] = useState(false);
  const [inputFocused, setInputFocused] = useState(false);
  const [kameraOppen, setKameraOppen] = useState(false);
  const [tarBortPktNr, setTarBortPktNr] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [deregistreratPaket, setDeregistreratPaket] = useState<DeregistreratPaket | null>(null);
  const [aterstaller, setAterstaller] = useState(false);

  const { data: session, isLoading } = useQuery<SessionOversikt>({
    queryKey: ["/api/inventering", id],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<SessionOversikt>(`/api/inventering/${id}`),
    enabled: !!id,
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    inputRef.current?.focus();
  }, [session?.id]);

  const focusInput = useCallback(() => inputRef.current?.focus(), []);

  // Autocomplete för den manuella pktNr-sökningen — samma sök-/UX-mönster
  // som "Lägg till paket" i Specifikationer (debounce + dropdown), men mot
  // en egen backend-endpoint som grupperar träffar per fysiskt paket.
  const debouncedQuery = useDebounce(inputValue.trim(), 250);
  const skannadeSet = new Set(session?.skanningar.map((s) => s.pktNr) ?? []);
  const { data: sokData, isFetching: sokLaddar } = useQuery<{ data: PaketSokTraff[] }>({
    queryKey: ["/api/inventering/sok-paket", debouncedQuery],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<{ data: PaketSokTraff[] }>(`/api/inventering/sok-paket?q=${encodeURIComponent(debouncedQuery)}`),
    enabled: debouncedQuery.length >= 1 && metod !== "kamera",
  });
  const sokResultat = sokData?.data ?? [];
  const visaDropdown = inputFocused && metod !== "kamera" && inputValue.trim().length >= 1;

  async function handleScan(raw: string, metodOverride?: "skanner" | "manuell" | "kamera") {
    const pktNr = raw.trim();
    if (!pktNr || !id) return;
    setScanning(true);
    setFeedback(null);
    try {
      const valdMetod = metodOverride ?? metod;
      const result = await customFetch<
        | { redanSkannad: boolean; klassificering: Klassificering; session: SessionOversikt; deregistrerad?: false }
        | (Omit<DeregistreratPaket, "metod"> & { deregistrerad: true })
      >(
        `/api/inventering/${id}/skanna`,
        { method: "POST", body: JSON.stringify({ pktNr, metod: valdMetod }) },
      );
      if (result.deregistrerad) {
        setDeregistreratPaket({ ...result, metod: valdMetod });
        setFeedback({
          kind: "warn",
          text: result.kanAterstallas
            ? `${pktNr} är avregistrerat och måste återinföras manuellt`
            : `${pktNr} är avregistrerat men kan inte återinföras säkert`,
        });
        return;
      }
      queryClient.setQueryData(["/api/inventering", id], result.session);
      if (result.redanSkannad) {
        setFeedback({ kind: "warn", text: `${pktNr} redan skannat i denna inventering` });
      } else if (result.klassificering === "forvantad_hittad") {
        setFeedback({ kind: "ok", text: `${pktNr} — stämmer, hör hemma här` });
      } else {
        setFeedback({ kind: "error", text: `${pktNr} — ${KLASS_LABEL[result.klassificering]}` });
      }
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Okänt fel";
      setFeedback({ kind: "error", text: msg });
    } finally {
      setScanning(false);
      setInputValue("");
      setMetod((m) => (m === "kamera" ? "skanner" : m));
      focusInput();
    }
  }

  async function handleAterstall() {
    if (!id || !deregistreratPaket || !deregistreratPaket.kanAterstallas) return;
    const kandidat = deregistreratPaket;
    setAterstaller(true);
    try {
      await customFetch(`/api/inventering/${id}/aterstall`, {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({ pktNr: kandidat.pktNr }),
      });
      setDeregistreratPaket(null);
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/inventering/sok-paket"] });
      await handleScan(kandidat.pktNr, kandidat.metod);
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Okänt fel";
      setFeedback({ kind: "error", text: msg });
    } finally {
      setAterstaller(false);
    }
  }

  function handleKeyDown(e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLInputElement>) {
    if (e.key === "Enter") handleScan(inputValue);
    if (e.key === "Escape") { setInputValue(""); inputRef.current?.blur(); }
  }

  function valjSokTraff(p: PaketSokTraff) {
    handleScan(p.pktNr, "manuell");
  }

  // Ångrar en enskild skanning (t.ex. felskanning). Tar bort HELA
  // pktNr-skanningen som en enhet — även paket med flera längdrader.
  // Rör aldrig lager_poster/område/fack/plats; enbart en "ångra
  // inventeringsskanning". Servern räknar om Hittade/Kvar/Felplacerade/
  // Okända/Otydliga live, så det räcker att ersätta sessionen i cachen.
  async function handleTaBortSkanning(pktNr: string) {
    if (!id) return;
    setTarBortPktNr(pktNr);
    try {
      const result = await customFetch<{ borttagen: boolean; session: SessionOversikt }>(
        `/api/inventering/${id}/skanna/${encodeURIComponent(pktNr)}`,
        { method: "DELETE" },
      );
      queryClient.setQueryData(["/api/inventering", id], result.session);
      toast({ title: `${pktNr} borttaget från inventeringen` });
    } catch (err) {
      const msg = err instanceof Error ? err.message : "Okänt fel";
      toast({ title: "Kunde inte ta bort skanningen", description: msg, variant: "destructive" });
    } finally {
      setTarBortPktNr(null);
    }
  }

  // Kamerans streckkodsläsning (multi-format, verifierad mot systemet) och
  // AI-fotofallback landar båda här med ett verifierat pktNr — precis som
  // fysisk handscanner och manuell sökning körs det sedan genom EXAKT
  // samma handleScan()/POST-skanna, så resultat och klassificering blir
  // identiska oavsett källa.
  function handlePktNrFranKamera(pktNr: string) {
    setKameraOppen(false);
    handleScan(pktNr, "kamera");
  }

  // Kameran kunde läsa en kod men INTE entydigt matcha den mot ett paket i
  // systemet (olika sågverk kodar sina lappar olika — se KameraSkanner.tsx).
  // Vi gissar aldrig: stäng kameran, förifyll den befintliga manuella
  // sökrutan med det avlästa värdet och låt operatören välja rätt paket i
  // autocomplete-dropdownen.
  function handleOklarKodFranKamera(raValde: string, kodTyp: string) {
    setKameraOppen(false);
    setMetod("manuell");
    setInputValue(raValde);
    setFeedback({ kind: "warn", text: `Oklar kod (${kodTyp}): "${raValde}" — välj rätt paket nedan` });
    focusInput();
  }

  async function avslutaInventering() {
    if (!id) return;
    setAvslutar(true);
    try {
      await customFetch(`/api/inventering/${id}/avsluta`, { method: "POST" });
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/inventering"] });
      toast({ title: "Inventering avslutad" });
      setLocation(sessionsHref("resultat", Number(id), omgangId, kartOmrade));
    } catch {
      toast({ title: "Kunde inte avsluta inventeringen", variant: "destructive" });
      setAvslutar(false);
    }
  }

  if (isLoading || !session) {
    return (
      <div className="flex-1 flex items-center justify-center h-full">
