                        <th className="text-left px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Längder</th>
                        <th className="text-left px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Styck</th>
                        <th className="text-left px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">NOM m³</th>
                        <th className="text-left px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Plats vid start</th>
                        <th className="text-left px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Beslut</th>
                        <th className="px-3 py-2.5" />
                      </tr>
                    </thead>
                    <tbody>
                      {saknade.paket.map((p) => {
                        const checked = valdaSaknatPktNr.has(p.pktNr);
                        return (
                          <tr key={p.pktNr} className="border-t">
                            <td className="px-3 py-2.5">
                              <Checkbox
                                checked={checked}
                                onCheckedChange={(v) =>
                                  setValdaSaknatPktNr((prev) => {
                                    const next = new Set(prev);
                                    if (v) next.add(p.pktNr);
                                    else next.delete(p.pktNr);
                                    return next;
                                  })
                                }
                              />
                            </td>
                            <td className="px-3 py-2.5 font-mono font-medium">{p.pktNr}</td>
                            <td className="px-3 py-2.5">
                              {p.traslag ?? "—"} {p.profil ?? ""} {p.kvalitet ? `· ${p.kvalitet}` : ""}
                            </td>
                            <td className="px-3 py-2.5">{p.nomTjocklek ?? "—"}×{p.nomBredd ?? "—"}</td>
                            <td className="px-3 py-2.5">{p.actTjocklek ?? "—"}×{p.actBredd ?? "—"}</td>
                            <td className="px-3 py-2.5">{p.langder.join(", ") || "—"}</td>
                            <td className="px-3 py-2.5">{p.styck}</td>
                            <td className="px-3 py-2.5">{p.nomM3.toFixed(2)}</td>
                            <td className="px-3 py-2.5">
                              {p.blandadPlacering ? "Blandad" : `${p.omradeNamnVidStart ?? "—"} / ${p.fackKodVidStart ?? "—"}`}
                            </td>
                            <td className="px-3 py-2.5">
                              {p.beslut ? (
                                <Badge
                                  className={
                                    p.beslut.typ === "avrapporterad"
                                      ? "bg-gray-100 text-gray-800 border-gray-300 border"
                                      : "bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 border-blue-300 border"
                                  }
                                >
                                  {SAKNAT_BESLUT_LABEL[p.beslut.typ]}
                                </Badge>
                              ) : (
                                <span className="text-muted-foreground">—</span>
                              )}
                            </td>
                            <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-right">
                              <Link href={`/lager/${p.lagerId}`}>
                                <Button size="sm" variant="ghost">
                                  <ExternalLink className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" /> Visa
                                </Button>
                              </Link>
                            </td>
                          </tr>
                        );
                      })}
                    </tbody>
                  </table>
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      {omgang.typ === "arsinventering" && tillkommit && tillkommit.harGlobalSnapshot && tillkommit.paket.length > 0 && (
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="p-4 space-y-3">
            <h2 className="font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
              <PackageSearch className="h-4 w-4" /> Tillkommet gods ({tillkommit.antal})
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              Dessa paket skannades någonstans under omgången, men fanns inte i lagrets bestånd när omgången startade (t.ex. nyinlevererat
              gods under en flerdagars inventering). De räknas varken som hittade eller saknade mot startsnapshoten — {tillkommit.styck} styck,{" "}
              {tillkommit.nomM3.toFixed(2)} NOM m³ totalt.
            </p>
            <div className="rounded-lg border overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-sm">
                <thead className="bg-muted/50">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="text-left px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Pkt-nr</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Produkt</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Styck</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">NOM m³</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-3 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Plats vid skanning</th>
                    <th className="px-3 py-2.5" />
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {tillkommit.paket.map((p) => (
                    <tr key={p.pktNr} className="border-t">
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5 font-mono font-medium">{p.pktNr}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5">
                        {p.traslag ?? "—"} {p.profil ?? ""} {p.kvalitet ? `· ${p.kvalitet}` : ""}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5">{p.styck}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5">{p.nomM3.toFixed(2)}</td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5">
                        {p.blandadPlacering ? "Blandad" : `${p.omradeNamnVidSkanning ?? "—"} / ${p.fackKodVidSkanning ?? "—"}`}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-3 py-2.5 text-right">
                        <Link href={`/lager/${p.lagerId}`}>
                          <Button size="sm" variant="ghost">
                            <ExternalLink className="h-4 w-4 mr-1" /> Visa
                          </Button>
                        </Link>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      {totalt && totalt.antalKonflikter > 0 && omgang.sammanfattning && omgang.sammanfattning.konflikter.length > 0 && (
        <Card className="border-amber-300">
          <CardContent className="p-4 space-y-3">
            <h2 className="font-semibold flex items-center gap-2 text-amber-800">
              <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4" /> {totalt.antalKonflikter} konflikt{totalt.antalKonflikter === 1 ? "" : "er"} vid sammanslagning
            </h2>
            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
              Dessa paket förekom i mer än en fack-session under omgången. De räknas endast en gång i totalsammanställningen ovan —
              se val nedan för vilken session som blev avgörande.
            </p>
            <ul className="space-y-2 text-sm">
              {omgang.sammanfattning.konflikter.map((k, i) => (
                <li key={i} className="rounded-md border border-amber-200 bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2">
                  <div className="font-medium font-mono">{k.pktNr} <span className="font-sans font-normal text-amber-800">— {KONFLIKT_LABEL[k.typ]}</span></div>
                  <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground mt-0.5">{k.beskrivning}</div>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      {omgang.sammanfattning && omgang.sammanfattning.perOmrade.length > 1 && (
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="p-4 space-y-3">
            <h2 className="font-semibold">Per område</h2>
            <div className="rounded-lg border overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full text-sm">
                <thead className="bg-muted/50">
                  <tr>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Område</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Fack klara</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Funnet NOM m³</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Lagervärde</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Saknade</th>
                    <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Saknat NOM m³</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {omgang.sammanfattning.perOmrade.map((r) => {
                    const namn = omgang.fack.find((f) => f.omradeIdVidStart === r.omradeId)?.omradeNamnVidStart ?? `Område ${r.omradeId}`;
                    return (
                      <tr key={r.id} className="border-t">
                        <td className="px-4 py-2.5 font-medium">{namn}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-2.5">{r.antalFackKlara}/{r.antalFackTotalt}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-2.5">{r.totaltNomM3.toFixed(2)}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-2.5">{r.totaltLagervarde.toLocaleString("sv-SE")} kr</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-2.5">{r.antalSaknade}</td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-2.5">{r.nomM3Saknat.toFixed(2)}</td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      {omgang.status === "avslutad" && (
        <Card>
          <CardContent className="p-4 space-y-3">
            <h2 className="font-semibold flex items-center gap-2"><Scale className="h-4 w-4" /> Jämför med tidigare omgång</h2>
            <div className="max-w-sm space-y-1.5">
              <Select value={jamforMedId} onValueChange={setJamforMedId}>
                <SelectTrigger><SelectValue placeholder="Välj omgång att jämföra mot…" /></SelectTrigger>
                <SelectContent>
                  {jamforbara.map((o) => (
                    <SelectItem key={o.id} value={String(o.id)}>{o.namn}{o.ar ? ` (${o.ar})` : ""}</SelectItem>
                  ))}
                </SelectContent>
              </Select>
            </div>
            {jamforelse && (
              <>
                {jamforelse.varning && (
                  <div className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-md border border-amber-300 bg-amber-50 px-3 py-2 text-sm text-amber-800">
                    <AlertTriangle className="h-4 w-4 mt-0.5 shrink-0" />
                    <span>{jamforelse.varning}</span>
                  </div>
                )}
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-3 text-sm pt-2">
                  <JamforCell label="NOM m³" f={jamforelse.nomM3} decimals={2} />
                  <JamforCell label="Styck" f={jamforelse.styck} />
                  <JamforCell label="Inventerade paket" f={jamforelse.antalInventeradePaket} />
                  <JamforCell label="Lagervärde (kr)" f={jamforelse.lagervarde} />
                </div>
                {jamforelse.perOmrade.gemensamma.length > 0 && (
                  <div className="rounded-lg border overflow-x-auto mt-3">
                    <table className="w-full text-sm">
                      <thead className="bg-muted/50">
                        <tr>
                          <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Område</th>
                          <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">NOM m³</th>
                          <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Styck</th>
                          <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Lagervärde</th>
                        </tr>
                      </thead>
                      <tbody>
                        {jamforelse.perOmrade.gemensamma.map((o) => (
                          <tr key={o.omradeId} className="border-t">
                            <td className="px-4 py-2.5 font-medium">{o.omradeNamn ?? `Område ${o.omradeId}`}</td>
                            <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
                              {o.nomM3.nu.toFixed(2)}{" "}
                              <span className={o.nomM3.forandring >= 0 ? "text-green-700 text-xs" : "text-red-700 text-xs"}>
                                ({o.nomM3.forandring >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{o.nomM3.forandring.toFixed(2)})
                              </span>
                            </td>
                            <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
                              {o.styck.nu}{" "}
                              <span className={o.styck.forandring >= 0 ? "text-green-700 text-xs" : "text-red-700 text-xs"}>
                                ({o.styck.forandring >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{o.styck.forandring})
                              </span>
                            </td>
                            <td className="px-4 py-2.5">
                              {o.lagervarde.nu.toLocaleString("sv-SE")} kr{" "}
                              <span className={o.lagervarde.forandring >= 0 ? "text-green-700 text-xs" : "text-red-700 text-xs"}>
                                ({o.lagervarde.forandring >= 0 ? "+" : ""}{o.lagervarde.forandring.toLocaleString("sv-SE")})
                              </span>
                            </td>
                          </tr>
                        ))}
                      </tbody>
                    </table>
                  </div>
                )}
                {(jamforelse.perOmrade.endastA.length > 0 || jamforelse.perOmrade.endastB.length > 0) && (
                  <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                    {jamforelse.perOmrade.endastA.length > 0 &&
                      `Endast i ${jamforelse.a.namn}: ${jamforelse.perOmrade.endastA.map((o) => o.omradeNamn ?? `Område ${o.omradeId}`).join(", ")}. `}
                    {jamforelse.perOmrade.endastB.length > 0 &&
                      `Endast i ${jamforelse.b.namn}: ${jamforelse.perOmrade.endastB.map((o) => o.omradeNamn ?? `Område ${o.omradeId}`).join(", ")}.`}
                  </p>
                )}
              </>
            )}
          </CardContent>
        </Card>
      )}

      <Card>
        <CardContent className="p-4 space-y-3">
          <h2 className="font-semibold">Fack i omfånget</h2>
          <div className="rounded-lg border overflow-x-auto">
            <table className="w-full text-sm">
              <thead className="bg-muted/50">
                <tr>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Fack</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Område</th>
                  <th className="text-left px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground">Status</th>
                  <th className="text-right px-4 py-2.5 font-medium text-muted-foreground" />
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {omgang.fack.map((f) => (
                  <tr key={f.fackId} className="border-t">
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5 font-mono font-medium">{f.fackKod}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5">{f.omradeNamnVidStart}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5">{statusBadgeFack(f.sessionStatus)}</td>
                    <td className="px-4 py-2.5 text-right">
                      {f.sessionStatus === "pagaende" && f.sessionId && (
                        <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => setLocation(`/inventering/skanna/${f.sessionId}`)}>
                          <ScanBarcode className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" /> Fortsätt
                        </Button>
                      )}
                      {f.sessionStatus === "ej_startad" && omgang.status === "pagaende" && (
                        <Button
                          size="sm"
                          disabled={startaMutation.isPending}
                          onClick={() => startaMutation.mutate({ omradeId: f.omradeIdVidStart, fackId: f.fackId })}
                        >
                          {startaMutation.isPending && startaMutation.variables?.fackId === f.fackId ? (
                            <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />
                          ) : (
                            <ScanBarcode className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
                          )}
                          Starta
                        </Button>
                      )}
                      {(f.sessionStatus === "avslutad" || f.sessionStatus === "avbruten") && f.sessionId && (
                        <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setLocation(`/inventering/${f.sessionId}`)}>
                          Visa
                        </Button>
                      )}
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
      <AlertDialog open={confirmBehall} onOpenChange={setConfirmBehall}>
        <AlertDialogContent>
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>Behåll {valdaSaknatPktNr.size} paket i lager?</AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>
              Detta registrerar bara att paketen är granskade och lämnas kvar i lagret oförändrade — ingen lagerpost ändras. Använd detta
              när ni bedömer att paketet bara inte hittades ännu.
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <Textarea
            placeholder="Kommentar (valfritt)"
            value={saknatKommentar}
            onChange={(e) => setSaknatKommentar(e.target.value)}
            className="mt-1"
          />
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <AlertDialogCancel onClick={() => setSaknatKommentar("")}>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
            <AlertDialogAction disabled={behallMutation.isPending} onClick={() => behallMutation.mutate()}>
              Behåll i lager
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>

      <AlertDialog open={confirmAvrapportera} onOpenChange={setConfirmAvrapportera}>
        <AlertDialogContent>
          <AlertDialogHeader>
            <AlertDialogTitle>Avrapportera {valdaSaknatPktNr.size} paket som utlevererade?</AlertDialogTitle>
            <AlertDialogDescription>
              Detta markerar paketen som utlevererade idag, precis som en vanlig utleverans — det går inte att ångra härifrån. Använd
              endast när ni är säkra på att paketen inte längre finns fysiskt i lagret.
            </AlertDialogDescription>
          </AlertDialogHeader>
          <Textarea
            placeholder="Kommentar (valfritt)"
            value={saknatKommentar}
            onChange={(e) => setSaknatKommentar(e.target.value)}
            className="mt-1"
          />
          <AlertDialogFooter>
            <AlertDialogCancel onClick={() => setSaknatKommentar("")}>Avbryt</AlertDialogCancel>
            <AlertDialogAction disabled={avrapporteraMutation.isPending} onClick={() => avrapporteraMutation.mutate()}>
              Avrapportera
            </AlertDialogAction>
          </AlertDialogFooter>
        </AlertDialogContent>
      </AlertDialog>
    </div>
  );
}

function JamforCell({ label, f, decimals = 0 }: { label: string; f: { da: number; nu: number; forandring: number; forandringProcent: number | null }; decimals?: number }) {
  const positiv = f.forandring >= 0;
  return (
    <div>
      <div className="text-muted-foreground text-xs">{label}</div>
      <div className="font-semibold">{f.nu.toFixed(decimals)}</div>
      <div className={positiv ? "text-green-700 text-xs" : "text-red-700 text-xs"}>
        {positiv ? "+" : ""}{f.forandring.toFixed(decimals)}{f.forandringProcent !== null ? ` (${positiv ? "+" : ""}${f.forandringProcent.toFixed(1)}%)` : ""}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/inventering/OmgangarPage.tsx ================

import { useState } from "react";
import { useLocation } from "wouter";
import { useQuery, useMutation, useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { customFetch } from "@workspace/api-client-react";
import { Card, CardContent } from "@/components/ui/card";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Skeleton } from "@/components/ui/skeleton";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
import { Select, SelectContent, SelectItem, SelectTrigger, SelectValue } from "@/components/ui/select";
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogHeader, DialogTitle, DialogTrigger, DialogFooter } from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { ArrowLeft, Layers, Plus, Loader2 } from "lucide-react";
import type { OmgangListItem, OmgangStatus, OmgangTyp } from "./types";

interface LagerOmradeSummary {
  id: number;
  namn: string;
  slug: string;
  fackAntal: number;
}

function statusBadge(status: OmgangStatus) {
  if (status === "pagaende") return <Badge className="bg-blue-100 text-blue-800 border-blue-300 border">Pågående</Badge>;
  if (status === "avslutad") return <Badge className="bg-green-100 text-green-800 border-green-300 border">Avslutad</Badge>;
  return <Badge variant="outline">Avbruten</Badge>;
}

function typLabel(typ: OmgangTyp) {
  return typ === "arsinventering" ? "Årsinventering" : "Kontrollinventering";
}

function formatDatum(iso: string | null) {
  if (!iso) return "—";
  return new Date(iso).toLocaleString("sv-SE", { dateStyle: "short", timeStyle: "short" });
}

export default function OmgangarPage() {
  const [, setLocation] = useLocation();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const [dialogOppen, setDialogOppen] = useState(false);
  const [namn, setNamn] = useState("");
  const [typ, setTyp] = useState<OmgangTyp>("arsinventering");
  const [ar, setAr] = useState(String(new Date().getFullYear()));
  const [valdaOmraden, setValdaOmraden] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());

  const { data: omgangar = [], isLoading } = useQuery<OmgangListItem[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/inventeringsomgangar"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<OmgangListItem[]>("/api/inventeringsomgangar"),
    refetchInterval: 15_000,
  });

  const { data: omraden = [] } = useQuery<LagerOmradeSummary[]>({
    queryKey: ["/api/lager/omraden"],
    queryFn: () => customFetch<LagerOmradeSummary[]>("/api/lager/omraden"),
  });

  const skapaMutation = useMutation({
    mutationFn: () =>
      customFetch<OmgangListItem>("/api/inventeringsomgangar", {
        method: "POST",
        body: JSON.stringify({
          namn,
          typ,
          ar: ar ? Number(ar) : null,
          omradeIds: Array.from(valdaOmraden),
        }),
      }),
    onSuccess: (data) => {
      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["/api/inventeringsomgangar"] });
      setDialogOppen(false);
      setNamn("");
      setValdaOmraden(new Set());
      setLocation(`/inventering/omgangar/${data.id}`);
    },
    onError: () => toast({ title: "Kunde inte skapa omgång", description: "Kontrollera att du valt minst ett område.", variant: "destructive" }),
  });

  function toggleOmrade(id: number) {
    setValdaOmraden((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(id)) next.delete(id); else next.add(id);
      return next;
    });
  }

  return (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <Button variant="ghost" size="icon" onClick={() => setLocation("/inventering")}>
          <ArrowLeft className="h-5 w-5" />
        </Button>
        <div className="flex-1">
          <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold flex items-center gap-2">
            <Layers className="h-5 w-5 text-[#2d5a27]" />
            Inventeringsomgångar
          </h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">Gruppera fack-sessioner i en gemensam insats, t.ex. en årsinventering.</p>
        </div>
        <Dialog open={dialogOppen} onOpenChange={setDialogOppen}>
          <DialogTrigger asChild>
            <Button className="bg-[#2d5a27] hover:bg-[#224420] text-white">
              <Plus className="h-4 w-4 mr-2" />
              Ny omgång
            </Button>
          </DialogTrigger>
          <DialogContent>
            <DialogHeader>
              <DialogTitle>Ny inventeringsomgång</DialogTitle>
            </DialogHeader>
            <div className="space-y-4">
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label>Namn</Label>
                <Input value={namn} onChange={(e) => setNamn(e.target.value)} placeholder="T.ex. Årsinventering 2026" />
              </div>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3">
                <div className="space-y-1.5">
                  <Label>Typ</Label>
                  <Select value={typ} onValueChange={(v) => setTyp(v as OmgangTyp)}>
                    <SelectTrigger><SelectValue /></SelectTrigger>
                    <SelectContent>
                      <SelectItem value="arsinventering">Årsinventering</SelectItem>
                      <SelectItem value="kontrollinventering">Kontrollinventering</SelectItem>
                    </SelectContent>
                  </Select>
                </div>
                <div className="space-y-1.5">
                  <Label>År</Label>
                  <Input type="number" value={ar} onChange={(e) => setAr(e.target.value)} />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <Label>Omfång — vilka områden ingår</Label>
                <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  Facken i valda områden fryses in i omgången direkt. Omfånget kan inte ändras efteråt.
                </p>
                <div className="rounded-lg border divide-y max-h-56 overflow-y-auto">
                  {omraden.map((o) => (
                    <label key={o.id} className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 px-3 py-2 cursor-pointer hover:bg-muted/50">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <Checkbox checked={valdaOmraden.has(o.id)} onCheckedChange={() => toggleOmrade(o.id)} />
                        <span className="text-sm">{o.namn}</span>
                      </div>
                      <span className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">{o.fackAntal} fack</span>
                    </label>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <DialogFooter>
              <Button
                disabled={!namn.trim() || valdaOmraden.size === 0 || skapaMutation.isPending}
                onClick={() => skapaMutation.mutate()}
                className="bg-[#2d5a27] hover:bg-[#224420] text-white"
              >
                {skapaMutation.isPending && <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 mr-2 animate-spin" />}
                Skapa omgång
              </Button>
            </DialogFooter>
          </DialogContent>
        </Dialog>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-2">
        {isLoading && <Skeleton className="h-20 rounded-lg" />}
        {!isLoading && omgangar.length === 0 && (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-12 text-center text-muted-foreground gap-2">
            <Layers className="h-8 w-8 opacity-30" />
            <p className="text-sm">Inga inventeringsomgångar ännu.</p>
          </div>
        )}
        {omgangar.map((o) => (
          <Card key={o.id} className="cursor-pointer hover:shadow-sm transition-shadow" onClick={() => setLocation(`/inventering/omgangar/${o.id}`)}>
            <CardContent className="p-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
              <div>
                <div className="font-medium">{o.namn}{o.ar ? ` (${o.ar})` : ""}</div>
                <div className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
                  {typLabel(o.typ)} · {o.antalFackKlara}/{o.antalFackTotalt} fack klara · Startad {formatDatum(o.startadAt)}
                </div>
              </div>
              {statusBadge(o.status)}
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


================ FILE: artifacts/lager/src/pages/inventering/__tests__/exportInventeringsomgangXlsx.test.ts ================

import { describe, it, expect } from "vitest";
import * as XLSX from "xlsx";
import {
  encodeField,
  generateProductKey,
  formatProductDesc,
  calculateActM3,
  calculateLpm,
  createInventeringsomgangWorkbook
} from "../exportInventeringsomgangXlsx";
import type { OmgangRapportSvar } from "../types";
import type { Branding } from "@/components/branding/BrandingContext";

describe("exportInventeringsomgangXlsx helpers", () => {
  describe("generateProductKey", () => {
    it("safely escapes delimiters and combines parts deterministically", () => {
      const k1 = generateProductKey("A", "B", null, null, null, null, null);
      const k2 = generateProductKey("A", null, "B", null, null, null, null);
      expect(k1).not.toBe(k2);
      expect(k1).toBe("TRASLAG=A|PROFIL=B|KVALITET=∅|NOM_T=∅|NOM_B=∅|ACT_T=∅|ACT_B=∅");
      
      const k3 = generateProductKey("A|B", null, null, null, null, null, null);
      expect(k3).toBe("TRASLAG=A\\|B|PROFIL=∅|KVALITET=∅|NOM_T=∅|NOM_B=∅|ACT_T=∅|ACT_B=∅");
    });
    
    it("ignores length (length exclusion)", () => {
      const k1 = generateProductKey("Gran", null, null, "50", "150", null, null);
      const k2 = generateProductKey("Gran", null, null, "50", "150", null, null);
      expect(k1).toBe(k2);
    });
  });

  describe("formatProductDesc", () => {
    it("formats with all fields including ACT", () => {
      expect(formatProductDesc("Gran", "O-S", "VI", "50", "150", "48", "148")).toBe("Gran O-S VI 50×150 (ACT 48×148)");
    });
    it("formats with missing ACT dimensions", () => {
      expect(formatProductDesc("Gran", "O-S", "VI", "50", "150", null, null)).toBe("Gran O-S VI 50×150");
    });
    it("handles completely empty", () => {
      expect(formatProductDesc(null, null, null, null, null, null, null)).toBe("Okänd produkt");
    });
  });

  describe("calculateActM3", () => {
    it("calculates correct m3", () => {
      expect(calculateActM3("50", "150", 45, 10)).toBeCloseTo(0.3375);
    });
    it("returns null if ACT is missing", () => {
      expect(calculateActM3(null, "150", 45, 10)).toBeNull();
    });
  });

  describe("calculateLpm", () => {
    it("calculates correct lpm", () => {
      expect(calculateLpm(45, 10)).toBe(45);
      expect(calculateLpm(null, 10)).toBe(0);
    });
  });

  describe("createInventeringsomgangWorkbook", () => {
    it("creates workbook with correct sheets and exact formulas", () => {
      const report = {
         metadata: { namn: "Test", typ: "Års", ar: 2024, startadAt: "2024", avslutadAt: null, antalFackKlara: 1, antalFackTotalt: 1 },
         dataQuality: { fullstandigtFryst: true, historiskaLuckor: [] },
         totals: { unikaPaket: 2, antalUnikaPaket: 2, styck: 15, totaltStyck: 15, lpm: 45, totaltLpm: 45, nomM3: 1.5, totalNomM3: 1.5, actM3: null, totalActM3: null, actM3Komplett: false, actM3SaknadeRader: 1, actM3SaknadePaket: 1, saknadePaketUtanBeslut: 0, varning: null },
         avvikelser: [],
         frystSammanfattning: [],
         paket: [],
         slutlager: [
            {
               pktNr: "PKT1", status: "Inventerad", beslut: null, iSlutlager: true, korrigerad: false,
               placering: { omradeId: 1, omradeNamn: "A", fackId: 1, fackKod: "1" },
               rader: 2, underliggandeFrystaLangdrader: 2,
               summering: { styck: 10, lpm: 30, nomM3: 1.0, actM3: null, actSaknadeRader: 0, mangdSaknadeRader: 0 },
               frystaLangdrader: [
                   { lagerId: 1, omradeId: 1, fackId: 1, traslag: "Gran", profil: null, kvalitet: null, nomTjocklek: "50", nomBredd: "100", actTjocklek: null, actBredd: null, langdDm: 30, antal: 5, nomM3: 0.5, inventeradSkannadAt: "2024-01-01T10:00:00.000Z", source: "Sys", historiskaLuckor: [] },
                   { lagerId: 2, omradeId: 1, fackId: 1, traslag: "Gran", profil: null, kvalitet: null, nomTjocklek: "50", nomBredd: "100", actTjocklek: null, actBredd: null, langdDm: 30, antal: 5, nomM3: 0.5, inventeradSkannadAt: "2024-01-01T10:00:00.000Z", source: "Sys", historiskaLuckor: [] }
               ]
            },
            {
               pktNr: "PKT2", status: "Inventerad", beslut: null, iSlutlager: true, korrigerad: false,
               placering: { omradeId: 1, omradeNamn: "A", fackId: 1, fackKod: "1" },
               rader: 1, underliggandeFrystaLangdrader: 1,
               summering: { styck: 5, lpm: 15, nomM3: 0.5, actM3: null, actSaknadeRader: 0, mangdSaknadeRader: 0 },
               frystaLangdrader: [
                   { lagerId: 3, omradeId: 1, fackId: 1, traslag: "Tall", profil: null, kvalitet: null, nomTjocklek: "50", nomBredd: "100", actTjocklek: null, actBredd: null, langdDm: 30, antal: 5, nomM3: 0.5, inventeradSkannadAt: "2024-01-01T10:00:00.000Z", source: "Sys", historiskaLuckor: [] }
               ]
            }
         ]
      } as unknown as OmgangRapportSvar;

      const branding = { companyName: "Test AB" } as Branding;

      const wb = createInventeringsomgangWorkbook(report, branding);

      expect(wb.SheetNames).toEqual(["Lagerlista", "Prislista", "Sammanställning"]);

      const lager = wb.Sheets["Lagerlista"];
      expect(lager["U2"].f).toBe('IFERROR(IF(INDEX(Prislista!J:J, MATCH(A2, Prislista!A:A, 0))="", "", INDEX(Prislista!J:J, MATCH(A2, Prislista!A:A, 0))), "")');
      expect(lager["V2"].f).toBe('IFERROR(INDEX(Prislista!K:K, MATCH(A2, Prislista!A:A, 0)), "")');
      expect(lager["W2"].f).toBe('IF(U2="", "", IF(V2="kr/NOM m³", U2*O2, IF(V2="kr/lpm", U2*N2, "")))');

      const serialized = XLSX.write(wb, { type: "buffer", bookType: "xlsx" });
      const readBack = XLSX.read(serialized, { type: "buffer", cellFormula: true });
      expect(readBack.Sheets["Lagerlista"]["U2"].f).toBe(lager["U2"].f);
      expect(readBack.Sheets["Lagerlista"]["V2"].f).toBe(lager["V2"].f);
      expect(readBack.Sheets["Lagerlista"]["W2"].f).toBe(lager["W2"].f);
      expect(readBack.Sheets["Sammanställning"]["B20"].f).toBe("SUM(Lagerlista!W:W)");
      
      const pris = wb.Sheets["Prislista"];
      expect(pris["A2"].v).toContain("GRAN");
      expect(pris["A3"].v).toContain("TALL");
      expect(pris["A2"].v).not.toEqual(pris["A3"].v);
      expect(pris["K2"].v).toBe("kr/NOM m³");

      expect(lager["!ref"]).toContain("4"); // multi-length row count checking
    });
  });
});
